eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttaluṅ kāṇka. patiṉorān tirumuṟiyiluḷḷa mūtta nāyaṉār tiruviraṭṭi maṇi māli, civaperumāṉ tiruviraṭṭi maṇimāli, civaperumāṉ tiruvantāti eṉṉum pirapantaṅkaḷ mūṉṟiyum aruḷic ceyta kapilatēva nāyaṉār ivarē yeṉṉiṉ, ivaratu  camayam civameṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyuṟaviṉṟu! caṅka nūlkoṇṭu pārkku miṭattum, ivariyaṟṟiya iṉṉānāṟpatu eṉṉum nūliṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttil, ‘mukkaṭ pakava ṉaṭitoḻā tārkkiṉṉā’ eṉṟu mutaṟkaṭ civapirāṉik kūṟi yiruppatum, puṟanāṉūṟṟil, ivar, ‘pullili yerukkamāyiṉu  muṭiyavi, kaṭavuḷ pēṇā meṉṉā’ eṉṟu civapirāṉik kuṟippāṉ  uṇarttumpaṭi, avar cūṭum erukkam pūvi yeṭuttuk kūṟi yiruppatum, ivaratu camayam civameṉṟu karutac ceykiṉṟaṉa. ivar iṉṉā nāṟpatiṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttil, civapirāṉi yaṭuttup palarāmaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, murukavēḷ eṉṉum ivarkaḷik kūṟiyiruppatu, ivaratu potu nōkkiṉiyum, ataṟku iṭaṅ koṭuttuḷḷa civa camayattiṉ virinta koḷkiyiyum pulap paṭuttuvatākum. caṅkattuc cāṉṟōr palarum iṉṉaṇam potu nōkkuṭiya reṉpatum, avarellām in nāṉku kaṭavuḷariyum ivviyalpāṉē pōṟṟi vantuḷḷārka ḷeṉpatum avarkaḷiyaṟṟiya pāṭṭukkaḷāṉē aṟiyalākum. nakkīrar, puṟattiṉkaṇ in nālvariyum  immuṟiyē kūṟi, ivarkaḷi, ‘ñālaṅkākkum kāla muṉpiṟ, ṟōlā nallici nālvar’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatuṅ kāṇka. kapilar naṟṟiṇi mutaliyavaṟṟilum ikkaṭavuḷari yeṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. kuṟiñcik kaliyil ilakkumip pirāṭṭiyāriyum eṭuttiyampi yuḷḷār.
perumi
iv vāciriyaratu kalvip perumiyi, ivariyaṟṟiya pāṭṭukkaḷāṉē naṉku aṟiyalākum. caṅkattār tokutta eṭṭut toki, pattup pāṭṭu, patiṉeṇkīḻk kaṇakku eṉṉum mūṉṟu vakuppilum ivaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eṭṭut tokiyuḷ, paripāṭa loḻinta ēḻaṉuḷḷum, ivariyaṟṟiyaṉa kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. paripāṭal eḻupatil, irupattiraṇṭu  ceyyuṭkaḷē ippoḻutu veḷi vantiruttaliṉ, ataṉuḷḷum ivar pāṭṭu illi yeṉat tuṇintu kūṟalamiyātu, paṉṉūṟu pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷil ivar pāṭiyaṉa ēṟakkuṟiyap patiṉoṉṟil oru paṅkāka iruttalum, avi ovvoru tokiyilum cērntiruttalum, ivaratu pāṭṭiyaṟṟum perumiyiyum, naṉ matippiyum naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, kampar mutalāyiṉār pōṉṟu, ivvāciriyar toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟi yiruppārēl, ivaratu perumi yāvaruḷḷattum ippoḻutiyiṉum naṉṟākap patikkap paṭṭirukkum. tamiḻulaku itaṉiṉum mikutiyāṉa payaṉiyum eytiyirukkum. eṉiṉum, ivariyaṟṟiya kuṟiñcip pāṭṭum, kuṟiñcik kaliyum iyaṟki vaḷaṅkaḷi yeṭutturippatil nikaraṟṟaṉavāyt, toṭar
nilic ceyyuḷiṉ ōraḷavāṉa payaṉi aḷikkavallaṉa eṉpatum tēṟṟam. tamiḻc cuviyaṟiyātirunta āriya varacaṉ pirakattaṉukku, ivar kuṟiñcip pāṭṭiyaṟṟit tamiḻ aṟivuṟuttiṉar eṉpatiliruntu, tamiḻiṉpāl ivarukkirunta perumpaṟṟum, ēṉōrum tamiḻiṉi yaṟintu iṉpuṟa vēṇṭumeṉṉum  ivaratu peru viruppamum, tamiḻiṉ cuvi yaṟiyātōrum aṟintu pulavarāmpaṭi teruṭṭavalla ivaratu pērāṟṟalum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, cēṉāvariyar, parimēlaḻakar mutaliya uriyāciriyaṉmā rellām āṟām vēṟṟumic ceyyuṭ kiḻamikkuk ‘kapilaratu pāṭṭu’ eṉṟu utāraṇam kāṭṭiyuḷḷārka ḷeṉṟāl, aṅṅaṉam cāṉṟōr palarkkum eṭuttukkāṭṭāka muṉṉiṟṟaṟ kuriya ivar pāṭṭuk kaḷiṉ arumi, perumikaḷi eṅṅaṉam aḷaviṭṭurikka vallēm? ivvāṟu pulamiyiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya iv vāciriyar, aruḷum, aṉpum mutaliya ciṟanta kuṇaṉellām oruṅkamiyap peṟṟavarāyum iruntaṉar. akkālattup pulava rellārum vāymiyiyē aṇiyeṉap pūṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉa reṉṟāl, ivar avvāymikkē aṇiyeṉat tikaḻntā reṉṉalām. ivaratu pāṭṭiyaṟṟum vaṉmiyiyum, vāymiyiyum, maṉattūymi yiyum, pukaḻ mēmpāṭṭiyum caṅkattuc cāṉṟōrkaḷē oruṅ koppap pukaḻntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
‘	ulakuṭaṉ ṟiritarum palarpukaḻ nallici
vāymoḻik kapilaṉ,’ 	(akam. 78)
eṉṟu pulavarēṟākiya nakkīraṉār pukaḻntu kūṟiṉār.
‘	aracavi paṇiya aṟampurintu vayaṅkiya
maṟampuri koḷki vayaṅkucen nāviṉ
uvali kūrāk kavaliyi ṉeñciṉ
naṉaviṟ pāṭiya nallicik kapilaṉ’ 	(patiṟṟuppattu - 85)
eṉṟu peruṅkuṉṟūrkiḻār pāṭiṉar.
                                           tāḻātu
ceṟutta ceyyuṭ ceycen nāviṉ
veṟutta kēḷvi viḷaṅkupukaḻk kapilaṉ 	(puṟam-53)
eṉṟu poruntiliḷaṅkīraṉār pāṭiṉar.
‘	nilamicip piṟanta makkaṭ kellām
pulaṉaḻuk kaṟṟa vanta ṇāḷaṉ
irantucel mākkaṭ kiṉiyiṭa ṉiṉṟip
parantici niṟkap pāṭiṉaṉ.’ 	(puṟam-127)
eṉṟum,
‘	poyyā nāviṟ kapilaṉ.’	 (puṟam-174)
eṉṟum māṟōk kattu nappacaliyār pāṭiṉar.
iṅṅaṉam pulavarellām pōṟṟum pulamiyum, cāṉṟāṇmiyum uṭiyarāya ivarpāl, akkālattu vēntarkaḷum, vaḷḷalkaḷum evvaḷavu matippu vittiruttal vēṇṭum? paṟampiṟ kōmāṉākiya vēḷpāri, ivari āruyirt tuṇiyākak koṇṭoḻukiṉamiyē, ivarpāl avvaḷḷal vitta peru matippukkuc cāṉṟākum. cēramāṉ celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ eṉṉum vēntar perumāṉ, ivar pāṭiya pattup pāṭalkaḷukkup paricilāka nūṟāyiram kāṇam koṭutta taṉṟi, ōr mali mītēṟik kaṇṭa nāṭellām koṭuttāṉ eṉṟāl, avvaracaṉ ivar pāl vitta matippiṉi aḷaviṭalākumō? māntarañcēralirumpoṟi eṉṟa cērar perumāṉ, ippulavar peruntaki tamatu kālattil illātu pōṉamikku maṉaṅ kavaṉṟu, ‘tāḻātu, ceṟutta ceyyuṭ ceycen nāviṉ, veṟutta kēḷvi viḷaṅkupukaḻk kapilaṉ, iṉṟuḷaṉāyiṉ naṉṟumaṉ’ eṉṟu iraṅkik kūṟiṉaṉ. itu kēṭṭa poruntiliḷaṅ kīraṉār eṉṟa pulavar, avvaracaṉip pāṭuṅkāl itaṉik koṇṭu moḻintaṉar.
iṉṉaṇam pulavarum, maṉṉarum pukaḻaminta ivar tollāṇi nallāciriyarum āvar. itu, tamiḻiṉi matiyātu kūṟiya kuyakkoṇṭāṉ  perum piṟituṟumāṟu, nakkīrar tām pāṭiya aṅkatappāṭṭiṉuḷ,
‘	muraṇil potiya mutaṟputtēḷ vāḻi 
paraṇa kapilarum vāḻi,’
eṉṟu, aruntavak koḷki akattiyaṉāroṭu oppavittu vāḻtti, ivaratu āṇi piḻiyāmi kāṭṭiṉamiyāṟ ṟeḷiyappaṭum.
kālam
kaṭic caṅka kālattiyum, akkālattu viḷaṅkiya aracar, vaḷḷiyōr, pulavar eṉpāriṟ ciṟcilaratu kālattiyum itaṟku muṉ ārāyntār palar. aṉṉāruṭiya koḷkikaḷ, tammil oṉṟu paṭṭaṉavum, vēṟupaṭṭaṉavumāyp pala tiṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. virivu karuti avaṟṟiyellām eṭuttuk kāṭṭā totukki, ōrvaḻiyāl kapilaratu kālatti iṅku ārāyntu kāṇpām.
civacamaya kuravarkaḷākiya tiruñāṉacampantarum, tirunāvukkara carum oru kālattiṉareṉpatum ciṟuttoṇṭa nāyaṉārum appoḻutiruntavarē yeṉpatum tēvāram, periyapurāṇam mutaliyavaṟṟāl naṉ kaṟiyappaṭṭavi ciṟuttoṇṭar, akkālattu naracimmavarma pallava vēntaṉukkup paṭit talivarāyc ceṉṟu, vātāpi eṉṉumiṭattil iraṇṭām pulikēci eṉṉum caḷukkiya maṉṉaṉi muṟiyaṭittu vantaṉar. naracimmaṉum vātāpikoṇṭa naracimmaṉ eṉap peyareytiṉāṉ. icceyti, 
maṉṉavarkkut taṇṭupōy vaṭapulattu vātāvit
toṉṉakaran tukaḷākat tuḷineṭuṅki variyukittup
paṉmaṇiyu nitikkuviyum pakaṭṭiṉamum parittokiyu
miṉṉaṉaveṇ ṇilakavarntē yikalaracaṉ muṉkoṇarntār. 
eṉṟu periyapurāṇattuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. makēntiravarmaṉ putalvaṉākiya vātāpi koṇṭa naracimmavarmaṉiṉ kālam, ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti eṉat teritalāl, tiruñāṉa campantarum akkālattava reṉpatu kūṟātē yamiyum. tirunāvukkaracarākiya appar, appoḻutu āṇṭiṉ mutirntō rākaliṉ, āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭi ṉiṟutiyum avarkkuriyatē yākum. iṉi, oru cārār, pāṇṭiyaṉ nel vēliveṉṟa neṭumāṟaṉum, nantipōtavarmaṉ allatu nantivarmaṉum orukālattava reṉṟum, nanti pōtavarmaṉ kālam ki.pi. 710 mutal 760 vari eṉṟu cātaṉaṅkaḷāṟ karutappaṭutaliṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭumāṟaṉ kālamum avvēntaṉic camaṇiliruntu civa camayattiṟ ṟiruppiya tiruñāṉacampantar kālamum atuvē yākavēṇṭu meṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu, ciṟuttoṇṭar naracimmavarmaṉ kālattava reṉṉum uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟuṭaṉ māṟupaṭutaliṉ nanti
varmaṉōṭum, avaṉ cēṉāpatiyākiya  utayēntiraṉōṭum pōr purinta arikēcari māṟavarmaṉum vēṟu, neṭumāṟaṉum vēṟu eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. neṭumāṟaṉ, māṟavarmaṉukku aṟupatu eḻupatu āṇṭiṉ muṉviḷaṅkiṉōṉ eṉka.
iṉi, kaṭic caṅkamāṉatu, in nāyaṉmār kālattiṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉar nikaḻntatākum. kālatti variyaṟuttaṟ kuriya karuvikaḷil, akkālattuc ceyyuṇaṭip pōkkum oṉṟu. caṅkacceyyuḷkaḷukkum tēvārattukkum uḷḷa naṭi vēṟṟumiyō peritākiṉṟatu. iḵtoru puṟamirukka, iv viruvēṟu kālaṅkaḷiṉ cēymiyik kuṟikkum vēṟu  cāṉṟukaḷ iṅkē kāṭṭappaṭum. tirunāvukkaracu nāyaṉār, ‘naṉpāṭṭup pulavaṉāyc caṅka mēṟi naṟkaṉakak kiḻitarumik karuḷiṉēṉkāṇ’ eṉṟu iṟiyaṉārkkum nakkīraṉārkkum vātu nikaḻntami kuṟittirutta loṉṟē, tamiḻc caṅkamāṉatu nāyaṉmār kālattiṟkuc ciṟitēṉum muṟpaṭṭatāmeṉṟu nāṭṭap pōtiya tākum. caṅkap pulavar
kaḷāṟ pāṭap peṟṟa muṭiyuṭi vēntarum, ciṟṟaracarum mikappala
rākavum, avarkaḷuḷ oruvarēṉum civattiṉ iru viḻikaḷāṉa nāyaṉmār kālattiṟku aṇmiyi lēṉum iruntaṉareṉat teriyavilli. kōcceṅkaṭcōḻar, tiruk kōyiṟṟiruppaṇi purintu, civattip pōṟṟiya tirut toṇṭa rākaliṉ, nāyaṉmār patikattu eṭuttōtum pēṟu peṟṟuḷḷār.1
ivar civapirāṉukkut tirukkōyil pala camittamiyi, ‘eṇṭō ḷīcaṟ keḻiṉmāṭa meḻupatu ceytulakāṇṭa ceṅkaṇāṉ’ eṉṟu tirumaṅki yāḻvārum kūṟiyuḷḷār. campantar kālattiṉ muṉpu toṭṭuc cuntarar kālattiṉ piṉṉaruṅ kūṭac cōṇāṭu pallavaratu āṭciyiṟṟāṉē yiruntatu. akkāla mellām āṟṟa liḻantirunta cōḻa maṉṉar cilar cēra, pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷi yaṭintu, av vēntarkaḷōṭu ottu vāḻntu vantaṉar eṉa aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. campantar kālattiṉ muṉṉarē pallava āṭci cōṇāṭṭil nilittu viṭṭa teṉpatu, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṟ pallavaṉāṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟamiyiṉ, pallavaṉīccaram eṉap peyar peṟṟa tirukkōyil campantaratu patikam peṟṟiruttalāl aṟiyalākum. cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉār, ‘maṇṇulakaṅ kāval pūṇṭa, vurimiyāṟ pallavarkkut tiṟi koṭā maṉṉavari maṟukkañ ceyyum, perumiyāṟ puliyūrcciṟ ṟampalattem perumāṉip peṟṟā maṉṟē’ eṉṟu pāṭutalālum, cēramāṉ perumāṇāyaṉār purāṇattuc cēkkiḻār kūṟum cila kuṟippukkaḷālum, piṟavaṟṟālum cuntarar kālattil cōṇāṭu pallava āṭciyi lirunta teṉpatum, cōḻaṉ, cēra, pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷic cārntiruntaṉa ṉeṉpatum aṟiyalākum. pallavariṟ ciṟcilar camaṇa, putta mataṅkaḷit taḻuvi yiruntaṉar. ciṟcilar civa, viṇavaṅkaḷic cārntu ceṅkōlōcci vantaṉar. tirut toṇṭat tokiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa civaṉaṭiyārkaḷil pallava aracarkaḷum uḷḷaṉar. iṅṅaṉam cōṇāṭṭi yaṭippaṭuttut, tiṟamiyuṭaṉ āṭci purintu, nāyaṉ mārālum, āḻvārkaḷālum pukaḻap peṟṟa pallavar kālattiṟkum kaṭic caṅkattiṟkum iyipu iruntiruppiṉ, avarkaḷ caṅkap pulavarkaḷāl ettuṇip pāṭṭukkaḷ peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum? caṅkap pulavarukkō pallavareṉṟa peyarum teriya nērntilatu.
iṉi, tiruñāṉacampantar, tirunāvukkaracar kālattil tamiḻnāṭu muḻutum camaṇa, putta mataṅkaḷ paravi yirunta taṉṟic camaṇa, puttarkaḷukkum civa, viṇavarkaḷukkum iṭiyil matap pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ nikaḻnta ceyti ulaka maṟintatu. kaṭiccaṅka nāḷilō, pukār, maturi pōṉṟa talinakarkaḷil putta, camaṇakkōṭṭaṅkaḷum, paḷḷikaḷum niṟuvappaṭṭu, ammataṅkaḷ paravutaṟkāṉa ceyalkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭu vantaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyap paṭiṉum, tamiḻaracar yārum akkoḷkikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭaṉa rilli;  tamiḻp pulavarkaḷum aṉṉar. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkut tampiyārākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, cāttaṉārum muṟiyē camaṇa, puttak koḷkikaḷi viritturittārka ḷeṉiṉum, avarkaḷ civa, viṇavak koḷkikaḷiyum pārāṭṭi yiruttaliṉ, em matattiṉum avarkaṭku veṟuppilli yeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ettuṇiyō pala aracarkaḷiyum, vaḷḷalkaḷiyum paṟṟip paṉṉūṟu pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷit tokuttuḷḷa eṭṭuttoki, pattup pāṭṭukkaḷil ōriṭattēṉum camaṇa, puttak koḷki kāṇappaṭavilli. iṉṉa pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl kaṭiccaṅka māṉatu tiruñāṉacampantar kālattiṟkuc cila pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉar nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu veḷḷiṭi malipōl viḷaṅka niṟpavum, ivaṟṟi yellām ōrātu, kaṭiccaṅkam eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil nikaḻnta teṉṟum, oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil niḻnta teṉṟum, tam maṉam pōṉapaṭi kūṟuvār cilarum uḷar eṉka! iṉi tirunāvukkaracar, tamatu tēvārattil, ‘nariyik kutiri ceyvāṉum’ eṉṟum, ‘maṇiyār viyit - tirukkōṭṭi ṉiṉṟatōr tiṟamun tōṉṟum’ eṉṟum, ‘kuṭamuḻa nantīcaṉi vācakaṉāk koṇṭāṉ’ eṉṟum pāṭiyiruttalāl, avarkku muṟkālattava reṉṟum, mūṉṟu allatu nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiyava reṉṟum karutappaṭukiṟa tiruvāta vūraṭikaḷ, kōviyāril, ‘ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cinti yuḷḷum uṟivā ṉuyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāyntavoṇṭīn tamiḻiṉ, ṟuṟi’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu, aṭikaḷ kālattiṉ muṉṉarē kūṭaliṟ ṟamiḻc caṅkam naṭanta teṉpati viḷakka vallatu. orukkāl aṭikaḷ piṟkālat tavareṉa nāṭṭap paṭiṉum, atu tamiḻc caṅkattiṉ paḻimiyi vilakkutaṟ kuriya tākātu.
iṉi, ivvāṟṟāl mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpaṭṭatu eṉa niṟuvappaṭum kaṭic caṅkattil viḷaṅkiya kapilaratu kālam yāteṉa ārāyntu tuṇitaṟkuc ciṟanta karuviyākavuḷḷaṉa cilappatikāramum, ilaṅkic carittiraṅkaḷum ākum. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṇṇakikkuk kōyileṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭa nāḷil, aṅku vantirunta maṉṉarkaḷil, ‘kaṭalcūḻilaṅkik kayavāku vēntaṉum’ oruvaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ kaṇṇakipāl varam peṟṟuc ceṉṟu, ilaṅki yilum naṅkikku viḻāc ceytāṉ eṉṟum, cilappatikāra varantaru kātiyālum, uri peṟu kaṭṭuriyālum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ilaṅkic carittiraṅ kaḷil, kayavāku eṉṉum peyaruṭiyār iruvaruḷareṉṟum, avaruḷ mutaṟ kayavākuvē cilappatikārattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭava ṉeṉṟum carita vallunar yāvarum oru paṭittākat tuṇikiṉṟaṉar. ik kayavākuviṉ kālam, makā vamcam kūṟukiṟapaṭi, ki. pi. 113 - 135. ākum eṉṟē koṇṭaṉar, ‘āyirat teṇṇūṟu āṇṭu kaḷiṉ muntiya tamiḻar’ eṉṉum nūliyaṟṟiya vi. kaṉaka capip piḷḷi mutaliyōr. tiru. es. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār, em.ē., ‘makā vamcam ki. pi. 113 - 135 eṉṟu kūṟum’ eṉak kūṟit, tammuṭiya koḷkiyāka, ‘mutaṟ kayavākuviṉ āṭcik kālam ki. pi. 179 - 201 ākum’ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.1 tiru. kē. ji. caṅkariyar, kayavākuviṉ kālam ki. pi 154 - 176 eṉṟu kūṟuvatōṭu, kōvalaṉiṟantatu 157 - eṉṟum, kaṇṇakikkuk kōyi leṭuppittu viḻāviyaṟṟiyatu 160 - eṉṟum, cōtiṭak kuṟippu mutaliya pala ātaravu koṇṭu viḷakkukiṉṟaṉar.2 ippoḻutu ivviṟutik koḷkiyiṉi vittuk koṇṭu, mēl ārāycci nikaḻttatum. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aracuvīṟṟirunta kālam aimpattintu āṇṭu eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattu, aintām pattiṉ patikat tiṟutik kaṭṭuriyāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avaṉ kaṇṇakikkuk kōyi leṭuppitta kālattil, avaṉatu āṭciyil aimpatiyāṇṭu āyiṟ ṟeṉpatum, avaṉ piṟantu ēṟakkuṟiya eḻupatiyāṇṭu āka vēṇṭu meṉpatum, māṭala maṟiyōṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉi nōkki, 
3 viyaṅ kāval pūṇṭaniṉ ṉalyāṇ
  ṭiyin tiraṭṭi ceṉṟataṟ piṉṉum
  aṟakkaḷa vēḷvi ceyyā tiyāṅkaṇum
  maṟakkaḷa vēḷvi ceyvō yāyiṉi
eṉṟum,
 ‘narimutir yākki nīyuṅ kaṇṭaṉi’
eṉṟum kūṟiyiruttalāṟ ṟeḷiyappaṭum. ivvāṟṟāl, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aracukaṭṭi lēṟiyatu ki. pi. 110 - ām āṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.1 paṭavē, avaṉ tantiyākiya imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ aimpatteṭṭiyāṇṭu aracu vīṟṟiruntā ṉeṉṟu patiṟṟup pattāl aṟiyappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉ ariyaṇi yēṟiyatu ki. pi 52 - l eṉṟu koḷḷalām. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉip patiṟṟup pattil aintām pattāṟ pāṭiya paraṇar eṉṉum pulavar perumāṉ, avaṉ ṟanti yākiya neṭuñcēralātaṉum, cōḻaṉ vēṟpaḵṟaṭakkip peruviṟaṟ kiḷḷiyum pōrppuṟattup porutu vīḻnta kāliyil iraṅkip pāṭiya pāṭṭu oṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivarē karikālaṉ ṟantiyākiya uruvappaḵṟē riḷañ cēṭceṉṉiyip pāṭiyiruttalōṭu, karikālaṉ makaḷākiya ātimantiyiṉ ceytiyip palaviṭattuk kūṟukiṉṟār. iv vāṟṟāl, cōḻaṉ karikālaṉum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum oru kālattava reṉṟu karutalām. eṉiṉum, cilappatikārattil karikālaṉ ceytikaḷ iṟanta kālattavākak kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum, maturik kāṇṭattu iṟutik kaṭṭuriyil neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉiyum, vañcik kāṇṭattu iṟutik kaṭṭuriyil ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiyum kiḷanteṭuttōtiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, pukār kāṇṭattu iṟutik kaṭṭuriyil aracar peyar yātum kūṟātu viṭṭiruttalum, karikālaṉ makaḷāya ātimanti, taṉ kaṇavaṉik kāviri aṭittuk koṇṭu pōkat, tāṉ puṉaliṉ piṉ pulampic ceṉṟu, taṉ kaṇavaṉi mīṭṭum eytiya ceytiyik, kaṇṇaki, vañciṉa māliyuḷ, eṭutturit tatākak kūṟutalum, maṇimēkaliyuḷḷum,  akkālattup pukāri lāṇṭavaṉ neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi yeṉṟu kūṟap paṭutalōṭu, karikālaṉ avaṉukku muṟpaṭṭavaṉ eṉpatu tōṉṟak kūṟiyirut talum ākiyavaṟṟāl, karikālaṉ akkālattu iruntilaṉ eṉpatu viḷaṅkutaliṉ, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu iḷamiyilum, imayavarampaṉatu āṭcikkālattilum avaṉ viḷaṅkiṉāṉ eṉal poruntum. ivvāṟṟāl, karikālaṉatu āṭcikkālam ki. pi. 55 allatu 60 - kku mēl 110 - kku uṭpaṭṭa kālam eṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. kayavākuviṉ kālamum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālamum iṉṉum irupatu muppatu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpeṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭiṉ, karikālaṉ āṭci ki. pi. 25 - kkum 40 - kkum iṭippaṭṭa kālattil toṭaṅkiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷalām. ivvāṟirukka, ñāṉacākarattil eḻunta ‘tirukkuṟaḷārāycci’ yeṉṉum ariya poruḷuri, karikālaṉ ki. mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉiṭiyil araciyaṟṟap pukuntāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
aḵtu avvāṟu kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam, patiṟṟup pattup peṟṟa cēravaracarkaḷil, imayavarampaṉukku muṉ peruñcēralirum poṟiyum, avaṉukkumuṉ celvakkaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉum muṟiyē aracu purintārkaḷeṉṟum, karikālaṉoṭu poruta peruñ cēralātaṉ, celvakkaṭuṅkōvukku muṉ āṇṭava ṉeṉṟum koṇṭamiyē, maṟṟum, atu, ‘karikāṟ peruvaḷat tāṉukkum cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkum iṭiyē oru nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḻintami ivaṟṟāṟ naṉku teḷiyappaṭum’ eṉkiṉṟatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉip pāṭiya paraṇarē, karikālaṉ ṟantiyiyum pāṭiyirukkum poḻutu, oru nūṟṟāṇṭu eṅṅaṉam iṭip paṭṭiruttal kūṭum? imayavarampaṉum celvakkaṭuṅkōvum vēḷāvik kōmāṉpatumaṉ eṉpavaṉuṭiya makaḷiri maṇañ ceytuḷḷārkaḷeṉṟu patiṟṟuppattāṟ ṟeritaliṉ, avviru varum ēṟakkuṟiya oru kālattava reṉṟu koṇṭuviṭiṉ, avvārāycciyum karikālaṉ ki. pi.  mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉiṭiyil viḷaṅkiyava ṉeṉṟē kāṭṭuvatākum. ‘mutalāvatu karikālaṉ’ eṉṉum ariya uri nūlum, ivvārāycciyiyē taṉakkurittākak koṇṭu, ‘karikālaṉ ki. mu. mutal nūṟṟaṟāṇṭiṉ iṭippaṭṭa kālattu aracu kaṭṭil ēṟiṉavaṉ’ eṉṟu muṭittiruttaliṉ, ituvum piḻiyeṉal kūṟavēṇṭā. 
iṉi, uruvappaḵṟēriḷañcēṭceṉṉiyip pāṭiya paraṇar, avaṉ makaṉukku makaḷākiya ātimantiyiṉ kaṟpu mēmpāṭṭip pukaḻnturittalāṉum, vēṟucila kuṟippuk kaḷāṉum, neṭunāḷ uyir vāḻntiruntavareṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ātaliṉ, paraṇaratu kālam ēṟakkuṟiya ki. pi. 50 - 140 ākumeṉak karutal amiyum. paraṇarāṟ pāṭappeṟṟa aracarum, kuṟunila maṉṉar mutalāyiṉārum mikap palarāvar. avarkaḷiṟ cilari iṅkē kuṟippiṭutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. uruvap paḵṟēriḷañ cēṭceṉṉi, vēṟpaḵṟa ṭakkipperunaṟkiḷḷi, neṭuñcēralātaṉ, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, viyāvikkōpperum pēkaṉ, atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉ añci, āy, ōri, titiyaṉ, naḷḷi, naṉṉaṉ eṉpōr.
iṉi, atiyamāṉ añciyip pala pāṭṭukkaḷāṟ peritum pārāṭṭiya oḷaviyār, añciyip pārttu1, ‘aṉṟum pāṭunark kariyi iṉṟum paraṇaṉ pāṭiṉaṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, paraṇarum oḷaviyārum orē kālattava reṉpatu peṟappaṭum. añciyip pāṭiya oḷaviyār, avaṉ makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉiyiyum, ciṟitu piṟkālattavarākat terikiṟa ukkirapperuvaḻuti, māveṇkō, irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpōriyum pāṭiyiruttalāl, avar neṭitu nāḷ uyir vāḻntava reṉṉuṅ koḷki valiyuṟu kiṉṟatu. avar, atiyamāṉ koṭutta nellikkaṉi yuṇṭu neṭitu nāḷ vāḻntava reṉpatu,2 ‘nīyē toṉṉilip, perumali viṭarakat tarumicik koṇṭa, ciṟiyili nellit tīṅkaṉi kuṟiyā, tātal niṉṉakat taṭakkic, cātal nīṅka vemakkīt taṉiyē’ eṉṟu avarē añciyi nōkkik kūṟutalāṟ ṟeḷiyap paṭum. ivvāṟṟāl, oḷaviyār ki. pi. 65 mutal 165 variyilāvatu vāḻntavarātal vēṇṭum. 
iṉik, ‘kapila paraṇar’ eṉat toṉṟu toṭṭu varum vaḻakkāṉē, kapilarum, paraṇarum oru kālattiṉa reṉṟum, otta perumiya reṉṟum, oṉṟiya kēṇmiya reṉṟum karutalākum. viyāvik kōpperumpēkaṉāl tuṟakkappaṭṭa kaṇṇaki kāraṇamāka, avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu, avaṉukku irakka muṇṭākumpaṭi pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ kapilar, paraṇar, aricil kiḻār, peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār eṉpōrākaliṉ, ivarkaḷellām oru kālattiṉa reṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷāl pāṭap paṭṭōrum ēṟakkuṟiya oru kālattiṉarātal vēṇṭum. celvak kaṭuṅkōvip pāṭiya kapilar, māntarañcēralirum poṟi iraṅkik kūṟumpaṭi, avaṉukku muṟkālattilō, avaṉatu iḷam paruvattilō ivvulakiṉi viṭṭakaṉṟār ākaliṉ, avaratu kālam ki. pi 50 mutal 125 vari eṉṟu koḷḷutal poruntum. 
iṉi, akkālattiṟ ṟamiḻakattiṉ nilimiyic ciṟitu ārāyntu nōkkiṉ, atu maṟṟen nāṭṭiṉum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟ ṟeṉpatu pulaṉām. tamiḻp pēraracarkaḷ, ‘añcāmi, īki, aṟivu, ūkkam’ mutaliyaṉa eñcātu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ, neṭuñ cēralātaṉ, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, āriyap paṭi kaṭanta neñceḻiyaṉ mutaliya tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ, vaṭakkil imayamali kāṟuñceṉṟu, āṅkuḷḷa vēntarkaḷi veṉṟu, tiṟi peṟṟu, imayattil tammuṭiya puliyum, villum, kayalum poṟittu, mīṇṭārka ḷeṉṉiṉ, avarkaḷuṭiya ūkkamum, vīramum aḷaviṭaṟpāla vāmō? aṉṟiyum, avarkaḷ kāṭu tirutti nāṭākkiyum, nīrnilikaḷip perukkiyum, maṟṟum pala purintum, kuṭi taḻuvik kōlōcci vantamiyāl, maḻi vēṇṭumaḷavu peyyā niṟka, ‘vēli yāyiram viḷiyuṭṭāka’ eṉṉumpaṭi, nila ṉellām tāṅkā viḷivu peṟṟiruntaṉa. paṇṭikaḷālum, kappalkaḷālum, uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikamum, puṟanāṭṭu vāṇikamum naṉku naṭi peṟṟu vantaṉa. maṟṟum, ulakap potu vāḻkkikku vēṇṭap paṭuvaṉavākiya neyvu, ciṟpaṅkaḷum, irumpu, veṇkalam, cempu, poṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ toḻilkaḷum, piṟavum mēm paṭṭiruntaṉa. aracarāyiṉār, pala araciyal maṉṟaṅkaḷamittu muṟi ceytu vantaṉar. ivvāṟṟāl, makkaḷāyiṉār, paciyum, piṇiyum, pakiyumiṉṟi, yāvaruṭaṉum kalantu uṟavāṭi, yiṉpuṟṟu vantaṉar. muṭiyuṭi vēntarum ciṟṟaracarum, tām tamiḻp pulamiyuṭi yavarāy viḷaṅkiya taṉṟit, tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ pāl āṉṟa matippuṭiyavarāyum iruntaṉar. karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ, paṭṭiṉappāli pāṭiya uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉārkkup patiṉāṟunūṟāyiram poṉ paricilaḷittāṉ. patiṟṟup pattup pāṭiya pulavarkaḷukkuc cēra maṉṉarkaḷ paṉṉūṟāyiram poṉ parintaḷittu nāṭum, piṟavum naṉi vaḻaṅkiṉar. pāri, pēkaṉ mutaliya vēḷirkula vaḷḷalkaḷum, atiyamāṉ mutaliya  piṟa vaḷḷalkaḷum, māriyaṉṉa variyā vaṇmiyāl pulavarkaḷukkup poṉṉaṇi yāṉiyum, tērum aḷittup pōṟṟi vantaṉar. ivvāṟu tamiḻp puravalar mikku viḷaṅkiṉamiyiṉ, tamiḻp pulavarum eṇṇiṟan tārāyiṉar. peṇ pālāruḷḷum palar pulami ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar. aci villāta ceḻuntamiḻ vaḻakkāṉatu ayal moḻi vaḻakkiṉi veṉṟu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. makkaḷellārum tamiḻamiḻ tuṇṭu, aṟivum, tiruvum, āṟṟalum uṭiyarāy, aṟam, poruḷiṉ paṅkaḷip perukki, vāḻntu vantaṉar.
iṭam
ivvāciriyar piṟattalāṟ perumiyeytiya ūr yāteṉṟu pārkkiṉ, tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇamāṉatu, caṅkappulavar nāṟpatteṇmarum 1palaviṭat tiruntu, orē kālattu maturikku vantaṉar eṉṟu  kūṟutaliṉ, atukoṇṭu tuṇitaṟku iṭaṉilli. kapilarakaval eṉṉum puttakam kūṟuvatum, muṉṉarē piḻiyeṉṟu kaṭiyappaṭṭatu. perumpaṟṟap puliyūr nampi yiyaṟṟiya tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattu, vātavūraṭikaḷukku ñāṉōpatēcañ ceyta tiruviḷiyāṭalil, 
nītiyār matūka nīḻal neṭṭili yiruppi yeṉṟōr 
kātalkūr kaviti pāṭuṅ kapilaṉār piṟanta mūtūr 
cōticēr vakuḷa nīḻal cilampoli tuḷaṅkak kāṭṭum 
vētanā yakaṉār vāḻum viyaṉṟiru vāta vūrāl.
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, kapilar piṟantavūr, pāṇṭi nāṭṭi luḷḷa tiruvātavūr eṉṉum tiruppati yeṉṟē koḷḷaṟ pālatu. maturiyum, paṟampum mutaliyaṉa ivvāciriyar vatinta iṭaṅkaḷākum.
kapilarip pāṭiṉōr
nakkīrar, peruṅkuṉṟūrkiḻār, poruntiliḷaṅkīraṉār, veḷḷi vītiyār eṉpōr ivarip pukaḻntu pāṭiṉōrāvar. 
pāṭappaṭṭōr
ivarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭōr, cēramāṉ celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ, pāri, pēkaṉ, maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri, ōri, naḷḷi mutaliyōr.
kapilar pāṭiyaṉa
1. kuṟiñcip pāṭṭu
2. kuṟiñcikkali
3. kuṟiñcikkuṟunūṟu
4. naṟṟiṇi
5. kuṟuntoki       ivaṟṟiṟ ciṟcila pāṭṭukkaḷ  
6. akanāṉūṟu	
7. puṟanāṉūṟu	
8. patiṟṟuppattil ēḻāmpattu
9. iṉṉā nāṟpatu
10. mūttanāyaṉār tiruviraṭṭimaṇimāli
11. civaperumāṉ tiruviraṭṭimaṇimāli 
12. civaperumāṉ tiruvantāti
13. kapilam. 
iviyum, ‘neṭṭili yiruppi’ eṉṉum taṉip pāṭṭum, tiruvaḷḷuvamāliyiluḷḷa ‘tiṉiyaḷavu’ eṉṉum veṇpāvum ivariyaṟṟiyaṉa vākum. 
4. kuṟiñcippāṭṭu ārāycci
makkaḷ aṟivu vaḷarccikku iṉṟi yamiyāk karuviyā yuḷḷatu moḻi. tam karuttip piṟarkku aṟivikkavum, piṟar karuttit tām aṟintu koḷḷavum, moḻipōl tuṇiyāvatu piṟitilli. ivvāṟṟāl, makkaḷiyakkattiṟkum, ākkattiṟkum karuviyāyuḷḷa moḻikaḷum palavāka vuḷḷaṉa. avvam moḻikaḷiliruntē avaṟṟi yuṭiya tollōr
kaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷum aṟiyappaṭum. nam tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻāṉatu iṉṉa kālattil utittatu eṉa evvāṟṟāṉum aṟiya vārāta attuṇip paḻimiyum, tiruttamum uṭiyatākum. tamiḻ moḻi, vaḻakku eṉavum, ceyyuḷ eṉavum iru kūṟupaṭṭu iyaṅkukiṉṟatu. cāttā vā,  nī poruḷit tēṭiṉāy, avaṉ mikap pacittāṉ, yāṉ cōṟu koṭuttēṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla, yāvarum vaḻaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭuk kūṟum coṟṟoṭarkaḷ vaḻakku eṉappaṭum. iḻintuḷōr. palavāṟu citivupaṭak kūṟuvaṉavum oruvāṟṟāl vaḻakkē yāyiṉum, avi aṟivuṭiyōrāl mēṟ koḷḷappaṭāvākum. nallaṟi vuṭiyōrāl palvakic coṟkaḷālum amiti peṟac ceyyap paṭuvaṉa ceyyuḷ eṉappaṭum. pāṭṭukkaḷē yaṉṟic, cūttiram, uri mutalāyiṉavum, ceyyuḷ eṉṉum peyarāl, cūttirac ceyyuḷ, uric ceyyuḷ eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiṉa. yām ippoḻutu ceyyuḷ eṉṉum peyarip pāṭṭukkē uriya tākkic cūttiram allātavikaḷi uri yeṉavum, pāṭṭu eṉavum iruvakip paṭuttuk koḷ kiṉṟōm. uriyiṉiyum, palvakip paṭuttuk kūṟukiṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ iṭiyē viravi varum uri cilappatikārattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu. ceyyuḷ allatu cūttirapporuḷit teḷivuṟuttum urikaḷ tamiḻil mikkuḷḷaṉa. yātāṉu moru varalāṟu allatu uṇmi yiṉic ceyyuḷiṉṟit toṭarcciyāyk kūṟum urinaṭiyā ṉiyaṉṟa nūl ‘urinūl’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkaṟkuriyatu. attakiya nūl paṇṭic cāṉṟōr ceytatāka namakku oṉṟum kiṭittilatu. urinaṭiyāṉatu, payilvāratu aṟivu nuṇuki niṉṟu ārāytaṟ kēṟṟa tiṭpamum, kūṟum poruḷuk kēṟṟa ōciyum uṭiyataṉṟu ākaliṉ, pāṭṭukkaḷ pōla, uṇarviṉi yeḻuppi, iṉpañ ceyva  tākātu. ōr variyaṟi yiṉmiyiṉ, citiviṉṟi nilipeṟuvatu mākātu. eṉiṉum, eḷitiṟ poruḷ viḷaṅkuvatāyc curuṅkiya aṟiviṉarkkup payaṉ ṟaruvatu uri naṭiyē ākaliṉ, ovvoru moḻikkum uri nūlkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa eṉpatiṟ ṟaṭiyilli. avvakiyil tamiḻ moḻi vaḷarcciyuṟā tiruntatu iraṅkat takka toṉṟē. 
tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ māṭci aḷaviṭaṟ pālaṉa valla. piṟa moḻic ceyyuḷi yellām tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷ veṉṟu viṭṭaṉa eṉṉalām. namatu muṉṉiyōr tam nuṇṇaṟi vāṉum, uḻippāṉum kaṇṭaṟinta arum poruṇ maṇikaḷi yellām namakku utavumāṟu tokuttu vitta kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ pāṭṭukkaḷēyākum. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṟpaṭṭa nam periyōr, eṉpu, tōl, narampukaḷāl pātikkap paṭṭa uṭampiṉi viṭuttu, eḻuttu, aci, cīrkaḷāl yākkap paṭṭa uṭampiṉi yeṭuttu, namakketiril vīṟṟirukka, nām avarkaḷōṭu aḷavaḷāviyum, avarkaḷ kūṟum uṟuti moḻi kaḷik kēṭṭum, aṟivum, āṇmiyum uṭiyarāy iṉpuṟṟirut taliṉum, peṟutaṟkariya pēṟu yātuḷatu? ettaṉiyō āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu viḷaṅkiyiruntu, piṉ māṟutaluṟṟu maṟintu pōṉa mali, nilaṉ, kaṭal, yāṟu mutalāyiṉa vellām, pulavaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷil tam paṇṭiya vuruvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkutalik kāṇumiṭattu, namakkuṇṭākum uṇarcciyi yāteṉṟu kūṟalākum? ulaka meṉṉum kāṭci maṉṟattil uyarvaṟa vuyarnta oruvaṉāl kālantōṟum amittu vikkappaṭum eṇṇil pētamāṉa poruḷkaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṉ paṇpukaḷiyum, poṟi kaḷeṉṉum varivu kōl koṇṭu, uḷḷa meṉṉum kiḻiyil oḻuṅkuṟat tīṭṭi vitta ōviyarākum nallicip pulavarkaḷ, avvōviyak kāṭciyil uṟittu niṟkuṅkāl uṇṭāya vuṇarcciyē vaṭivāka, aṉṉavartam cennāviṉiṉ ṟeḻunta pāṭṭukkaḷiṉum namakkiṉpam viḷippaṉa yāvi yuḷa? ‘ urukip peruki yuḷaṅkuḷira mukantu koṇṭu, parukaṟ kiṉiya paraṅ karuṇit taṭaṅ kaṭal’ pōṉṟu, parukum toṟum parukun toṟum vaṟṟāta pērūṟṟāyk, kaḻi pēriṉimi curantūṭṭuvaṉa pāṭṭukkaḷēyā meṉka.
ikkālattē poruṭpolivu ciṟitumiṉṟi, etuki, mōṉi yamiyac coṟkaḷit teṟṟivippatē pāṭṭeṉṟu karutik koṇṭiruppārum uḷar. maṭakkum, tiripumākac coṟ kaḷi aṭukki vittu, iraṇṭoru pōlip pāṭṭiyaṟṟi ṉārip pulavar variciyiṟ cērtteṇṇuvārum uḷar. antō! ivaṟṟukkellām kāraṇam, pāṭṭiṉ ṟaṉmi iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyāmiyēyākum. tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya, ‘nōkku’ mutaliya iṉṟiyamiyāc ceyyuḷuṟuppuk kaḷi yoḻittu viṭṭup, piṟkālattār yāppilakkaṇam vakuttatum, pāṭṭiṉṟaṉmi yaṟiyātu, piḻi neṟiyiṟ piṟar cellutaṟku ētuvāyiṟ ṟeṉka. ivaṟṟi yellām otukkic, curuṅkiya coṟkaḷil āḻntu akaṉṟa poruḷkaḷi yuṭiyaṉa vāy, iyaṟki neṟi vaḻātaṉavāy, uṇarviṉi yeḻuppi, meyppāṭu tōṟṟuvikkum cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷiyē, aṟivuṭiyōr ‘pāṭṭu’ eṉṟu koḷvārāvar. attakiya pāṭṭukkaḷ tām  eṇṇiṟantaṉavuḷa. avaṟṟuḷ, ‘kapilaratu pāṭṭu’  eṉa āṉṟōrāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭum perumi vāynta ceyyuṭṭuṟikaḷil, appulavar perumāṉatu aṭittuṇiyē puṇiyākac ceṉṟu, kiṭikkum poruḷkaḷi veḷip paṭuttal karuti, mutaṟkaṇ kuṟiñcippāṭṭu eṉṉum tuṟiyiṟ cellukiṉṟām. 
kuṟiñcippāṭṭu eṉpatu, caṅkattuc cāṉṟōrāl ceyyappaṭṭu, akkālattē tokukkap paṭṭaṉavākiya 1pattuppāṭṭuḷ eṭṭāvatāka vuḷḷatu. iḵtu, āriyavaracaṉ 2pirakattaṉi tamiḻaṟivittaṟkuk kapilar pāṭiyatu eṉṟu, ippāṭṭiṉatu uriyiṟutiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa toṭarāl terikiṉṟatu. eṉavē, āriya varacaṉākiya pirakatta ṉeṉpāṉ oruvaṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ pōntu, ciṟitu tamiḻ payiṉṟa tuṇi yāṉē, tamiḻiṉ perumiyil aiyuṟavu koṇṭiruntāṉ eṉavum, ataṉi yaṟinta nallicip pulavarum, pulaṉaḻukkaṟṟa antaṇāḷarum ākiya kapilar, avaṉukkut tamiḻiṉ oppuyar villā māṭciyit terivikka eṇṇi, ippāṭṭiṉi yiyaṟṟik kaṟpittāreṉavum, itaṉik kēṭṭa āriya varacaṉ tamiḻc cuviyil īṭupaṭṭut, tamiḻiṉi mēṉmēluṅ kaṟṟuc ceyyuḷiyaṟṟa valla pulavaṉāyiṉāṉ eṉavum ōrntu koḷḷal cālum. ivaṉ pāṭiya pāṭṭu oṉṟu kuṟuntokiyiṟ kāṇap paṭuvatu, ivaṉ kaṟṟu vallaṉāyiṉāṉ eṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttu vatākum. 
ippāṭṭu; 261 - aṭikaḷiyuṭiya akavaṟpā ākum. tamiḻc ceyyuḷiṉ ōci yamitikkup pā eṉṟu peyar. pāvi yuṭiya ceyyuḷum, ākupeyarāl ‘pā’ eṉa vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṟatu. ceppal, akaval, tuḷḷal, tūṅkal, eṉac ceyyuḷōci nāṉku vakippaṭum. ceppalāvatu, oruvaṉ kēṭpa, maṟṟeruvaṉ māṟu kūṟuvatu pōl, iṭiyiṭi niṉṟu cellum iyaṟki yōciyām. akavalāvatu, oruvaṉ tāṉ karutiya vāṟellām variyātu kūṟa, iṭiyiṭi niṟṟaliṉṟit toṭarpu paṭṭuc cellum iyaṟki yōciyām. tuḷḷalāvatu, ici vēṟu pāṭu tōṉṟat tuḷḷic cellum ceyaṟki yōciyām. tūṅkalāvatu, cīrtoṟum niṟittu niṉṟucellum ceyaṟki yōciyām. ceppalōci yuṭiyatu veṇpā eṉavum, akavalōci yuṭiyatu akavaṟpā eṉavum, tuḷḷalōci yuṭiyatu kalippā  eṉavum, tūṅkalōci yuṭiyatu vañcippā eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. ivi yaṉṟic ceppalum, akavalum viravivaruvatu maruṭpā eṉavum, ceppa lōciyēṉum, ellā vōciyumēṉum peṟṟuk, kalikkuriya vuṟuppukkaḷuṭiyatāy varuvatu paripāṭṭu eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. ippāṭṭu muḻutum orē toṭarcciyāyc cellum akavalōci uṭittā yiruttaliṉ, iḵtu akavaṟ pāvā meṉpatu aṟika. 
 kuṟiñcit tiṇikkuriya poruḷ kūṟutaliṉ, itaṟkuk kuṟiñcippāṭṭu eṉṟu peyar kūṟiṉār. ulakattup poru ḷellā vaṟṟiyum, mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ, urip poruḷ eṉa mūvakip paṭuttuk kūṟuvatu tamiḻnūl vaḻakku. nilaṉum kālamum mutaṟporuḷ eṉappaṭum. nīr, tī mutaliya maṟṟip pūtaṅkaḷum nilaṉeṉa aṭaṅkum. piṟa poruḷellām ivaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟutaliṉ, ivi mutaṟporuḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. tēvar, makkaḷ, vilaṅku, puḷ, maram mutalāyiṉavum, uṇavu, ceyal, paṟi, yāḻ mutalāyiṉavum, karupporuḷ eṉappaṭum. nilattiṉum, kālattiṉum tōṟṟutaliṉ, ivi karupporuḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. (karu = tōṟṟam). avvannilattu makkaṭku vēṟu vēṟāyt tōṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ, teyvamum karupporuḷāyiṟṟu. makkaḷ eytaṟ pālaṉavākiya aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉpaṉa uripporuḷ eṉappaṭum. uriya poruḷākaliṉ urip poruḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟi, akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ eṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkik kūṟuvatu tamiḻ nūṉmuṟi. iṉpam akapporuḷ. aṟaṉum, poruḷum puṟapporuḷ. vīṭu, akam puṟam iraṇṭiṉum aṭaṅkum. iṉpam, tāṉē yuṇartalaṉṟip, piṟarkku iṉṉa taṉmitteṉak kūṟalākāmiyiṉ, akam eṉṟum, aṟaṉum, poruḷum piṟarkkuk kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, puṟam eṉṉum peyar  peṟuvavāyiṉa, iṉpamum palavāyiṉum, oru kālattu oru poruḷāl aimpulaṉum nukarum ciṟappuṭiya kāmaviṉpamē iṅku koḷḷappaṭṭatu. avviṉpantāṉum, cuvi mikuti tōṉṟa ulaka naṭiyōṭu ottalum, ovvāmiyum uṭiya tākkik kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. uruvum, tiruvum, paruvamum, kuṭiyum, kuṇaṉum, aṉpum mutaliyavikaḷāl tammuḷ oppumi yuṭiyarāya talivaṉum, taliviyum, piṇi, mūppātiya viṉṟi nukarum iṉpamē akam eṉak kūṟappaṭum iṉpa meṉka.
avviṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇamāya oḻukkam akattiṇi yeṉappaṭum. atu, kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli, peruntiṇi, kikkiḷi eṉa eḻuvakippaṭum. aṟam, poruḷukkuk kāraṇamāya oḻukkam puṟattiṇi yeṉappaṭum. atuvum, veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki, kāñci, pāṭāṇṭiṇi eṉa eḻuvakippaṭum. ciṟcila oppumi paṟṟik, kuṟiñci mutaliya akattiṇi yēḻukkum, veṭci mutaliya puṟattiṇiyēḻum muṟiyē iṉamāka āciriyar tol kāppiyaṉār kūṟiyuḷḷār. akattiṇiyuḷ, peruntiṇiyum, kikkiḷiyum allāta aintumē ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. kuṟiñci yeṉpatu, kūṭalum, kūṭal nimittamum ām. mulli yeṉpatu, piriviṉ kaṇ āṟṟiyiruttalum, iruttal nimittamum ām. maruta meṉpatu, ūṭalum, ūṭal nimittamum ām. neytaleṉpatu, iraṅkalum, iraṅkal nimittamum ām. pāli eṉpatu, piritalum, pirital nimittamum ām. kuṟiñci mutaliya tiṇikaḷukku, mutaṟ poruḷākiya nilam, poḻutukaḷum, karup poruḷākiya teyvam mutaliyavum vēṟuvēṟu vakukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiñcikku nilam maliyum, mali cārnta iṭamum ām. perum poḻutu, aippaciyum kārttikiyum ākiya kūtirp paruvamum,  mārkaḻiyum, tiyum ākiya muṉpaṉip paruvamum ām. ciṟu poḻutu, apparuvaṅkaḷatu iraviṉ naṭukkūṟākiya yāmam ām. karupporuḷ, murukavēḷ ākiya teyvamum, kuṟavar mutaliya makkaḷum, puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum, mayil mutaliya puṭkaḷum, vēṅki mutaliya maraṅkaḷum, maṟṟum innilattiṉum, kālattiṉum tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷum piṟavum ām. ivvāṟṟāl, ippāṭṭiṉ kaṭ kūṟappaṭum poruḷ ivi yātal vēṇṭum eṉṟaṟika. ivaṟṟuḷ uripporuḷ iṉṟēl poruṭ pēṟu iṉṟām ākaliṉ, aḵtu orutaliyākak kūṟappaṭuvatām. ataṉic ciṟappikkavē mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷkaḷum kūṟappaṭum.
iṉi, ippāṭṭil urip poruḷiṉ eppakuti kūṟappaṭuvatu eṉavum, itu, yār, yārukku, eppoḻutu, evvāṟu, kūṟuvatāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷatu eṉavum mutaṟkaṇ aṟintu kōṭal vēṇṭum. kuṟiñci mutaliya aka voḻukkamellām, kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉa iruvakippaṭuttuk kūṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟil kaḷaviṉṟik kaṟpu nikaḻumā ṟilli yeṉavum, kaḷavē ciṟappuṭit teṉavum kūṟupa. kaḷavāvatu, muṟkūṟiya uruvum, tiruvum mutaliyavaṟṟāl tammuḷ oppumi yuṭiyarāya talivaṉum taliviyum, koṭuppārum, aṭuppāru miṉṟi, ūḻ vakiyāl tāmē yetirppaṭṭu iṉpam tuyttu varuvatu. mutalil ivvāṟu oru poḻilil etirppaṭṭuc cērvatu iyaṟki eṉavum, ataṉ piṉ avarkaḷ pirintu ceṉṟu, muṉ pōṉṟu mīṭṭum avviṭattē kūṭappeṟiṉ, aḵtu iṭantalippāṭu eṉavum, pāṅkaṉāṟ kūṭap peṟiṉ, atu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉavum, tōḻiyāṟ kūṭap peṟiṉ, atu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭuk kaḷavu nāl vakippaṭum. pāṅkaṉum, tōḻiyum, talimakkaṭku uyirpōṟ ciṟantārākaliṉ, avarkaḷaṟivatu piṟa raṟivatākātu, talimakka ḷaṟivatē pōlum eṉka. ippāṭṭu, iyaṟkiyum, piṉṉikaḻum kūṭṭaṅkaḷukku nimittamum kūṟuvatu eṉpar nacciṉarkkiṉiyar. iyaṟkiyiṉ piṉpu, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻntu varuṅkāl, iravukkuṟi yiṭiyīṭu paṭṭut, talimakaṉ paṉmuṟi vaṟitē mīḷā niṟpa, talivi āṟṟāmi perituṭiyaḷāy, uṭal melivuṟalāṉāḷ. taliviyiṉ mēṉi iḷittu vēṟu paṭṭirut talik kaṇṭa ceviliyāṉavaḷ, iv viḷippukkuk kāraṇamāṉa nōy iṉṉateṉa aṟiyum poruṭṭuk kuṟi colluvāri viṉāvi, avarkaḷ teyvattā ṉāyiṟṟu eṉak kūṟaliṉ, teyvaṅkaṭkuc ciṟappuc ceytu, piṉṉum nōy nīṅkāmi kaṇṭu varuntukiṉṟāḷ. anniliyil, talivi, mēlum āṟṟāmi yuṭiyaḷāy, ‘emakkuḷ nikaḻnta im maṉṟali nām tāykku aṟivuṟuttalāl paḻiyum varuvatuṇṭō?’ eṉṟu tōḻikku uṇartta, avaḷ  cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟpatāka, iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu ippāṭṭu. aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉpatu, talivaṉukkum, talivikkum nikaḻnta kūṭṭatti veḷippaṭuttal. inta nilimiyil itaṉi veḷippaṭuttalē aṟattoṭu poruntiya ceyalākaliṉ, iḵtu aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, ippāṭṭiṟkut tiṇi, kuṟiñci eṉavum, tuṟi, tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉavum koḷka. tōḻiyāṉavaḷ, uyar kuṭippiṟanta taliviyatu kaḷavoḻukkatti veḷippaṭuttum poḻutu, cevilikkuc ciṉa muṇṭākāvāṟum, taliviyiṭattātal, taṉṉiṭattātal, talivaṉiṭattātal, avaḷiṭattātal ciṟitum kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉa avaḷuṇarumāṟum, iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntatu nalviṉip payaṉē yeṉat tōṟṟumāṟum, paṭittu moḻiyum tiṟappāṭu peritum viyakkat takkatākum. ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷuṇaravē, atu naṉku viḷaṅku mākaliṉ, īṇṭu ataṉic curukkik kūṟukiṉṟām.
“tāyē! yāṉ kūṟuvataṉi virumpik kēṭpāyāka! nī tāṉ, oḷḷiya neṟṟiyi yuṭiya eṉ tōḻiyatu iḻiyiṉi nekiḻacceyta poṟuttaṟkariya, nōyik kaṇṭu, alamantu, kaṭṭiṉālum, kaḻaṅkiṉālum aṟintu kuṟicolluvāri viṉāvi, avar teyvattāṉ vanta varuttam eṉṟaliṉ, palvēṟu teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭum, ataṉāṟ ṟīrāmiyiṉ, innōyi aṟiyāta mayakkatti uṭiyiyāy, varuntā niṉṟāy. ivaḷō, taṉatu nalla vaṉappuk kuṉṟavum, tōḷ meliyavum, kūṟutaṟku arumiyālē taṉ maṉattuḷḷē maṟittirukkum, āṟṟutaṟkariya, nōyiṉik kuṟittu yāṉ neruṅkik kēṭkaluṟṟapoḻutu, eṉṉi nōkki, ‘muttāṉum, maṇiyāṉum, poṉṉāṉu miyaṉṟa aruṅkalaṉ keṭṭa tāyiṉum, piṉṉum vantu poruntum, aḵtaṉṟik, kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ amitiyum, mēmpāṭum, oḻukkamum keṭṭāl, uṇṭāya mācupōmpaṭi kaḻuvi, muṉpōlap pukaḻi niṟuttal, kuṟṟamaṟṟa aṟiviṉiyuṭiya muṉivarkkum eḷiyataṉ ṟeṉṟu toṉṉū laṟiñar kūṟuvar. yāṅkaḷ iruvēmum āynta maṉṟal ituveṉa, nām tāykku aṟivuṟuttiṉ,  namakkup pukaḻaṉṟip, paḻi varuvatu muṇṭō? aḵtilliyē. iṅṅaṉam veḷip paṭutta piṉpu, talivarkkē nammik koṭukka nērntila rāyiṉum, nām uyir pōntuṇiyum ivvaruttattip poṟuttiruppiṉ, namakku maṟumiyiṉum ikkūṭṭam kūṭuva toṉ ṟākumē’. eṉak kūṟi, āṟṟaru nōyiṉiyuṭiyaḷāy, miyal nōkkaṅ koṇṭu, ceya loḻintu, ayarntu, meliyā niṉṟāḷ. yāṉuntāṉ, māṟupaṭṭu amarilē mikkuc cellum iraṇṭu periya aracaric cantu ceyvikkum toḻilil niṉṟa cāṉṟōr pōla, niṉakkum, ivaḷatu varuttattiṟkum añcum iraṇṭu periya accattālē varuntā niṉṟēṉ. koṭuppiṉ ellā vaṟṟāṉum naṉṟāki muṭitalum, kuṭiyum, kuṇamum, cuṟṟat tutaviyum eṉu mivaṟṟi oppittup pārttup palaruṭaṉum eṇṇāmalum, nummiyiṉṟiyum, yāṅkaḷē tuṇinta, uyirkkup pātukāvalāṉa, ivvariya maṇam muṉpu nikaḻnta vāṟṟi nī naṉṟāka vuṇarumpaṭi kūṟutaṟ kamintēṉ. atu kēṭṭuc ciṉavātiruppāyāka!   tāyē! nītāṉ, viḷinta tiṉiyiṉ kaṇ vīḻum kiḷikaḷi yōṭṭi, mālip poḻutil varuvīrāka eṉac colli, emmi aṉuppukiyālē, yāṅkaḷum ceṉṟu, marattiṉucciyil, iravu kāttiruppōṉ paṇṇiṉa puli yañcum paraṇilē yēṟik, kavaṇum, taṭṭiyum mutaliyavaṟṟi muṟi muṟi kikkoṇṭu, kiḷikaḷi yōṭṭiṉēm. piṉpu, ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmi mikukiṉṟa pakaṟ poḻutilē, vāṉiṟpaṟakkum paṟavikaḷellām tām virumpum cēkkiyiṟ cellāniṟka, yāṅkaḷ, neṭiya maliyucciyi ṉiṉṟum kutikkum teḷinta nīriyuṭiya, veḷiya tukil pōlum, aḻakiya aruviyilē, tavirāta viruppuṭaṉ mēlum mēlum viḷiyāṭi, ataṉ piṉpu, paḷiṅkiṉik karittuc corintu vittāṟ pōlum cuṉiyiṟ kuṭintu viḷiyāṭuṅ kāl, eṅkaḷ maṉattiṟku viruppamāṉa pala pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉēm. āṉapiṉ, ematu piṉṉiya kūntaliṉ nīrip piḻintu, īram pulartti, uḷḷiṭam civanta kaṇṇiṉēmāy. āmpal, aṉiccam, ceṅkaḻunīr, kuṟiñci, veṭci mutaliya palvakip pūkkaḷiyum, mikka vēṭkiyuṭaṉ palakālum tirintu paṟittu vantu, tūytāṉa akaṉṟa pāṟiyilē kuvittup paṟavikaḷiṉ ōciyākiya palliyaṅkaḷi yuṭiya malip pakkattiṟ puṉattē, teḷinta iciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya coṟkaḷāl iṭiyiṭi kiḷikaḷi yōppiyum, puṟavitaḻkaḷik kaḷin teṟintu, oḻuṅku peṟak koytu iyaṟṟiya ‘taḻi’ yeṉṉum uṭiyiṉi iṭiyilē uṭuttiyum, palvēṟuruviṉ aḻakaminta mālikaḷi emmuṭiya melliruṅ kūntalil aḻakupeṟac cuṟṟiyum, neruppup pōlum niṟattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum aḻakiya taḷiri yuṭiya acōkiṉatu tātu utirkiṉṟa taṇṇiya niḻalilē mēviyiruntēm. aṅṅaṉam irukkiyil, āṇṭu, ōr tōṉṟal paṉṉiṟamuḷḷa palvakip pūkkaḷālum toṭukkap peṟṟa toṭiyaliyum, veḷiya tāḻi maṭaliṟ poruntac ceyta kaṇṇiyiyum ceṉṉiyil aḻakuṟac cūṭiyum, picci malarāṟ ṟoṭutta  oru caramākiya māliyic cuṟṟiyum, acōkiṉatu taḷirit tōḷil vīḻntaciyumpaṭi oru kātilē cerukiyum, cantaṉam pūcappeṟṟu, akaṉṟu, uyarntu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa cem poṟiyi yuṭiya mārpilē toṉṟu paṭṭu varum ciṟanta aṇikalaṅkaḷōṭu, naṟiya mālikaḷip polivuṟa aṇintum, nuṇṭoḻilaminta kacciṉi uṭiyiṉ mēl iṟukak kaṭṭiyum, kiyilē villi yeṭuttu ampukaḷit terintu piṭittum, poṉṉāṟ ceyta vīrakkaḻal, peyarum poḻu tellām tiruntiya aṭiyilē ēṟṟiḻivu ceytu viḷaṅkum paṭiyākap pōntaṉaṉ. pōntavaṉ, aṇukutaṟkariya valiyaminta pakivarip puṟaṅkaṇṭa vīrar pōlē, cerukkum, ciṉamu muṭiya nāykaḷ, imiyāta kaṇṇiṉavāy, emmi vaḷittuk koṇṭu mēṉmēl neruṅki varutaṟku yāṅkaḷ añci, aṭi taḷarntu, varuttam mikka maṉattuṭaṉ, vēṟu pulattēṟac cellā niṟpataṉik kaṇṇuṟṟāṉāy, māṟāya viṭikaḷiyellām poru tōṭṭiyatum, vēṟu pulattirukkum putiya āviṉik kāṇaluṟṟatumākiya ēṟu pōla aḻaku peṟat tōṉṟi, yāṅkaḷ añcuvataṟkut tāṉ añci, meṉmiyum, iṉimiyumuṭiya coṟkaḷi emakkup poruntu māṟu colli, em kūntaliyum, aḻakiyum pukaḻntu, ‘oḷḷiya toṭiyum, melliya cāyalum, aḻakiya untiyum, matariya kaṇṇum uṭiya iḷiyīrē! eṉṉit tappi, ivviṭattup pōnta yāṉi mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭīrō?’ eṉa viṉāviṉāṉ. ataṟku yāṅkaḷ maṟumoḻi tantilē mākiyāl, avvāṇṭaki neñcaḻintu, ‘nīvir avaṟṟik kāṭṭit tārīrāyiṉum, emmuṭaṉ oru vārtti collutalum numakkup paḻiyāmō?’ eṉac colli, vaṇṭu mūcum pūkkaḷiyuṭiya taḻiparanta kompi muṟittup parikkōlik kikaṭanta matayāṉipōla ōcci, kalleṉṉum ōcipaṭak kattum vēṭṭi nāykaḷiṉ kaṭuṅ kurali māṟṟi, yāṅkaḷ vārtti colluvatōr kālattip pārttu niṉṟaṉaṉ. appoḻutu, vēṟu puṉattilē kāvalāḷarkaḷ kaḷḷuṇṭu kāvaṉ maṭintirunta vaḷavil, ōr vēḻamāṉatu puṉattiṉit tiṉṟu nokki viṭukiyiṉāl, avarkaḷ varuttam mikkut tamatu pāmpupōlum villi nāṇēṟṟi, ampiṉi yeytu, kāṭellām kalleṉṉum ōci piṟakka, vāyi maṭittu vīḷiyōci yeḻuppi, ataṉip puṉattiṉiṉṟum ōṭṭa, akkaḷiṟu matac cerukkiṉi yuṭittāki, urumēṟu pōla muḻaṅki, maraṅkaḷi muṟittu, kiyic curuṭṭi nilattē yeṟintu, kūṟṟuvaṉippōla eṅkaḷ mītu kaṭuka vantatu. yāṅkaḷ, uyirkoṇṭu piḻippatōriṭam aṅku vēṟuṇarēmāki, ulivuṟṟa maṉattuṭaṉ, uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇiṉip pātukāttali maṟantu, vaḷi kaḷolikkumpaṭi virintu ceṉṟu, avaṉic cērntu, teyvamēṟiṉa mayilpōla naṭuṅkā niṟka, avaṉ kaṭuvicip pakaḻiyi nirampa valittu, avviyāṉiyiṉ mukattē  yeytami yāl, ampu paṭṭuruviṉa puṇṇi ṉiṉṟum kurutiyoḻukā niṟka, atu, taṉṉi maṟantu, āṇṭu niṟka lāṟṟātu puṟaṅkoṭut tōṭiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ, yāṅkaḷ, murukavēḷukku vari makaḷir kikōt tāṭuvatu pōṉṟu, kaṭampiṉ ariyiṉi neruṅkac cūḻnta mālipōl, kikōttuk koṇṭu, yāṟṟiṉ perukkilē kutittu viḷiyāṭa luṟṟu, iṭikariyiṉiṉṟa vāḻipōlē, kāl taḷarntu, nīriṟ cella luṟṟēmāka, atu kaṇṭu, virintu vanteṭuttu, aḻakiya mali poruntiṉa nāṭṭiyuṭiyaṉāṉa avvāṇṭaki, ‘yāṉ niṉatu aḻakiya nalatti nukarvēṉ; niṉṉi nīṅkuvēṉeṉac ciṟitum añcātē.’ eṉṟu colli, oḷi poruntiya nutaliṉit tuṭittu,  neṭunāḷ ikkaḷa voḻukkam nikaḻa vēṇṭumeṉa niṉittu, eṉ mukatti nōkkic cirittāṉ. appoḻutu, taṉakku iyalpākiya nāṇamum, accamum vantu tōṉṟukiyāl ivaḷ taṉṉiṭattu niṉṟu virintu nīṅkavum viṭāṉāy, aṅṅaṉam niṉṟa niliyilē kiyālaṇittu, ivaḷ mārpu taṉ mārpilē yoṭuṅkumpaṭi taḻuvā niṉṟu, piṉ, ivaḷuḷḷak karuttu mēl varintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutalā yirukkumeṉa uṭkoṇṭu ivaḷi nōkki, ‘āruyirppāvāy! palarum vantuṇṇumāṟu katavu akalat tiṟantu kiṭakkum vāyiliyuṭiyatāy, miṭāc cōṟṟi varuvārk kellām variyāma liṭukiṉṟa, viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉāṟ pōlum celvatti yuṭiya maṉiyakam polivu peṟumpaṭi, pinniṇa moḻukiya neymmikka aṭicili nī iṭā niṟka, kuṟṟamillāta uyarkuṭip piṟantōr tamatu cuṟṟattōṭē viruntuṇṭu eñciyuḷḷa miccili nīyum yāṉum uṇṇutal mēṉmiyuṭittu.’ eṉṟu colli, illaṟam taṅkaḷik kariyēṟṟuvatākat teḷivittu, mali yucciyiluṟiyum murukavēḷi vāḻtti, vaṇaṅki, avaṉ muṉpu vañciṉaṅ kūṟi, aḻakiya iṉiya aruvi nīrik kuṭittaṉaṉ. ataṉāl ivaḷ neñcu poruntiyirukka, kaḷiṟṟiṉāl tarappaṭṭa kūṭṭattit tēvarkaḷum virumpum pūkkaḷ niṟinta cōliyil aṉṟip pakaṟ poḻutellām pōkki, ñāyiṟu maliyiṉkaṇ maṟiya, mālippoḻutu varutal kaṇṭu, ‘ilaṅkiḻiyīr! nummuṭiya cuṟṟattār numatu nēriḻi muṉki paṟṟi emakkut tara, nāṭaṟiyum naṉ maṇattiṉip piṉpu nikaḻttuvēm. yām ikkaḷa voḻukkattāṟ peṟum pēriṉpam nukartaṟkuc ciṟitu nāḷ iṅṅaṉam oḻukuvatu paṟṟi neñcu kalaṅkātiruppīrāka’ eṉṟu aruḷuṭittākiya naṉ moḻiyi ivaḷ varuttan tīrumpaṭi colli, āvip puṇarnta ēṟu pōlē viṭāmal emmuṭaṉ kūṭa vantu, muḻavōci yaṟāta nam ūr vācalil, palarum nīruṇṇun tuṟiyil emmi niṟutti mīṇṭu pōṉāṉ. atu mutalāka, aṉṟupōlum viruppattōṭē, ennāḷum iravukkuṟiyil varutalit taṉakkiyalpāka vuṭiyaṉ. aṅṅaṉam varum pōtellām, ūr kāvalar kaṭukik kāttā rāyiṉum, ciṉattiṉi yuṭiya nāy kuritta tāyiṉum, nī tuyiluṇarntāyāyiṉum, nilavu veḷiyāka eṟitta tāyiṉum, mūṅkili yotta meṉṟōḷiṟ peṟum iṉṟuyil peṟātu pōvaṉ. aṅṅaṉam vāḷā peyariṉum ataṟku veṟuttalic ceyyāṉ. avaṉṟāṉ iḷamip paruvattik kaṭantatumilaṉ. celvac cerukkāl ennāḷum taṉ kulattiṟkuriya naṟkuṇaṅ kaḷiṉ nīṅkiyatu milaṉ. alar mutaliyavaṟṟāl accatti yuṭiya ivvūriṉkaṇ, taṉakkup poyyā yirukkiṉṟa iravuk kuṟiyil varuntaṉmiyi, iḵtoḻukka maṉṟeṉṟu niṉittu, avaṉ varintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutalē nalloḻukka meṉṟu tuṇikiyiṉālē, ivaḷuṭiya periya matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ, peruntuḷi yeṟintu maḻi peyyappaṭṭa malar pōl aḻaku keṭṭu, imi cōrntu, īrattiṉiyuṭiyavāyk kalaṅkā niṟkum. ataṟkumēlē, ivaḷ, avar varukiṉṟa maliyiṭam puliyum, yāḷiyum mutaliya iṭumpi ceyvaṉavuṭiya vāyirukku meṉṟu niṉikkun tōṟum, valiyilakappaṭṭa mayil pōlē taṉatu nalam pōmpaṭi melintu, kaṇṇil arittu vīḻkiṉṟa nīr nāḷ tōṟum mārpilē tuḷippak kalaṅkā niṟkum. itu kāṇ nalviṉi nikaḻnta vaṇṇam, eṉṟāḷ”.
tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkuṅ kālattut talivaṉi eḷiyaṉākak kūṟutalum, uyarttuk kūṟutalum, avaṉatu vēṭki mikutturittalum, avaṉ tammik kippaṟṟiṉamikkuk kāraṇamiṭṭurittalum, talippaṭṭami kūṟutalum, paṭittu moḻiyātu uṇmi kūṟutalum eṉṟu ivvāṟṟāl uṇarttutaṟ kuriyaḷ, eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya ilakkaṇam ippāṭṭil iṭiyiṭi amintiruttal kāṇka.
ivvāṟu kūṟakkēṭṭa ceviliyāṉavaḷ, piṉpu naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṟkum. naṟṟāyum tantikkum, taṉṉiyarkkum aṟattoṭu niṟkum. piṉ, talimakaṉ tamaroṭum pōntu, tali makaḷi varintukoṇṭu, illaṟam nikaḻttā niṟpaṉ eṉka.
iṉi, kaḷavoḻukkam kuṟṟamuṭiya taṉṟō eṉiṉ, itutāṉ, evvāṟṟāṉum oppumiyuṭiyā riruvar māṭṭut teyvattāṉ nikaḻnta tākalāṉum, kantaruvar vaḻakkattōṭu otta tākalāṉum, piṉpu ivviruvarumē nāṭaṟiya naṉ maṇañ ceytu kōṭalāṉum kuṟṟamuṭittākātu. talivaṉukkum talivikkum uḷḷa taliyaṉpu ivvoḻukkattil ciṟantu kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, itu kuṇamuṭiyatu mākiṉṟatu. iṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇam aṉpē yākaliṉ, makkaḷuḷ talivarāyiṉāriṭattu nikaḻum aṉpiṉi viḷakki, ataṉ kāriyamākiya iṉpattik kāṭṭuvatu āṉṟōr karuttām. iṟiyaṉārum mutaṟ cūttirattu, ‘aṉpi ṉintiṇik kaḷaveṉap paṭuvatu’ eṉṟum, iṟutic cūttirattu, ‘kaḷavu kaṟpeṉak kaṇṇiya vīṇṭi yōruḷa nikaḻaṉpi ṉuyarccimēṉa’ eṉṟum kūṟivittut talimakka ḷoḻukkam aṉpiṉic cārntu nikaḻvateṉṟu kāṭṭi yaruḷiṉār. talivaṟkum talivikkum iṭiyē tōṉṟum ivvaṉpē, piṉ ellā vuyirkaḷiṭattum aṉpu ceytaṟkum, ulakelāmāki, vēṟāy, uṭaṉumāyirukkum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṭattu aṉpu ceytaṟkum, vittup pōṉṟuḷḷa teṉpatu uyttuṇar vārkku viḷaṅkum.
iṉi, ‘poyyum vaḻuvum’ mikkuḷḷa ikkālattiṟku ivvoḻukkam poruntiya taṉṟāmeṉiṉum, makkaḷ aṟivu varappeṟṟu, oruvari yoruvar aṟintu, maṉam paṟṟiya piṉṉarē maṇañ ceytal takkateṉavum, ciṟumiyar marappāvikku vatuviyāṟṟutal pōl iḷaṅ kuḻavikaṭku maṇam muṭittal ciṟitum takāteṉavum aṟika.
peṇṭirk kuriya kaṭami palavaṟṟuḷḷum mutalāka niṟpatu, avarkaḷ kaṟpiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvātu tammip pātukāttuk koḷḷutalē. ‘taṟkāttut taṟkoṇṭāṟ pēṇit takicāṉṟa - coṟkāttuc cōrvilāḷ peṇ.’ eṉṟu vaḷḷuvaṉārum ataṉiyē mutalāka vittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. kaṟpeṉṉum oḻukkamē avarkaṭkuc ciṟanta aṇikalaṉāvatu. uyarkuṭip piṟanta peṇṭir tam kaṟpiṉi nilināṭṭutaṟku uyiriṉi yiḻakka vēṇṭu
meṉiṉ, avvuyiriṉiyum ōr turumpeṉak karuti viṭuvar. 
1 uyiriṉuñ ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉuñ
 ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpucciṟan taṉṟeṉat
 tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu.
eṉat toṉṟu toṭṭu varum tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇṭiratu kaṟpiṉ tiṟatti āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ciṟanteṭuttuk kūṟutaluṅ kāṇka. ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ, taliviyāṉavaḷ, iru mutukuravar koṭuppa maṇam nikaḻāviṭiṉum, ūḻāl taṉṉi yuṟṟāṉukkē tāṉ uriya ḷeṉṉum uṟutip pāṭuṭiyaḷāy, itaṉi veḷippaṭuttā  toḻiyiṉ, tāy tantiyarāl taṉ kaṟpiṟku ēlātaṉa karutappaṭṭut taṉakkum, kuṭikkum vaṭuvuṇṭā
meṉṟu añci tōḻiyi nōkki, ‘viliyurnta aṇikalaṉ keṭiṉum mīṇṭum vantu kūṭum; cālpu mutaliya kuṉṟiṉ uṇṭāya paḻiyip pōkkip paḻiya pukaḻi niṟuttal muṉivarkkum aritu eṉpar nūlōr’ eṉavum, ‘emakkuḷ nikaḻnta immaṉṟalic cevilikku aṟivuṟuttalē naṉṟām’ eṉavum, ‘ataṉpiṉ tāy tantiyar nammit talivarkku aḷikka nērntilarāyiṉum, ivvaruttattip poṟuttiruntu uyir nīppiṉ, maṟumiyiṉum ikkūṭṭam namakku iyivatākum’ eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷāka,
‘	muttiṉu maṇiyiṉum poṉṉiṉu mattuṇi
nērvaruṅ kuriya kalaṅkeṭiṟ puṇarum;
cālpum viyappu miyalpuṅ kuṉṟiṉ
mācaṟak kaḻīi vayaṅkupukaḻ niṟuttal
ācaṟu kāṭci yiyarkku mannili
yeḷiya veṉṉār toṉmaruṅ kaṟiñar;
iruvēm āynta maṉṟa lituveṉa
nāmaṟi vuṟāliṟ paḻiyu muṇṭō?
āṟṟiṉ vārā rāyiṉu māṟṟa 
ēṉiyula kattu miyivatāl namakkeṉa’
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa toṭarkaḷ, ettuṇi uyariya karuttit tiṭpamuṟa viḷakki makiḻvuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa pārmiṉ! ‘kalaṅkeṭiṟ puṇarum’ eṉṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāl, makkaṭku aḵtu illā toḻiyiṉum, uyirkku aṇiyāya oḻukka muṭiyārē yāvarāṉum pukaḻappaṭum mēṉmiyuṭiya reṉpatum, aḵtuṭittāyiruppiṉum oḻukka milātavar pōkkutaṟkariya paḻiyiṉiyē eytuvareṉpatum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittuṇi aṟivum uṟutiyum vāynta peṇ makkaḷip peṟṟiruttaliṉum oru nāṭu peṟutaṟkuriya pēṟu yātuḷatu?
iṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ,
‘	kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭaṉem viḻitaru peruviṟal
uḷḷat taṉmi yuḷḷiṉaṉ koṇṭu
cāṟayarn taṉṉa miṭāac coṉṟi
varunarkku variyā vaḷanakar poṟpa
malarat tiṟanta vāyil palaruṇap 
pinniṇa moḻukiya neymmali yaṭicil
vaciyil vāṉṟiṇip puriyōr kaṭumpoṭu
viruntuṇ ṭeñciya miccil peruntaki
niṉṉō ṭuṇṭalum puriva teṉṟāṅku
aṟampuṇi yākat tēṟṟi.’
eṉa illaṟa viyalpu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu, taliviyiṉ karuttu varintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutalā yirukkumeṉa uṭkoṇṭa talimakaṉ, avaḷi nōkkik kūṟiṟṟākat tōḻi kūṟiyatākum. itil, talivaṉṟaṉ maṉiyakam, palarum vantu uṇṇumpaṭi katavu akalattiṟantē kiṭakkum vāyiliyuṭiya teṉṟum, miṭāc cōṟṟi varuvārkkellām variyāmaliṭu taliṉ, viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉāṟ pōlum celvatti yuṭiya teṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatu, tamiḻ makkaḷatu aṟattiṉ mēm pāṭṭiṉi mali viḷakkākkukiṉṟatu. ‘paṭippup pala paṭittup palarō ṭuṇṇum, uṭipperuñ celvar’ eṉap puṟattilum, ‘pukaḻniliiya moḻi vaḷara, aṟaniliiya akaṉaṭṭiṟ, cōṟuvākkiya koḻuṅkañci, yāṟu pōlap parantoḻuki’ eṉap paṭṭiṉap pāliyilum uṇavaḷittaliṉ ciṟappuk kūṟiyiruttaluṅ kāṇka. ivaṟṟāl, palaruṭaṉāka uṇṇutalē celvattup payaṉāmeṉpatum, itu peṟṟārē celvarāmeṉpatum peṟappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu. ‘malarat tiṟanta vāyil’ eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉik kamparum, ‘aṭiyā neṭuṅkatavu’ eṉṟu kūṟuvar. piṟarum piṟavāṟu kūṟuvar. ittakiya maṉiyiṉ kaṇ, paciya niṇamoḻukiṉa ney mikka aṭicili nī iṭukiyiṉālē, kuṟṟamillāta uyarkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta periyōr tamatu cuṟṟattōṭē viruntuṇṭu eñciyuḷḷa miccili niṉṉōṭu uṇṇutal mēṉmiyuṭittu’ eṉṟum, ‘iv villaṟamē nammik kariyēṟṟuvatākum’ eṉṟum talivaṉ talivikkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. eṉavē, varintukoṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttuvēm eṉṟāṉāyiṟṟu. illiliruntu ceyyappaṭum aṟam palavaṟṟuḷḷum viruntōmpal ciṟappuṭittu. vaḷḷuvaṉārum viruntōmpaliyē ciṟanteṭuttu mutaṟkaṭ kūṟuvārāyiṉar. piṟa aṟam puriyiṉum, viruntōmpal ilvaḻi, illaṟam illi yāyiṟṟeṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivvaṟam tāṉ otta aṉpuṭiya kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum kūṭiyiruntallatu ceyyalākātu. ‘tollōr ciṟappiṉ viruntetir kōṭalum iḻanta eṉṉi’ eṉṟu kaṇṇaki kūṟuvatāka iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, ‘viruntu kaṇṭapō teṉṉuṟumō veṉa vetumpum’ eṉṟu cīti aḻuṅkuvatākak kamparum kūṟiyirukkum nuṭpam uḷaṅkoḷaṟ pālatu. ippāṭṭil, ‘pinniṇamoḻukiya neymmali yaṭicil eṉṟamiyāl’ viruntiṉark kaḷippatu ciṟanta vuṇavātal vēṇṭu meṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘vaciyil vāṉṟiṇip puriyōr’ eṉṟamiyāl viruntiṉar takuti yaḷavē viruntōmpaliṉ payaṉaḷavā meṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘viruntuṇ ṭeñciya miccil’ eṉṟamiyāl, viruntiṉarkku iṭutaṟ poruṭṭē uṇavu aṭutal vēṇṭumeṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘peruntaki’ eṉṟamiyāl, aṅṅaṉam micciluṇpāṉē periya takutip pāṭuṭiyāṉ eṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘niṉṉōṭuṇṭalum’ eṉṟamiyāl, maṉivi muṉṉiruntu maṉa makiḻn tūṭṭa uṇṇa vēṇṭu meṉpatāyiṟṟu.
iṉittuṇit teṉpatoṉṟilli viruntiṉ
ṟuṇittuṇi vēḷvip payaṉ.
vittu miṭalvēṇṭuṅ kollō viruntōmpi
micciṉ micivāṉ pulam.
eṉṉum maṟi moḻikaḷ iṅku niṉikkaṟ pālaṉa. oruvaṉiyum oruttiyiyum paṭittuk koṇṭu, avarkaḷ vāyilāka ulakattārkku iṉpattiṉ peṟṟiyi aṟivippāṉ pukka āciriyar, avviṉpam nilipeṟutaṟku aṟam paṟṟukkōṭātali yaṟintu, kaṟpoḻukkamum, viruntōmpalum mutaliya illaṟa muṟimikaḷi iṭaṉaṟintu kūṟivittā reṉka.
iṉi, ippāṭṭiyaṟṟiya nallicip pulavarāya kapilar, tām kūṟum ipporuḷiṉ cuviyi mēmpaṭuttuk kāṭṭutaṟkup pala tiṟappaṭṭa vaṉappuṭiya ulakiyaṟ poruṭ kāṭciyil nammik koṇṭu celkiṉṟār. ulakattil aḻaku mikkuṭiya poruḷkaḷik kāṇuṅkāl makkaḷatu aṟivu, avaṟṟil aḻunti niṉṟu, iṉpuṟutal kaṇkūṭām. attakiya poruḷ
kaḷi vevvēṟiṭattut taṉittaṉiyākak kāṇpatiṉum, avi palavaṟṟiyum tokuttu vittuḷḷa kāṭci maṉṟattil orucēra kāṇuṅkāl, avaṟṟiṉ niṟaṅkaḷum, vaṭivukaḷum, kuṇaṅkaḷum, ceyalkaḷum, oṟṟumi, vēṟṟumikaḷum eṇṇiṟanta vakiyāyt tōṉṟa, avikaḷi aṅṅaṉam paṭittaruḷiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉ varampilā āṟṟali niṉintu, viyantu  urukukiṉṟōm. aṅṅaṉam palporuḷum tokuttu vittuḷa kāṭci maṉṟam tāṉum, ekkālattum evviṭattumuḷḷa yāvarkkum orucērap payaṉpaṭuvatiṉṟu. nallicip pulavarāyiṉārē apporuḷkaḷit tam pāṭṭukkaḷil tokuttu vittu, ekkālat tiyāvaruḷḷattum nilavumāṟu ceyya vallunar. iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷum, niliyiyaṟ poruḷum ākiya ulakattup poruḷkaḷ ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum poḻutu, avaṟṟiṉiyaṟki piṟaḻāmal uḷḷavāṟu kūṟappaṭutal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu kūṟumiṭattum, orōvaḻip poruḷ pulappaṭātoḻiyiṉum, cuvi tōṉṟāviṭiṉum avaṟṟōṭu oppumiyuṭiyavaṟṟik kaṇṭu cārttik kūṟutal vēṇṭum. oru poruḷi ataṉ taṉmi piṟaḻāmal kūṟak kēṭkumiṭattum, otta taṉmiyuṭiya vēṟu poruḷōṭu uvamittuk kūṟak kēṭkumiṭattum uṇṭākum makiḻcci peritu. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē taṉmi naviṟciyeṉavum, uvami eṉavum kūṟuvar aṇinūlār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār uvama moṉṟē kūṟi, ataṉkaṭ pala aḻakum aṭaṅka vitta māṭci aḷaviṭṭurikkaṟ pālataṉṟu. piṟkālattār alaṅkārameṉap pala nūlkaḷ eḻuti vittārēṉum, aviyellām tamiḻil illāta aḻakiṉik kūṟuvaṉavumalla; uḷḷa aḻakiṉi eñcātu eṭutturippaṉavu malla; iyaṟkiyiṉ vaḻāmal iyampuvaṉavu malla. ivvuṇmiyiṉi, ‘niraṉiṟuttamitta’ eṉṉum uvamaviyaṟ cūttira vuriyil, ‘iṉi ivvōttiṉiṟ kūṟukiṉṟa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiyum, collatikārat tiṉuḷḷum, ceyyuḷiyaluḷḷum collukiṉṟaṉa. cila poruḷ kaḷiyum vāṅkik koṇṭu maṟṟavi ceyyuṭ kaṇṇē aṇiyāmeṉa ikkālattāciriyar nūl ceytāru muḷar. avi orutaliyākac ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭā. eṉṉi? vallār ceyyiṉ aṇiyākiyum, allār ceyyiṉ aṇiyaṉṟākiyum varum tāṅkāṭṭiya ilakkaṇattiṟ citiyā vaḻiyumeṉpatu’ eṉavum. ‘allatūum poruḷatikārattuṭ poruṭ pakutikaḷellām ceyyuṭku aṇiyākalāṉ aviyellān tokuttu aṇiyeṉak kūṟātu vēṟu cilavaṟṟi varintu aṇiyeṉak kūṟutal payamil kūṟṟā meṉpatu.’ eṉavum pērāciriyar viḷakkiya vāṟṟāṉaṟika. poruḷkaḷik kiṭantavāṟu kūṟiyum, uvamittuk kūṟiyum, meyppāṭum, payaṉumuṇṭākac ceyyuḷiyaṟṟa vēṇṭil, pulava rāyiṉār palvakip poruḷkaḷiyum kaṇṭu kaṇṭu, avaṟṟiṉ taṉmikaḷi nuṉittaṟintu uḷatti lamittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. paltiṇi makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum, uṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum naṉkaṟintārātal vēṇṭum. maṉaviyaṟkit taṉmikaḷi mika aṟintiruntal vēṇṭum. curuṅka urikkiṉ ulakamē avarkkuk kallūriyākavum, ulakiluḷḷa epporuḷum avar kaṟkum pāṭamākavum amintiruttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ulaka viyalpi muṟṟa vuṇarnta pulavarkaḷiyaṟṟum nūlkaḷē, et tēyattiṉum em moḻiyiṉum yāvarāṉum pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. nam pulavar perumāṉākiya kapilar pul, maram mutalākavuḷḷa aṉittuyirkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷiyum, ulakat tōṟṟaṅkaḷiyum ārāyntaṟinta aṟivu mutircci  yuṭiyōrāvar.
ippāṭṭilē ceṅkāntaṭ pū mutal maliyerukkam pū iṟutiyākat toṇṇūṟ ṟoṉpatu vakip pūkkaḷi oru cērak kūṟiyiruppatum, avaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟi, ‘vaḷḷi taḻoṇ ceṅkāntaḷ, eripuri yeṟuḻam, vāṉ pūṅkuṭacam, maṇippūṅ karuviḷi, palliṇark kuravam, palliṇarkkāyā, tēṅkamaḻ pātiri, koṅkumutir naṟuvaḻi, maṇikkulik kaṭkamaḻ neytal, tūṅkiṇark koṉṟi, cuṭarp pūntōṉṟi’ eṉa avvavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu tōṉṟumāṟu ēṟṟa aṭikaḷuṭaṉ kūṟiyiruppatum maram, ceṭi, koṭi vakikaḷil ivvāciriyarkkuḷḷa paranta ārāycciyiyum, ivaratu niṉiviṉ māṭciyiyum naṉku pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṉum ippāṭṭilē ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷik kūṟumiṭattu, nelliṉik koṇṭa neṭiya mūṅkilit tiṉṉaṟku mēlnōkki niṉṟu varuntiṉa yāṉi, avvaruttan tīrumpaṭi taṉatu kiyik kompiṉ mēlēṟṟit toṅka viṭṭuk koṇṭiruttalum; ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir kāyum vemmi mikka poḻutil, vāṉilē paṟakkum paṟavikaḷ tām virumpum cēkkikaḷiṟ cellutalum; tiṉittāḷāl vēynta kuṟuṅ kāṟ kuṭiciyil, maṉivi yeṭuttuk koṭuppa matuviyuṇṭu kaḷittup puṉaṅ kāppōṉ kāvaṟṟoḻili maṟantiruttalum; yāṉi puṉattit tiṉṟu aḻittu viṭukiyik kaṇṭa kāṉavar, ciṉam mikku, villi nāṇēṟṟi ampeytum, taṭṭiyip puṭittum, kāṭellām kalleṉum ōciyuṇṭāka vāyi maṭittu vīḷiyeḻuppi ataṉi yōṭṭutalum; aṅṅaṉam ōṭṭappaṭṭu matac cerukkuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭa vēḻam maraṅkaḷi muṟittu, iṭiyēṟu pōl muḻaṅki, kiyic curuṭṭi nilattē yeṟintu kaṭuki varutalum; talimakaṉ taliviyatu oḷi poruntiya nutalit tuṭittut tōḻiyiṉ mukam nōkkic cirittalum; avaṉ taliviyi varintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutal meyyeṉṟu terivittaṟ poruṭṭu, mali ucciyil uṟikiṉṟa murukak kaṭavuḷi vāḻtti vaṇaṅki, vañciṉaṅ kūṟi, iṉimi mikka aruvi nīr kuṭittalum; piṟavum, iyaṟki vaḻāmal, ōviyam varintu kāṭṭiṉāṟpōla aḻaku peṟak kūṟiyiruppatiliruntu, puḷ, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ ṟaṉmi kaḷiyum, makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum, maṉaviyaṟ kiyiyum evvaḷavu naṉṟāka ivar aṟintuḷḷāreṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
maṟṟum, itil talivaṉ ṟaṉmi kūṟuṅkāl, ‘naṟiya mayirccantaṉam pūci, anta īram pularumpaṭi viralālalittup  piṇippu viṭuttu, akiṟ pukiyūṭṭukiyiṉālē, vaṇṭukaḷ moykkumpaṭi akiliṉ ney kalakkap peṟṟu, nīlamaṇi pōlum niṟattiṉi yuṭittāyk kaṭikuḻaṉṟu vaḷarn tirukkum, eṇṇey pūcappeṟṟa kuṭumiyilē palvaki malar kaḷālum toṭukkappeṟṟa toṭiyaliyum, tāḻi maṭaliṟ kaṭṭiya kaṇṇiyiyum murukaṉeṉṟu accamuṟumpaṭi cūṭi, aḻakiya picci malarāṟ ṟoṭutta oru kāḻākiya māliyic cuṟṟi, acōkiṉatu taḷirit tiraṇṭa tōḷilē vīḻn talikkumpaṭi oru kātilē ceruki, valiyamintu, akaṉṟu, uyarntu, cāntu pūcappeṟṟu, uttama vilakkaṇa mākiya mūṉṟu varikaḷiyuṭittāy viḷaṅkum mārpilē, toṉṟu paṭṭu varum pēraṇikalaṅkaḷōṭē naṟiya mālikaḷi yaṇintu, uṭuttavuṭi tuḷakkamaṟa nuṇṇiya toḻilkaḷiyuṭiya kacciṉi iṟukak kaṭṭi, taṭakkiyil villi yeṭuttu ampu kaḷit terintu piṭittu,poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nalla vīrakkaḻal peyarum poḻutellām tiruntiya aṭiyilē ēṟṟiḻivu ceyyumpaṭiyāka, pakip pulattip pāḻākkum valiyaminta vēliṉiyuṭiya vīrarip pōlē ciṉattāṟ cerukkuṟṟut tammēl oṉṟu neruṅkun tōṟum vekuḷum, mūṅkil muḷipōlum paṟkaḷiyum, periya ukiriyu muṭiya nāykaḷ imiyāta kaṇkaḷi yuṭiyavāy vaḷittuk koṇṭu pōtara, āṉēṟu pōl vantāṉ’ eṉṟum;
mālip poḻutiṉ ṟaṉmi kūṟuṅkāl, ñāyiṟu maliyic cērntu maṟitalāl māṉkaṇam marattaṭikaḷilē tiraḷa, āṉ kūṭṭam kaṉṟukaḷi yaḻikkum kuraliyuṭiyavāy maṉṟukaḷ niṟiyap pukutalic ceyya, vaḷinta vāyiyuṭiya aṉṟil paṉiyiṉ uṇ maṭalilēyiruntu pēṭṭi yaḻikka, aravutāṉ mēytal kāraṇamāka maṇiyiṉi yumiḻa, āyarkaḷ palaviṭaṅkaḷiṉum niṉṟu āmpaleṉṉum paṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kuḻaliṭattu iṉiya iciyi eḻuppa, āmpaliṉ aḻakiya itaḻkaḷ taḷiyaviḻa, antaṇar mālip poḻutiṟ ceyyum kaṭaṉkaḷi nikaḻtta, vaḷamikka maṉikaḷil aḻakiya toṭiyiṉi yuṭiya makaḷir, viḷakkiṉik koḷutti vittut tām ceyyuṅ kaṭaṉkaḷic ceyya, vaṉattiṉ kaṇ vāḻkiṉṟavarkaḷ vāṉi yaḷāviya paraṇiṉmēlē tīkkaṭi kōlāl neruppi yuṇṭākki yerippa, mukil maliyiṭattic cūḻntu kaṟuppa, kāṭṭiluḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷellām kalleṉṉum ōciyuṭi yavāy oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu māṟik kūppiṭa, paṟavikaḷ kuṭam piyiliruntu āravārikka māli tuṉṉiṟṟu’ eṉṟum; avvavaṟṟiṉ ṟaṉmiyi ārāyntu iyaṟki neṟi vaḻuvātu kūṟiyiruppatu mika makiḻcci viḷippatākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ muppattintu uvamikaḷ āṇṭāṇṭuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, karuttoṟṟumi yuḷḷa aintaṉi viṭuttu, ēṉiya muppatu uvamikaḷi īṇṭut tarukiṉṟām.
ceviliyiṉ muṉivukkum taliviyiṉ varuttattiṟkum añcum tōḻiyiṉ accattiṟku, ikalpuriyum iruperu vēntark kiṭi niṉṟu cantu ceyvikkum cāṉṟōratu accamum; tuyyiyuṭiya tali vaḷintu muṟṟiṉa periya tiṉikkatirukku, yāṉiyatu kompiṉmē lēṟṟit toṅka viṭṭirukkum tutikkiyum; kārkālattu iṭiyiṉ muḻakkattiṟku, muraciṉ atircciyum; miṉṉalukku, murukaṉ acurarik kolla eṭutta vēṟpaṭiyum; maliyucciyiṉiṉṟum viḻukiṉṟa aruvikku, viḷaṅkukiṉṟa veṇṭukilum; malip pāṟiyiṟ cuṉiyiṉ nīrukku, paḷiṅkik karittuc corintu vitta nīrum; ciṟiya mutukilē tāḻntu kiṭakkum piṉṉiya kūntalukku, poṉṉilē yaḻuttiṉa nīlamaṇiyum; eṟuḻampūviṉ niṟattiṟku, neruppiṉ niṟamum; cenniṟap pūkkaḷip parappi vittaṟku, arakkiṉi virittu vittalum; acōkiṉatu taḷiriṉ niṟattiṟku, neruppiṉ niṟamum; vēṭṭi nāyiṉ ciṉattiṟku, vēl vīrariṉ ciṉamum; nāyiṉ pallukku, mūṅkil muḷiyum; taliviyik kaṇṭu virumpiyaṭiyum talivaṉukku, vēṟu pulattuḷḷa  putiya āvik kaṇṭu aṇiya varum viṭiyum; immeṉa imirum vaṇṭiṉōcikku, naṭṭarākam muṟṟup peṟṟa pāli yāḻi vācikka vallavaṉ taṉ kiyālē vācitta narampiṉōciyum; tali makaṉ taḻi paranta pūṅkompi muṟittu vēṭṭi nāykaḷi yaṭittalukku, mata yāṉi pūṅkompi muṟittu ōcci niṟṟalum; kuṭiciyiliruntu puṉaṅkāppōṉatu maṉiviyiṉ nōkkiṟku, māṉ piṇiyum; kāṉavaratu villukku, ciṉam mikka pāmpum; matayāṉiyiṉ piḷiṟutalukku, urumēṟṟiṉ muḻakkamum; matakkaḷiṟṟiṉ varukikku, kūṟṟuvaṉatu varukiyum; naṭukkamuṟṟa makaḷirkku, teyvamēṟiṉa mayilum; makaḷir kikōt tiruttaṟku, kaṭampiṉatu aṭimaratti neruṅkac cūḻnta māliyum; makaḷir aruviyilē kāl taḷarntu cellutaṟku, iṭi kariyiṉiṉṟa vāḻi carintu cellutalum; maliccāralil tēṟali ayiṉṟa mayil taḷarvataṟku, āṭu makaḷ kaḻāyk kayiṟṟilē ēṟi āṭukiṉṟa tāḷattiṉāl āṟṟātu taḷarutalum; ceṅkāntaṭ pūkkaḷ parantu kiṭakkiṉṟa nilattiṟku, aḻakiya pala kaccup paranta nilamum; maṉiyiṉkaṇ varuvōrkkellām cōṟiṭutaṟku, viḻāk koṉṭāṭutalum; vaḷinta vāyiyuṭiya aṉṟiliṉōcikku, ūtu kompiṉōciyum; paṟavikaḷiṉoliyōṭu mālip poḻutu tuṉṉutaṟku, ciṉam mikka vēntaṉ amariṉkaṭ cellutalum; taliviyiṉ ṟōḷukku, iḷa mūṅkilum; imi cōrntu aḻaku keṭṭuk kalaṅkum taliviyiṉ kaṇkaḷukku, peruntuḷi yeṟintu maḻi peyyappaṭṭa malarum; iravilē talivaṉ varum vaḻiyiṉ ētam niṉintu kalakkamuṟum talivikku, valiyiṟpaṭṭa maññiyum; uvamiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ciṟappu mutaliyaṉa nilikkaḷamāka, viṉi, payaṉ, mey, uru eṉpaṉa paṟṟit tōṉṟum ivvuvamaṅ kaḷellām, ‘uvamiyum poruḷum ottal vēṇṭum’ eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭavāṟē amintu, pāṭṭiṉi aḻaku ceytu, iṉpam viḷippaṉavātal kāṇka.
iṉi, veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟum iv vuvamam pōlaṉṟi, nuṇṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyntu kāṇattakka uḷḷuṟi yuvamam eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu. ivvuḷḷuṟi yuvamantāṉ paṇṭic cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷil malintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. akap poruṭ ceytikaḷil veḷippaṭak kūṟutal nākarikamaṉṟeṉat tōṉṟumavikaḷi maṟittuk kūṟutaṟ poruṭṭu, iḵtu iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. talivaṉ mutalāyiṉār nāṭu mutaliyavikaḷip puṉinturikku miṭattu, kēṭpōr nuṇṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyntu, ap puṉinturiyi uvami yākak koṇṭu vēṟu poruḷ koḷḷumāṟu kūṟuvatē uḷḷuṟi yuvamam ākum. iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvatāl nākarika muṟi vaḻuvāmiyum, iru poruḷi oru colliṟ kūṟalākiya curuṅkak kūṟutalum, kēṭpōr aṟivu nuṇuki niṉṟuṇarntu iṉpuṟutalum ākiya pala payaṉ viḷikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa māṭcikku iv vuḷḷuṟi vuvamam oṉṟu tāṉē cāṉṟāta lamiyum. ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ, talivaṟkum talivikkum nikaḻnta kūṭṭattic cevilikku aṟivikkum tōḻiyāṉavaḷ, talimakaṉ evvāṟṟāṉum tammiṉ mikkāṉ eṉpatum, avaṉuṟavu tamakku eytutaṟ kariyateṉpatum, attuṇip perumiyuṭiyāṉ tamakku eḷiyaṉāy vantu taṇṇaḷi ceytatu tamatu nalviṉip payaṉē yeṉpatum uṇarttak karutiṉāḷ. ivaṟṟi veḷippaṭak kūṟiṉ ivaḷ taliviyi avamatittā ḷeṉṟum, ivaḷāṉēyē icceyal nikaḻntuḷḷa teṉṟum cevilikkuc ciṉamuṇṭākum. kūṟāviṭiṉ kūṭṭa muṇmi yuṇarnta cevilikkut taṉ makaḷ yārō iḻi takavuṭiyā ṉoruvaṉukku uriya ḷāyiṉaḷēyeṉṟu kavaṟci mikum. in nilimiyil yātu ceyvāḷ tōḻi?  tammi yāṟṟu nīr iḻuttuc cellum poḻutu, kariyēṟṟiṉavaṉ ittaṉmiyāṉa nāṭṭiyuṭiyaṉ eṉṟu nāṭṭiṉic ciṟappittuk kūṟuvāḷāy, talivaṉ perumi mutalāyiṉa uyttuṇarumāṟu uḷḷuṟiyākak kūṟiviṭṭāḷ. ‘paḻutta miḷaku cintik kiṭakkiṉṟa pāṟiyiṭattu, nīṇṭa cuṉiyilē, nekiḻntu utirnta māmpaḻattālum palāppaḻattālum viḷinta tēṟal iṟāliliruntu īkkaḷit taḷḷi yukutta tēṉālē vantu tiraḷa, attēṟalit taṉakku eḷiya nīrākak karuti yuṇṭa mayilāṉatu, viḻāc cey kaḷattil āṭal makaḷ kaḻāyk kaḷiṟṟil ēṟi yāṭukiṉṟa tāḷattiṉāl tāṉ āṟṟātu taḷarumāṟu pōlat taḷaruñ cārali yuṭiyatum, vāṉiyaḷāviya ucciyilē kiḷitta ceṅkāntaḷiṉ pūkkaḷ, variyara makaḷir  viḷiyāṭutaliṟ ṟannalam ciṟitu keṭṭuk kīḻē vantu parantu, nalla pala kaccu viritta kaḷampōla aḻaku peṟṟa iṭattiṉi yuṭiyatumākiya mali poruntiṉa nāṭaṉ,  eṉpatu tōḻi kūṟiyatu. itaṉkaṭ kūṟiya miḷaku, mā, palā, tēṉiṟāl, mayil, ceṅkāntaṭpū eṉṉum kuṟiñcik karup poruḷkaḷāl malivaḷam veḷippaṭi. itaṉi uḷḷuṟi yuvamamākak koḷḷumiṭattu, ‘miḷaku ukka pāṟi annilattu mākkaḷuṟi kiṉṟa ūrākavum, neṭuñcuṉi talivaṉ kuṭiyākavum, māmpaḻattālum palāp paḻattālum viḷinta tēṟal tantiyālum tāyālum uḷaṉākiya talivaṉākavum, iṟāṟṟēṉ ivarik kūṭṭiṉa pālvari teyvamākavum, tēṟaliyuṇṭa mayil uyarnta talivaṉit taṉ kulattiṟku ottāṉākak karuti nukarnta taliviyākavum, attēṟaliṟ piṟanta kaḷippuk kaḷavoḻukkattāṟ piṟanta pēriṉpamākavum, mayil āṭavāṟṟāt taṉmi varuntik kuṟinta taṉmiyākavum’ koḷḷappaṭum. iṉṉum, ‘uyarnta nilattē niṉṟu maṇakkiṉṟa kāntaḷ, variyara makaḷirāṟ kīḻ nilattē parantu, avviṭattik kaccu virittāṟ pōla aḻaku peṟuttu meṉṟataṉāl, nammi luyarcciyiyuṭiya talivaṉ, namatu nal viṉiyāṟ ṟaṉatu perumikaḷ tāṉu moḻintu ivviṭattē vantu kūṭi, namakkum uyarcciyuḷa tākki, nammi aḻaku peṟuttukiṉṟāṉ.’ eṉṟami koḷḷappaṭum. uccimēṟ pulavarkoḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar virittapaṭi iṅku uḷḷuṟi yuvamam kāṭṭappaṭṭateṉka.
iṉi, ippāṭṭiliruntu, vēṟupaṭṭa pala uruviṉiyuṭiya kaṭavuḷari malartūviyum, naṟumpuki yeṭuttum, cantaṉam mutaliyavi koṭuttum vaḻipaṭutalum, muttu, maṇi, poṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytalum, aracar tēr uṭiyarātalum, iraṇṭu vēntar amar puriyum poḻutu aṟivuṭiyōr iṭiniṉṟu cantu ceytalum, peṇ koṭukkum muṉ kuṭiyum, kuṇamum, cuṟṟattutaviyum mutalāya vaṟṟi oppittup pārttu eṇṇittuṇiya vēṇṭutalum, murukak kaṭavuḷ muraciṉiyum, pūṇiṉiyum, eḵkiṉiyum uṭiyarātalum, aruviyiṉṟiri pōlum veṇmiyum, meṉmiyum uṭiya tukilkaḷ neyyappaṭutalum, vīrarāyiṉār vil, ampu, vēl eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ pōr puritalum, naṭṭarākam mutaliya paṇkaḷi yāḻilē vācittalum, ūrkaḷil tiruviḻā naṭakkumiṭattu viṟaliyar kaḻāyk kūttu āṭutalum, cōṟṟu viḻāk koṇṭāṭappaṭutalum, kaṭavuḷic cāṉṟu vittu vañciṉaṅ kūṟutalum, cūḷuṟutaṟku aṭiyāḷamākat taṇṇīr kuṭittalum, mālip poḻutilē makaḷir viḷakkēṟṟi vittu illuṟi teyvatti vaṇaṅkutalum, antaṇar canti ceytalum, kōvalar kuḻal vācittalum, cuṟṟattār peṇ tara nāṭaṟiya naṉmaṇañ ceytalum, piṟavum akkālac  ceyti kaḷāka aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
iṉṉum, ippāṭṭil, teyvap pakutiyākak kaṭavuḷar, tēvar, variyara makaḷir eṉpōrum; makkaṭ pakutiyāka muṉivar, antaṇar, cāṉṟōr, aracar, kāṉavar, kōvalar, ūrkāvalar, viṟaliyar eṉpōrum; vilaṅkiṉ pakutiyil yāṉi, puli, nāy, āṉ, viṭi, piṇi, māṉ, yāḷi, karaṭi, āmāṉēṟu, mutali eṉpaṉavum; puḷḷiṉ pakutiyil kiḷi, mayil, aṉṟil, vaṇṭu, curumpu eṉpaṉavum; paṟavit tokutiyum; ōraṟiviṉavākiya maram mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pakutiyil oruvaḻit tokuttuk kūṟap paṭṭa toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatuṭaṉ, kaṭampu, palā, paṉi, miḷaku, tiṉi eṉpaṉavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṭaṅkal, cūr, picācu, pāntaḷ eṉpaṉavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, vāṉ eṉpaṉavum; mali, kaṭal, mukil, miṉ, iṭi eṉpaṉavum; ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, ñāyiṟṟiṉ maṇṭilam,ñāyiṟṟiṉ ēḻ pari, ñāyiṟu maṟiyum mali eṉpaṉavum;muttu , maṇi, poṉ, pāmpiṉ maṇi, paḷiṅku, arakku eṉpaṉavum; puviyiṉ vēṟāya ulakamum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷiṉ uṭi, aṇi, uṇavu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ, ippāṭṭiṉāl aṟiyap paṭuvaṉa muṉ eḻutiyavaṟṟuṭ kāṇka.
kiḷiyōṭṭuṅkaruvikaḷ taḻal, taṭṭi, kuḷir mutaliyaṉa veṉṟum, mutali, iṭaṅkar, karām eṉpaṉa iṉa vēṟupā ṭeṉṟum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
‘iruvēm āynta maṉṟal’ eṉpataṟku, ‘talivaṉum, yāṉum, perumiyum, uraṉum, accamum, nāṇum nuṇukiya niliyāṟ piṟanta kantaruva  maṇam’ eṉṟum, ‘akaliru vāṉam’ eṉpataṟku ‘ēṉip pūtaṅkaḷ viritaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa kariya ākāyam’ eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyirukkum poruḷ niṉikkattakkaṉa. eḷitiṟ poruḷpaṭum toṭarkaḷukku iṅṅaṉam orō vaḻi ariya poruḷ kūṟuvatu ivarkkiyalpu. ivar coṟkaḷi māṭṭēṟ ṟākak koṇṭu poruḷ ceytirukku miṭaṅkaḷil, kiṭantavāṟē vittup poruḷ ceytaṟ kēṟṟavikaḷi ārāyntaṟika.
ippāṭṭil ēṟakkuṟiya āyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatu coṟkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ amayam, pāyam, cēmam, piracam, camam eṉpaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ. muracu, anti, muttu, maṇi eṉpaṉa iru moḻikkum potuc coṟkaḷ. ñamali, coṉṟi, pāṇi eṉpaṉa ticic coṟkaḷ. ivvāṟṟāl nūṟṟukku oru col viḻukkāṭum itil vēṟṟuc col varavilli yeṉpatu uṇarattakkatu.
iṉi, ippāṭṭil muppattiraṇṭu aḷapeṭic coṟkaḷum, āṟu ‘ceyyu’ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷum vantuḷḷaṉa. ikkālattē cilar aḷapeṭiyi ikaḻntu kūṟuvar. vēṟu cilar evviṭattum aḷapeṭiyāka eḻutuvar. ippāṭṭiṉum, itupōṉṟa ēṉic caṅkac ceyyuḷiṉum, aḷapeṭi vantuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷi aṟivuṭiyōr nuṇukiyuṇariṉ, avvavviṭattu aḷapeṭiyiruttaliṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi pulappaṭum. iṅkē cāṉṟāka oṉṟu kāṭṭutum. ‘tumpi tuḻāay cuṭarppūn tōṉṟi’ eṉpatu ippāṭṭiluḷḷa ōr aṭi. itiluḷḷa aḷapeṭiyi eṭuttuviṭiṉ, ‘tuḻāy’ eṉpatu oru cīrātalilli. itu cīrātaṟ poruṭṭu vēṟu collic cērttiṭil maṟṟoṉṟu virittalum, ōciyiṉmiyum mutaliya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭātal kūṭum. ivvōr aḷapeṭiyāl cīr cemmiyātalum ōciyuṭit tātalum, mōṉi peṟutalum, muṉ piṉ aṭikaṭku ēṟṟavāṟu amitalum ākiya pala nalaṉ viḷintaṉa. cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷiluḷḷa iṉṉōraṉṉa nuṇukkaṅkaḷit tamiḻt tuṟiyil iṟaṅki yaṟiyātār eṅṅaṉam kāṇavallarāvar?
ippāṭṭil, virintuḷḷa vēṟṟumi yurupu aimpatukku mēlilli. avaṟṟuḷḷum pala oppup poruḷil varum iṉṉurupākum. pāṭṭiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām perum pālum iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉku eḻuttukkaḷālāṉaviyē. ivvāṟṟāl iḵtu evvaḷavu coṟ curukkamum, tiṭpamum uṭiyateṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum. ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷi ippoḻutu uṇarttutaṟku mūṉṟu maṭaṅkiṟku mēlāṉa eḻuttukkaḷum, coṟkaḷum vēṇṭiyuḷḷaṉa veṉka.
iṉi, ikkuṟiñcip pāṭṭiṉkaṇ piṟa tiṇikaṭkuriya poḻutum, pū mutaliyaṉavum orōvaḻi vantiruttaṟku viti.
‘	uripporu ḷallaṉa mayaṅkavum peṟumē.
ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum 
annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum
vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum.’
eṉṉum akattiṇi yiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷākum. ‘ennila maruṅkil’ eṉṉum cūttira vurikkaṇ, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, ‘kapilar pāṭiya peruṅ kuṟiñciyil variyiṉṟip pū mayaṅkiyavāṟu kāṇka’ eṉṟār. ataṉāl, peruṅkuṟiñci yeṉavum ippāṭṭukku ōr peyaruṇṭeṉpatu peṟṟām. kuṟiñcit tiṇip pāṭṭukkaḷil ituvē aḷavāṟ peritāyiruttaliṉ, peruṅ kuṟiñci yeṉappaṭṭatu pōlum? maṟṟum ippāṭṭiṉ arumi, perumikaḷi aṟivuṭiyōr ārāyntu makiḻvārāka kapilar pāṭiya ēṉic ceyyuṭkaḷiyum iṅṅaṉam ārāyiṉ. mika viriyumākaliṉ, iṉi avaṟṟiṉ amitikaḷic curuṅkavē kūṟuvāmeṉka.
pūviṉ pakuti
                                        vaḷḷita
ḻoṇceṅ kānta ḷāmpa laṉiccam
taṇkayak kuvaḷi kuṟiñci veṭci
ceṅkoṭu vēri tēmā maṇicciki
uritunā ṟaviḻttot tuntūḻ kūviḷam
eripuri yeṟuḻañ cuḷḷi kūviram
vaṭavaṉam vāki vāṉpūṅ kuṭacam
eruvi ceruviḷi maṇippūṅ karuviḷi
payiṉi vāṉi palliṇark kuravam
pacumpiṭi vakuḷam palliṇark kāyā
virimala rāviri vēral cūral
kurīip pūḷi kuṟunaṟuṅ kaṇṇi
kurukili marutam viripūṅ kōṅkam
pōṅkan tilakan tēṅkamaḻ pātiri
cerunti yatiral peruntaṇ caṇpakam
karanti kuḷavi kaṭikamaḻ kalimāt
tilli pāli kallivar mulli
kulli piṭavañ ciṟumā rōṭam
vāḻi vaḷḷi nīṇaṟu neytal
tāḻi taḷavam muṭṭāṭ ṭāmari
ñāḻal muval naṟuntaṇ kōkuṭi
cēṭal cemmal ciṟuceṅ kurali
kōṭal kiti koṅkumutir naṟuvaḻi
kāñci maṇikkulik kaṭkamaḻ neytal
pāṅkar marāam palpūn taṇakkam
īṅki yilavan tūṅkiṇark koṉṟi
aṭampama rātti neṭuṅkoṭi yavari
pakaṉṟi palācam palpūm piṇṭi
vañci pittikam cintu vāram
tumpi tuḻāay cuṭarppūn tōṉṟi
nanti naṟavam naṟumpuṉ ṉākam
pāram pīram piṅkuruk katti
āraṅ kāḻvi kaṭiyirum puṉṉi
narantam nākam naḷḷiru ṇāṟi
māyiruṅ kuruntum vēṅkiyum piṟavum
arakkuvirit taṉṉa parēram puḻukuṭaṉ
uriyiṟutiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa
iru veṇpākkaḷil oṉṟu
‘	niṉkuṟṟa milli niritoṭiyum paṇpuṭiya 
ḷeṉkuṟṟam yāṉu muṇarkalēṉ - poṉkuṟ
ṟaruvi koḻikku maṇimali nāṭaṉ
ṟeriyuṅkāṟ ṟīya tilaṉ.’
5. kuṟiñcikkali ārāycci
kuṟiñcikkali eṉpatu caṅkattuc cāṉṟōrālē ceyyap paṭṭu akkālattē tokukkappaṭṭaṉavākiya 1eṭṭut tokiyu ḷoṉṟākiya kalittokiyil iraṇṭāvatu pakutiyākum. kalittoki kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷ oṉṟum, pāli muppattintum, kuṟiñci irupattoṉpatum, marutam muppattintum, mulli patiṉēḻum, neytal muppattu mūṉṟum āka nūṟṟimpatu ceyyuṭkaḷi yuṭiyatu. pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, mulli, neytal eṉa immuṟiyē kōkkap paṭṭuḷatu. aintiṇikaḷip palvakiyāṉum muṟippaṭuttal tolkāppiyaṉār karutteṉa uṇarka. pālikkali mutaliya aintu pakutiyum muṟiyē peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ, kapilar, marutaṉiḷanākaṉār, cōḻaṉ nalluttiraṉ, nallantuvaṉār eṉpavarkaḷāṟ pāṭap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟu,
peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ pāli kuṟiñci kapilaṉ
marutaṉiḷa nākaṉ marutam - aruñcōḻaṉ
nallut tiraṉmulli nallan tuvaṉeytal
kalvivalār kaṇṭa kali.
eṉṉum veṇpāvāṟ ṟerikiṉṟatu. ‘purivuṇṭa’ eṉṉum neytaṟ kalic ceyyuḷuriyil, ‘colleccamum kuṟippeccamu mākat tam pēraṟivu tōṉṟa āciriyar nallantuvaṉār ceyyuṭ ceytār’ eṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiṉ uriyil, ‘ātalāṉ īṇṭup pālittiṇiyiyum tiṇiyāka āciriyar nallantuvaṉār kōttā reṉṟu kūṟuka’ eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyiruttalāl, neytaṟkaliyi iyaṟṟiṉārum, pālikkali mutaliyavaṟṟik kōttārum nallantuvaṉā reṉpatu avar karuttātal peṟappaṭum. ittokiyiluḷḷa aintu pakutiyum taṉittaṉi ovvoruvarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, aim pakutiyiyum cērttu ōr toki yākkiṉamiyē tokut tāratu ceyki yātal vēṇṭum. aiṅkuṟuṉūṟum, patiṟṟup pattum tokuttāruṭiya ceykiyum ittuṇiyē yākum. itiluḷḷa ceyyuṭka ḷellām kalippā ākaliṉ, ittoki pāṭṭāṟ ṟokukkap paṭṭateṉka. itu kalittoki yeṉṟum, kali yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
‘	1nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉu mulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkaṅ
kaliyē paripāṭ ṭāyiru pāṅkiṉum
uriyatāku meṉmaṉār pulavar,’
eṉṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutaliṉ, akattiṇikkuc ciṟappurimiyuṭiyatu kalippāvām eṉka. ittokiyiluḷḷa pākkaḷiṉ ōciyiṉpamum, piṟa arumi, perumikaḷum kaṟṟu vallōr yāvarum oruṅkē pārāṭṭun takiyaṉa.
itiluḷḷa kuṟiñcikkali kapilar pāṭiyateṉṟu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. vēṟu yāvartām pāṭiyiruppar? ivvāciriyar kuṟiñciyit tamakkē urimiyākkik koṇṭaṉareṉṉiṉ, atu puṉinturi yākātu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷ kaliyiṉ vakiyākiya kaliveṇpāvum, ottāḻicik kaliyum, koccakak kaliyum mutaliyavāka uḷḷaṉa. ippākkaḷ kūṟum poruḷum pala tuṟippaṭṭaṉa vākum. ivi aṟitaṟku ariyavātal paṟṟiyē ittokikku urikaṇṭa āciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar iṉṉa iṉṉa uṟuppukkaḷ koṇṭu ivvāṟu muṭitaliṉ, iḵtu iṉṉa pāvāku meṉṟum, iḵtu iṉṉa tuṟiyiyuṭiya teṉṟum ovvoru pāṭṭilum viḷakkic cellukiṉṟār.
itiluḷḷa irupattoṉpatu pāṭṭukkaḷilum, taṉic cīr kaḷoḻiya eḻunūṟṟimpatu aṭikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil nūṟṟirupattu nāṉku uvamikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi yaṉṟi, uḷḷuṟi yuvamaṅkaḷum, uruvakaṅkaḷum palavuḷḷaṉa. maṉōtattuva iyaṟki vaḻāmal iṉpattiṉ kāraṇamāya oḻukkattiṉik kūṟappukunta ippāṭṭukkaḷil, aviyēyaṉṟi, ettaṉiyō pala nītikaḷum, iyaṟki yuṇmikaḷum potintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. iviyiṟṟi yuṇarvārkku akkālattil tamiḻ evvaḷavu uyariya niliyil irunta teṉpatum, tamiḻarāyiṉār evvaḷavu mēmpāṭuṟ ṟiruntaṉa reṉpatum aṅki nelli yeṉa viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
iṉi, itiluḷḷa uvamikaḷil iṉṟiyamiyātavaṟṟi urinaṭip paṭuttu iṅku eḻutukiṉṟām. pūtta vēṅki marattip puliyeṉṟu karutik kaṟuvukoṇṭu ataṉaṭiyiṟ kuttiya matayāṉi, taṉ kompiṉip paṟikkalāṟṟātu kūvik koṇṭu varuntuvatu, iṟivaṉ iṟiviyuṭaṉ vīṟṟirunta kayili maliyi yeṭukkaluṟṟa arakkar kōmā ṉākiya irāvaṇaṉ, ammaliyiṉ kīḻk kiṭantu varuntuvatu pōlum eṉavum; talivaṉatu kūṭṭam peṟātu talivi polivaḻin tiruppatu, nīraṟṟa nilattiṟ payirum, poruḷillāṉ iḷamiyum, aṟaṉiṉṟā mūp peytiṉāṉ maṟumiyum poliviḻantiruttal pōlum eṉavum; talivaṉ kūṭṭattāl talivi  aḻaku peṟṟiruppatu, maḻiyiṉip peṟṟa nilattiṟ payirum, aruḷuṭiyā ṉiṭattu uṇṭākiya ākkamum, ceyyuṅ kūṟupāṭaṟintavaṉ celvamum aḻaku peṟṟiruttal pōlum eṉavum; tiṉippuṉaṅ kāppār paraṇilē yeritta akiṟ pukiyāl oḷi maḻuṅkiya matiyam malittaliyil porunti yiruppatu, ammaliyil tēṉiṉ iṟāl iruppatu pōlum eṉavum; tiṉikkatir muṟṟit tāḻntiruppatu. naṟkuṇamuṭiya makaḷir nāṇattāl iṟiñciyiruppatu pōlum eṉavum; maliyiṭattil kāntaḷiṉ kottu uyarntu acivatu, koṭicciyar murukavēḷik ki kuvittut toḻuvatu pōlum eṉavum; talimakaṉ mātarār nalatti nukarntu avarit tuṟappatu, vaṇṭu tātiṉiyuṇṭu pūvit tuṟappatu pōlum eṉavum; talimakaṉ vāymiyil poy tōṉṟumāyiṉ, atu tiṅkaḷiṭattē nerupput tōṉṟiyatu pōlum eṉavum; avaṉatu aruḷilē koṭumi tōṉṟumāyiṉ, atu niḻaṟkuḷattu nīril niṉṟa kuvaḷi ventatu pōlum eṉavum; avaṉatu toṭarpil naṭpiṉmi tōṉṟumāyiṉ atu ñāyiṟṟiluḷḷē iruḷ tōṉṟiyatu pōlum eṉavum; talivaṉatu aṟattiyē virumpiṉa kōṭutalillāta neñcam, oru kūṟṟiṟ cāyāta tulākkōl pōlum eṉavum; kōṅku pūtta pakka malikaḷ poṉṉaṇi yaṇinta yāṉikaḷ pōlum eṉavum; kāntaḷ mukimēṟ poruntiyirukkum tumpi, mātarār kiyilaṇinta nīlak kaṭic ceṟi pōlum eṉavum; mūṅkil muḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip potintiruntu kaḻaṉṟu viḻum pāḷi, nuṇ poṟiyi yuṭiya māṉiṉ ceviyip pōlum eṉavum; vāḻik kuliyil tūṅkum vaḷinta kāy, puliyiṉ aṭiyip pōlum eṉavum uvamikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
etiretir ōṅkiya maliyiṉ cāralil iḷañāyiṟṟiṉ katiraḻakik koṇṭa māṇikkap pāṟiyil niṟkum vēṅki maram, taṉ aḻakiya kompukaḷiṉ mēlē iru maliyiṉiṉṟum aruvi vīḻap peṟṟiruppatu, centāmari malariṉ akavitaḻil vīṟṟirukkum tirumakaḷ taṉatu tirumuṭiyiṉ mēlē aḻakiya iraṇṭu yāṉikaḷ pūvuṭaṉ kūṭiya nīric coriyumpaṭi yiruppataṉiyum;1 cuṉiyiṭattu vaḷarnta kāntaṭ kuliyāṉatu, nīruṇṇukiṉṟa maṇiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya pāmpiṉiyum; yāṉiyum, puliyum pōr ceyyumiṭattē, vēṅkiyiṉ pūṅkompeṉṟu karutip puliyic cūḻntum, vēṅkic ciṉiyeṉṟu karuti yāṉiyiṉ pukar mukatti yaṇukiyum tirintu koṇṭiruk kiṉṟa vaṇṭu, ciṉamikkup pōrpuriyum iru vēntari naṭpākkum tiṟaṉaṟintu cantu ceyvāriyum; talimakaḷ talivaṉatu aḷiyi nacciyiruppatu, vāṉam pāṭippuḷ vāṉiṉ tuḷiyi nacciyiruppatiyum; talivaṉ kūṟuvataṉiyē talivi kūṟuvatu, cārntār kūṟuvataṉiyē mali etiroliyākak kūṟuvatiyum; taliviyiṉ kivaḷiyukuvatu, talivaṉatu malic cāraliṟ kōṭaṟ pū ukuvatiyum; nīr coriyum kaṇkaḷ, maḻiyēṟṟa nīlattiyum; taliviyiṉ māminiṟam, poṉṉuritta maṇiyiṉ niṟattiyum; villiṉ mēluḷḷa poṟikaḷ, pāmpiṉ poṟiyiyum; talivaṉ taliviyip pārāṭṭuvatu, maliccāralil māṉkuḻavi vaḷarppār ceyaliyum pōlumeṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
puliyāṉatu taṉ ceviyilē maṟintu vantu pāynta viṭattu, ataṉ mārpiṉit taṉ kōṭṭiṉ nutiyāṟ kuttit tiṟantu māṟupāṭu tīrntu niṉṟa yāṉikku, nūṟṟuvar talivaṉākiya turiyōtaṉaṉik kuṟaṅkiṉiṉṟum uyirpōkki niṉṟa vīmacēṉaṉiyum; puliyik kutti māṟupāṭu tīrntu taṉ cuṟṟattiṟku naṭuvē kūṭit tiriyum yāṉikku, mallaratu maṟattiṉik keṭuttaruḷiya tirumāliyum; kāntaḷiṉ cevviya mukikku, paki vēḻattiṉ nutaliṉik kutti viḷaṅkum yāṉiyiṉ kompiyum; talivi kaṇ paṉi kaluḻvataṟku, kayalmīṉ nīrumiḻvatiyum; taliviyatu nutaliṉ vēṟupaṭṭa niṟattiṟku, pīrkkam pūviṉ niṟattiyum; taliviyiṉ melliya viraliyuṭiya kikku, malarnta naṟavam pūviyum; talivaṉ taliviyiṉ kikaḷiyeṭuttut taṉ kaṇkaḷ maṟiyumpaṭi vittu veytāka uyirppataṟku, kollaṉ ulimūkku vevvitāka uyirppatiyum; talivaṉ taliviyiṉ meym muḻutum taṭavi iṉpuṟuttuvataṟku, yāṉi tāṉ virumpiya piṭiyi aruḷ ceyvatiyum; poṉ vakirkaḷi yaṇin tirukkum aimpālākiya kūntalukku, miṉṉalkaḷ iṭiyē piḷantōṭum koṇmūviyum; muttukkaḷ patitta toṭi kaḷukku, vaḷarnta tāmari vaḷiyattiyum; tōḻiyiṭattē taṉkuṟi kūṟutaṟkuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ, piṉpu ataṉik kiviṭṭu, avaḷ mukattip palamuṟiyākap pārttu niṟpataṟku, aṟivuṭiyōr celvam kuṉṟi vaṟumiyuṟṟapoḻutu tam iṭukkaṇ kaḷiyat takka kēḷiriṭañ ceṉṟu, tam kuṟiyi avarkku vāyviṭṭuc collat toṭaṅkip piṉpu colla māṭṭā tiruppataṉiyum; taliviyiṉ mukattic cārntu viḷaṅkum kūntalukku, matiyip poruntiya kariya mukiliyum; piṉṉiya mayiriṉkaṇ nuṇṇiya nūlāṟ kaṭṭiya malarkaḷukku, karum pāmpiṭattē ataṉ kariya niṟattōṭu māṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkum kārttiki yeṉṉum mīṉiyum uvami kūṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
piṉ varum ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷil uḷḷa  uvamikaḷum kāṇka:
‘	nilleṉa niṟuttā ṉiṟuttē vantu 
nutalu mukaṉun tōḷuṅ kaṇṇum 
iyaluñ collu nōkkupu niṉii
aitēyn taṉṟu piṟiyu maṉṟu
mitīrn taṉṟu matiyu maṉṟu
vēyamaṉ ṟaṉṟu maliyu maṉṟu
pūvamaṉ ṟaṉṟu cuṉiyu maṉṟu
mella viyalu mayilu maṉṟu
collat taḷaruṅ kiḷiyu maṉṟu
eṉavāṅku, 
aṉiyaṉa palapā rāṭṭi.’	(kuṟiñci - 16)
                                   ‘akaṉmati 
tīṅkatir viṭṭatu pōla mukaṉamarn
tīṅkē varuvā ḷivaḷyārko lāṅkēyōr
vallavaṉ ṟiiya pālikol nallār
uṟuppelāṅ koṇṭiyaṟṟi yāḷkol 
                                                        veṟuppiṉāl
vēṇṭuruvaṅ koṇṭatōr kūṟṟaṅkol āṇṭār
kaṭitivaḷik kāvā viṭutal.
‘	āytūvi yaṉameṉa vaṇimayiṟ peṭiyeṉat
tūtuṇam puṟaveṉat tutintaniṉ ṉeḻiṉalam
mātarkoṇ māṉōkkiṉ maṭanallāy niṟkaṇṭārp
pētuṟūu meṉpati yaṟitiyō vaṟiyāyō.’
‘	nuṇaṅkamit tiraḷeṉa nuṇṇiḻi yaṇiyeṉa
muḻaṅkunīrp puṇiyeṉa vamintaniṉ ṟaṭameṉṟōḷ
vaṇaṅkiṟi vāleyiṟ ṟannallāy niṟkaṇṭārk
kaṇaṅkāku meṉpati yaṟitiyō vaṟiyāyō.’ 
(kuṟiñci - 20)
vēyeṉat tiraṇṭatōḷ veṟikamaḻ vaṇarimpāl
māṉveṉṟa maṭanōkkiṉ mayiliyaṟ ṟaḷarpolki
āycilam pariyārppa valiroḷi yiḻiyimippak
koṭiyeṉa miṉṉeṉa vaṇaṅkeṉa yātoṉṟun
terikallā viṭiyiṉkaṭ kaṇkavar poruṅkōṭa
vaḷamicā luyarciṟappi ṉuntitol viyaṉakar
iḷamiyā ṉeṟipantō ṭikattantāy kēḷiṉi. 
	(kuṟiñci - 21)
vāruṟu vaṇarimpāl vaṇaṅkiṟi neṭumeṉṟōṭ
pēreḻiṉ malaruṇkaṭ piṇiyeḻiṉ māṉōkkiṟ 
kāretir taḷirmēṉik kaviṉpeṟu cuṭarnutaṟ
kūreyiṟṟu mukiveṇpaṟ koṭipuriyu nucuppiṉāy
(kuṟiñci - 21)
iṉi, itil vantuḷḷa uḷḷuṟi yuvamaṅkaḷ pala. pāṭṭiṉ mutaluṟuppākiya taravukaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa uḷḷuṟi amin
taṉavē. aviyaṉittiyum, kāṭṭaluṟiṉ mika viriyumātaliṉ, cāṉṟāka oṉṟu kāṭṭutum.
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻnta piṉṉar, tōḻiyāṉavaḷ talivaṉi yeyti, ‘nī taliviyi varintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutal vēṇṭum’ eṉpatu tōṉṟak kūṟukiṉṟavaḷ, ‘vaḷi
kiṉṟa mūṅkiliṉ kōli nelluṭaṉē vaḷittut tiṉkiṉṟa peṇ yāṉi, ili potuḷiya vāḻikaḷ ceṟinta iṭattē pukuntu, paḻatti nukarntu, varuṭimāṉiṉ maṟi parantu tiritarum iṭattil, viṭiyaṟ kālattē, tuyil koḷḷum veṟpiṉi
yuṭiyāṉē’ eṉṟu viḷikkiṉṟāḷ. mūṅkil nel viḷin tiruppatum, vāḻi ceṟintiruppatum, yāṉi avaṟṟi yuṇpatum, varuṭi māṉ parantu tirivatum ākiya iviyellām maliyilē kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. iṅṅaṉam maliyiyaṟki kūṟu mukattāl, tōḻi tāṉ karutiya vēṟu poruḷit talivaṉukku uṇarttu kiṉṟāḷ. ‘muṉ tammiṭattut tāḻntu niṉṟa ariya talivaṉit talivi ippoḻutu peṟṟu nukarun taṉmiyum, avaṉ varintu koṇṭuḻi, avaḷ avaṉatu maṉikkaṇ nalivaṟa iruntu iṉpan tuyttu, avaṉ cuṟṟattiṭi illaṟam nikaḻttit taṅkun taṉmiyum’ tōḻi karutiya uḷḷuṟiyākum. ‘mūṅkili micinta piṭi, tammiṭattut tāḻnta talivaṉip peṟṟu nukarnta taliviyākavum, piṉṉar atu vāḻic ceṟivilē pukuntu paḻatti nukarnta taṉmi, avaṉ varintu koṇṭuḻi, avaḷ maṉikkaṇ nalivaṟa iruntu avaṉi nukarntatākavum, puli mutaliyaṉaviṉṟi maṟi parantu tiriyumiṭattē tuyilutal, alar kūṟuvāriṉṟi avaṉatu cuṟṟattiṭi illaṟam nikaḻttit taṅkun taṉmiyākavum uḷḷuṟi yuvamaṅ koḷka.’ iṅṅaṉam aḷaviṉṟik kiṭakkum uḷḷuṟiyum, veḷip paṭiyum āṉa uvamikaḷiṉ peṟṟiyi ārāyntu kāṇuṅkāl, ak kālattup pulavarāyiṉār evvaḷavu nuṇṇaṟivu vaḷamum, cennā vaṉmiyum paṭitta mētakkō reṉpatum, tamiḻ moḻi ettuṇic cevviyum, cuviyum, cīrmiyum, kūrmiyum poruntiyirunta teṉpatum yāvarkkum pulaṉām. 
iṉi, itil nāṭakattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta iṉpac cuviyum  nakic cuviyum tōṟṟuvikkum toṭarkaḷ āṇṭāṇṭuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi urippaṭuttuk kāṭṭutum.
kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭattiṉ piṉpu, talivaṉ maṇam pēciyataṟkut tamar oruvāṟṟāl uṭaṉpaṭṭamiyit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟit tāṉum avaḷum varivu kaṭitiṉ muṭitaṟ poruṭṭu variyuṟi teyvattiṟkuk kuraviyāṭuṅkāl pāṭiya ‘koṇṭu nili’kaḷil, tōḻiyāṉavaḷ taliviyi nōkki, ‘mali nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉum nīyum kaliyāṇa kālattē paṇṭu oruvari yoruvar kaṇṭaṟiyātīr pōla naṭappīrkaḷō! yāṉum uṅkaḷ paḻaṅ kēṇmiyik kaṇṭaṟiyātēṉ pōl maṟippēṉmaṉ!’ eṉṟu kūṟiyavaḷ, piṉpu, ‘talivaṉ maṇak kōlattik kāṇāmal kiyāl maṟikkappaṭum kaṇkaḷum kaṇkaḷeṉṟu kūṟappaṭumō?’ eṉṟāḷ; atu kēṭṭa talivi ‘ēṭi! yāṉ niṉṉuṭiya kaṇṇālē talivaṉ kōlatti mikavum kāṇpēṉaṉṟō?’ eṉṟāḷ. ataṟkut tōḻi, ‘appaṭi yāyiṉ, neyta litaḻpōlum niṉkaṇ tāṉē eṉkaṇ āvatāka!’ eṉṟu kūṟi makiḻntaṉaḷ; eṉṉum karuttukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa (kuṟiñci - 3).
taliviyāṉavaḷ talivaṉuṭiya kāṭci viruppum, āṟṟāmiyum, taṉatu vēṭkiyum pulaṉāmāṟu tōḻikkuk kūṟiṟṟākavuḷḷa ōr pāṭṭiṉ poḻippu: “oṇṭoṭīi, ītoṉṟik kēṭpāyāka; teruviṉkaṇ nām viḷiyāṭum maṇalāṟ ceyta ciṟṟilik kālāl aḻittum, nam kūntaliṉ māliyik kiyāl aṟuttum, viḷiyāṭum pantiṉi yeṭuttuk koṇṭu ōṭiyum, nām nōvat takka toḻilkaḷic ceyyum ciṟu paṭṭiyāṉavaṉ, muṉṉoru nāḷ aṉṉiyum yāṉum oruṅkirunta vaḷavilē vantu, ‘illil uṟivōrkaḷē! nīr vēṭki yuṭiyēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ; kūṟa, aṉṉiyum, ‘avaṉukku uṇṇu nīrip poṉ vaḷḷattāl ūṭṭi vā’ eṉṟu coṉṉamiyiṉ, yāṉum avaṉuṭiya paṭṭimiyi aṟiyātu ceṉṟēṉ; ceṉṟa piṉ, avaṉ eṉṉuṭiya vaḷiyaṇinta muṉ kiyip paṟṟi, eṉṉi nalintamiyāl, yāṉ alamantu, ‘tāyē! ivaṉoruvaṉ ceytataṉip pārāy’ eṉṟu kūviṉēṉ; uṭaṉē tāy alaṟik koṇṭu ōṭivara, yāṉ avaṉ ceytataṉi maṟittu, ‘uṇṇu nīr vikki varuntiṉāṉ kāṇ’ eṉṟu oru poy kūṟiṉēṉ; tāyum avaṉatu mutukip palakāl aḻittaḻittut taṭava, av vañcakaṉṟāṉ, eṉṉit taṉ kaṭikkaṇṇālē kolvāṉ pōṟ pārttu, nakik kūṭṭam ceytu ceṉṟāṉ; itu kāṇ nikaḻntatu eṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ” eṉpatu. (kuṟiñci - 15).
talivaṉ tam vēṭki mikutiyāl nikaḻttiyaṉa ivi yeṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟuvāḷāy, “tōḻi! avaṉṟāṉ, ‘poṟpūvāṟ ceyta kōtiyum, toṭi ceṟinta muṉkiyum, aṇipōlum meṉṟōḷum uṭiyāy! niṉ aṭiyilē yāṉ uṟitaṟku nī aruḷātiruttal niṉakkup poruntiya toṉṟō?’ eṉṟu kūṟi, kariya kūntaliṉ muṭiyi iṭi viṭātu mikappiṭittu, atil nalamuṟak kaṭṭiṉa oru māliyi viralāl muṟiyākac cuṟṟi, ataṉi mōntu pārttaluñ ceytāṉ; atuvēyaṉṟi, naṟavampū alarntāṟ pōlum eṉ melliya kiyit tāṅkikkoṇṭu, aruḷ poruntiya tam civanta kaṇ maṟiyumpaṭi vittuk kollaṉ ulimūkkup pōla vevvitāka uyirtaluñ ceytāṉ; atuvēyaṉṟi, toyyil eḻutiṉa mārpi iṉiyavākat taṭavi attoyyilik koṇṭu vanta taṭakkiyālē, tāṉ virumpiṉa piṭiyi aruḷ ceyyum matayāṉi pōla meym muḻutiṉiyum taṭavutaluñ ceytāṉ; acceyalāl yāṉ kippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiya varuttattip pōkkiṉēṉ eṉṟāḷ.” (kuṟiñci - 18.)
