talivaṉum, taliviyum uṟaḻntu kūṟum pakuti: “ivaṉoruttaṉ, taṉṉōṭu kūṭuṅ kuṟippiṉṟi niṟpāriyum, tāṉ kūṭuṅ kuṟippuṭiyaṉāyk kiyālē valitiṟ piṭittukkoḷḷum, ātalāl, ēe! nāṇilaṉāyiruntāṉ” eṉat talivi kūṟiṉāḷ; atu kēṭṭa talivaṉ, ‘akkuṟippāl nikaḻvaṉavellām nī aṟivāy; yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. melliya pūṅ kottukkaḷiyuṭiya koṭiyi yoppāy! niṉ mēṉi pullutaṟku iṉitāyiruttalāl niṉṉi yāṉ pulliṉēṉ’ eṉṟāṉ; eṉṟalum talivi, ‘ēṭā! tamakku iṉitāyirukku meṉṟu karutip piṟarkku iṉṉāvaṟṟi valitiṟ ceyvatu iṉpattit tarumō?’ eṉṟāḷ; ataṉik kēṭṭa talivaṉ, ‘cuṭart toṭī! niṉ mutukkuṟiviṉi īṇṭup pōṟṟātu kiviṭu; yāṉ kūṟukiṉṟataṉip pēṇik kēḷ; nīruṇpār cilar, vēṭkiyuṟṟa tamakku aḵtu iṉitāyiṉ uṇpatallatu, annīrkku iṉitāyirukkumeṉṟu uṇparō? uṇṇār kāṇ’ eṉṟāṉ” eṉpatu. (kuṟiñci - 26)
‘tiruntiḻāy kēḷāy’ eṉṉum iṟutippāṭṭil tōḻi puṉinturi vakiyāṟ paṭittu moḻiyun  tiṟaṉum, āṇṭut tōṉṟum cuviyumellām paṭittaṟintiṉpuṟaṟ pālaṉa.
iṉi, taliviyum tōḻiyum vaḷḷippāṭṭilē, nel mutaliya kuṟṟum iyalpu, ‘muṟṟi vaḷinta tiṉik katiri yuruvic cantaṉa marattāṟ ceyta uralilē peytu, yāṉik kompāṟ ceyta ulakkiyāṟ kuṟṟi, talivaṉi viḷittup pāṭuvēm’ (4) eṉṟum, ‘mūṅkiliṉ nellip pāṟiyākiya uralilē peytu, yāṉik kompu ulakkiyākak kuṟṟi, nalla cēmpiṉili cuḷakākap puṭittu, pāṭuvēmāka’ (5) eṉṟum, ‘aivaṉamākiya veṇṇellip pāṟiyākiya uralilē corintu, puliyik koṉṟa yāṉiyiṉ kompālum, cantaṉa marattālum ceyta ulakkikaḷālē kuṟṟi, mali poruntiya nāṭṭi yuṭiyāṉip pāṭuvēm’ (7) eṉṟum palapaṭiyāka aṇinturikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉi, itil, talivaṉ paṇpum, talivi paṇpumākak kūṟiyuḷḷa ciṟcila toṭarkaḷ aṟintu makiḻaṟpālaṉa. talivaṉ, talivi eṉpōr āciriyaṉāṟ paṭittuk koḷḷap peṟṟōrē yeṉiṉum, akkālattu naṉ makkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷē aṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ mēlvittu viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu orutali. 
talivaṉ paṇpu, 
‘	taṉṉuṟṟār iṭukkaṇ ṭavirppāṉ’ 
eṉṟum,
‘	ōrvuṟ ṟorutiṟa molkāta nērkō
laṟampuri neñcat tavaṉ’
eṉṟum,
	                                        ‘ taṉmali
nīriṉuñ cāya luṭiyaṉ nayantōrkkut
tērīyum vaṇki yavaṉ’
eṉṟum,
‘	añcuva tañcā aṟaṉili yallaṉ’
eṉṟum,
                                              ‘pakiyeṉil
kūṟṟam variṉun toliyāṉṟaṉ naṭṭārkkut
tōṟṟali nāṇā tōṉ’
eṉṟum,
‘	māṟukoṇ ṭāṟṟā reṉiṉum piṟarkuṟṟam
kūṟutal tēṟṟātōṉ’
eṉṟum,
                                            ‘iṭumpiyāl
iṉmiyurittāṟ katuniṟikka lāṟṟākkāl
taṉmey tuṟappāṉ!’
eṉṟum,
                                                ‘ulakam
purappāṉ pōlvatōr matukiyu muṭiyaṉ
vallāri vaḻipaṭ ṭoṉṟaṟin tāṉpōl
nallārkaṭ ṭōṉṟu maṭakkamu muṭiyaṉ
illōr puṉkaṇ īkiyiṟ ṟaṇikka
vallāṉ pōlvatōr vaṉmiyu muṭiyaṉ’
eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
   talivi paṇpu,
‘arumaḻi taral vēṇṭil tarukiṟkum perumiyaḷ’
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya, ‘taṉmali nīriṉuñ cāyaluṭiyaṉ nayantōrkkut tērīyum vaṇkiyavaṉ’ eṉṉum toṭarum,
nīyē, vaḷiyiṉ ikaṉmikun tērum kaḷiṟum,
taḷiyiṟ ciṟantaṉi vanta pulavarkku, 
aḷiyōṭu kitūvali.
eṉṉum toṭarum, vēṟucila toṭarkaḷum, kapilar, tam arum peṟal naṇpaṉākiya vēḷpāriyi uḷattiṟ koṇṭu pāṭiyaṉa vākal vēṇṭum. eṉṉi? pāri paṟampiṟ paṉiccuṉit teṇṇīrum, avaṉatu māriyaṉiya vaṇmiyum, tērk koṭiyum vēṟu palliṭattum eṭutturikkappaṭutaliṉ eṉka.
maṟṟu ivar, ‘pūntaṇṭārp pular cāntiṟ ṟeṉṉavaṉuyar kūṭal’ eṉṟum, ‘poḻipeyal vaṇmiyāṉ acōkan taṇkā viṉuḷ’ eṉṟum kūṟuvaṉa, āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ pōlvāṉōr pāṇṭiyaṉ appoḻutu vaḷḷiyōṉāy viḷaṅkiṉaṉ eṉpatu kuṟippaṉavām.
‘	maṉṉā vulakattu maṉṉiyatu purimē’
eṉavum,
‘	īta lirantāṟkoṉ ṟāṟṟātu vāḻtaliṟ
cātaluṅ kūṭumō maṟṟu’
eṉavum pōnta toṭarkaḷum iṅkē aṟitaraṟ pālaṉa.
iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya toṭarkaḷil palavaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷ piṉvarum kuṟaṭpākkaḷilum oruvāṟu amin tiruttal kāṇka.
‘	uṭukki iḻantavaṉ kipōla vāṅkē
yiṭukkaṇ kaḷivatā naṭpu.’
‘	camaṉceytu cīrtūkkuṅ kōlpō lamintorupāṟ
kōṭāmi cāṉṟōrk kaṇi. ’
‘	añcuva tañcāmi pētimi yañcuva
tañca laṟivār toḻil. ’
 ‘	kūṟṟuṭaṉṟu mēlvariṉuṅ kūṭi yetirniṟkum
āṟṟa latuvē paṭi.’
‘	tuṉṉiyār kuṟṟamun tūṟṟu marapiṉār
eṉṉiko lētilār māṭṭu.’
‘	cātali ṉiṉṉāta tilli yiṉitatūum
īta liyiyāk kaṭi. ’
‘	teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyu maḻi.’
iṉi, tamar varivu maṟuttuḻit, tōḻi mutaliyōr aṟattoṭu niṟkuṅkāl, ‘immaliyil vāḻvār, muṉ uyirutavi purinta talivaṟkut taliviyik koṭukka niṉiyātu notumalarkkuk koṭukka niṉintu, aṟam allaṉa virumpi yoḻukutaliṉ, iṉi, ivviṭattu vaḷḷiyum kiḻaṅku viḻā; mali miciyil tēṉum toṭā; kolliyil tiṉikaḷum katirvāṅki yīṉā’ eṉṟum, ‘maliyiṭattuk kuṟavar maṭamakaḷir, tām tam kaṇavarip piḻiyārāy, avarē teyvameṉṟu vaṇaṅki yeḻutalāl, avaruṭiya aiyaṉmār tāmum tām toṭutta ampukaḷ piḻiyārāyiṉār’ eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppaṉa viyakkaṟpālaṉa. vēṭṭamāṭun toḻiluṭiya malivāḻnar makaḷir, ittuṇi uṟutiyāṉa kaṟpuṭiyarāyum, aṟattiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷi aṟintōrāyum iruntāreṉṉil, innāṭṭu makaḷiratu perumiyi yāteṉṟu urikkalākum?
iṉi, itil ciṟumakaḷir tittiṅkaḷil nīrāṭutalum, piṟar maṉiyiṉ kaṇṇē ceṉṟu, pāṭi, aiyamēṟṟu, āṇṭup peṟṟa vaṟṟip piṟarkku aḷittalum, kuḻa makaṉi (pommiyi) vaḷarttu, avaṉukkup peṇ kōṭaṟkuc ciṟucōṟu camittu, āyattiṟkiṭṭu makiḻtalum eṉṉum mūvaki nōṉpukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. 
‘nalkūrntār celvamakaḷ’ eṉṟu talivi arumi pārāṭṭik kūṟappaṭukiṟāḷ. ‘piḷḷiyāl miṭippaṭṭāruṭiya celvattiyuṭiya makaḷ’ eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷām.
‘neṟiyaṟi ceṟikuṟi puritiri paṟiyā vaṟivaṉ’ eṉṟu kaṇi kūṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. ‘nūṉeṟiyālē anta iruvarum kūṭutaṟ kētuvākiya muḻuttam vēṟupaṭṭut tapputali, orukālattum aṟiyāta kaṇi’ (cōtiṭaṉ) eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷām. 
‘pariyatu kūrṅkōṭṭa tāyiṉum yāṉi verūum pulitāk kuṟiṉ’ eṉṟu tamiḻ maṟi kūṟutaṟku vēṟāka, vēḻam puliyiṉip pōr tolitta ceyti palkāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
‘eṉ ceyvām’ eṉṟārkkup ‘poṉceyvām’ eṉpatu ōr nākik kuṟippu moḻiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. 
mey toṭṭuc cūḷuṟutal kūṟiyuḷḷatu.
kayilimaliyilē, iṟivaṉ iṟiviyōṭirukka, irāvaṇaṉ ataṉi yeṭukkaluṟṟatum, yāṉikaḷ pūvōṭu kūṭiya  nīri mēlē coriyā niṟkac centāmari malariṉ akavitaḻil tirumakaḷ vīṟṟiruppatum, nūṟṟuvar talivaṉākiya turiyōtaṉaṉi, vīmaṉ kuṟaṅkilaṭittu uyir cekuttatum, tirumāl mallaratu maṟattik keṭuttatum ākiya purāṇa vitikāca katikaḷum, vāraṇavāci yeṉṉum ūriluḷḷāratu aruḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
itiluḷḷa tiricoṟkaḷik kāṇuṅkāl, ivvāciriyar, coṟkaḷit tam vaḻippaṭuttu āṇṭuḷār eṉpatu pulaṉām. ‘urittikkāṇ, pāṭitti, citittati, kēṭṭī, varuvati, iṟantīvāy, ikattantāy, aṟintī yātu, pōttanta, ikattara, iḵtottaṉ, maṟātīvāḷ, vanti, kaṇṭi, tiṉṟi, koṇṭī, eṉpaṉa kāṇka. ivi muṟiyē urittukkāṇ, pāṭu, citittatu, kēḷ, varuvatu, iṟappāy, ikantāy, aṟiyātu, pōtal tanta, ikakka, ivaṉoruttaṉ, maṟāḷ, vā, kāṇ, tiṉ, koḷ;’ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripākum. 
piṉvarum pāṭṭukkaḷiluḷḷa iyaṟki naviṟciyum, piṟa aḻakum ōrntu koḷka.
koccakavuṟuppu
‘	iṭiyumiḻ piraṅkiya viravuppeya ṉaṭunāṭ
koṭiviṭu piruḷiya miṉṉuccey viḷakkattup 
piṭiyoṭu mēyuñ ceypuṉ yāṉi
aṭiyotuṅ kiyakkaṅ kēṭṭa kāṉavaṉ
neṭuvari yāciṉip paṇavi yēṟik
kaṭuvicik kavaṇiyiṟ kalki viṭutaliṉ
iṟuvari vēṅkiyi ṉoḷvī citaṟi
āciṉi meṉpaḻa maḻintavi yutirāt
tēṉce yiṟāal taḷipaṭap pōki
naṟuvaṭi māviṉ pintuṇa ruḻakkik
kuliyuṭi vāḻik koḻumaṭal kiḻiyāp
palaviṉ paḻattiṟ ṟaṅku malikeḻu veṟpaṉip 
pāṭukam vāvāḻi tōḻinaṟ ṟōḻi pāṭuṟṟu ’	(5)
‘	imayavil vāṅkiya vīrñcaṭi yantaṇaṉ
umiyamarn tuyarmali yiruntaṉa ṉāka
aiyiru taliyi ṉarakkar kōmāṉ
toṭipoli taṭakkiyiṟ kīḻpukut tammali
eṭukkal cellā tuḻuppavaṉ pōla
uṟupuli yuruvēyppap pūtta vēṅkiyik
kaṟuvukoṇ ṭataṉmutaṟ kuttiya matayāṉi
nīṭiru viṭarakañ cilampak kūyttaṉ
kōṭupuyk kallā tuḻakku nāṭa kēḷ!
	āriṭi yeṉṉāynī yaravañcāy vantakkāl
nīraṟṟa pulamēpōṟ pulleṉṟāḷ vikaṟi
kārpeṟṟa pulamēpōṟ kaviṉpeṟu makkaviṉ
tīrāmaṟ kāppatōr tiṟaṉuṇṭē lurittikkāṇ!
	iruḷiṭi yeṉṉāynī yiravañcāy vantakkāṟ
poruḷillā ṉiḷamiyōṟ pulleṉṟāḷ vikaṟi
aruḷvallā ṉākkampō laṇipeṟu mavvaṇi
teruḷāmaṟ kāppatōr tiṟaṉuṇṭē lurittikkāṇ!
	maṟantiruntā reṉṉāynī maliyiṭi vantakkāl
aṟañcārāṉ mūppēpō laḻitakkāḷ vikaṟi
tiṟañcērntā ṉākkampōṟ ṟiruttaku mattirup 
puṟaṅkūṟṟut tīrppatōr poruḷuṇṭē lurittik kāṇ!
	eṉavāṅku,
	niṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅ kūṟak kēṭṭu
varumē tōḻi naṉmali nāṭaṉ 
vēṅki viriviṭa nōkki
vīṅkiṟip paṇittōḷ varintaṉaṉ koḷaṟkē’ 	(2)
6. maṟṟiya akappakutikaḷ
naṟṟiṇi
eṭṭuttokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nāṉūṟṟoru akavaṟpākkaḷiyuṭiyatu; akattiṉ pakutikaḷiyē poruḷāka vuṭiyatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām oṉpataṭic ciṟumiyum, paṉṉiraṇṭaṭip perumiyum uḷḷaṉa. itil irupatu pāṭṭuk kapilar pāṭiyaṉa.
ippākkaḷil, talimakkaḷiṉ kēṇmikku, tāmariyiṉ taṇṇiya tātiṉiyūtic cantaṉamarattil vikkappaṭṭa tēṉum; talivi, talivaṉiyiṉṟi yamiyāmikku, ulakam nīriyiṉṟi yamiyāmiyum; taliviyiṉ cevvari paranta kaṇkaḷukku, ēṉal kāvalar vilaṅkiṉi yeytu paṟitta pakaḻiyum; vēṅkiyiṉ malarutirtaṟku, kollaṉ ulikkaḷattu aṭikkum irumpiṉ poṟi citaṟutalum; malikku, māyōṉum; malippakkattu oḻukum aruvikku, vāliyōṉāṉa palatēvaṉum; kaḷiṟṟi yāṉi kāṇuntōṟum polintu viḷaṅkutaṟku, pukaḻuṇṭāka vāḻvāruṭiya celvam politalum; kaṭuñciṉattuṭaṉ pōrceyta valimikka yāṉikku, murukavēḷum; vāḻi appoḻutu īṉṟa kūriya mukikku, yāṉiyiṉ kuruti paṭinta kompum; vāḻippūvukku, makaḷiratu kūntalum; talivaṉatu veytuyirppukku, paṭukkiyilē kiṭanta yāṉiyiṉ uyirppum; ñeṇṭiṉ kaṇṇukku, nocciyiṉ kariya arumpum; ñeṇṭiṉ kūṭṭam ñāḻaliṉ utirnta malarik kālāl varittuk kōlañ ceytaṟku, makaḷir veyililē kāyun tiṉiyik kiviralālē tuḻavutalum; mīṉit tiṉṉumāṟu vantu kūṭum puṭkaḷiṉ kuralukku, ciṟiya nāviyuṭiya maṇiyiṉ teḷinta ōciyum; nīr vaṟṟiya cēṟṟiluḷḷa koḻuviya mīṉi nukartaṟ poruṭṭu maṇaṉ mēṭṭilē kūṭiyirukkum nāri kaḷukku, aracaratu paṭit tokutiyum; kāṟṟāl alikiṉṟa mukilukku, nūṟkiṉṟa nuṇṇiya pañcum uvamikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
talivi yāṉavaḷ pāriyiṉ paṟampumali pōlap peruṅkavi ṉeytiṉamiyāl, ariya kāvali yuṭiya ḷāyiṉāḷ eṉṟum; taliviyiṉ aḻaku, muḷḷur maṉṉaṉākiya maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri pariyēṟic ceṉṟu; iravilē aṭittuk koṇṭuvanta āṉirikkuriyōr avaṉō ṭetirttup pōr muṉiyil aḻintāṟpōl, aḻintatu eṉṟum; kollimalit talivaṉākiya valvillōriyik koṉṟa tirumuṭikkāriyāṉavaṉ, uṭaṉē avvōriyiṉ  perun teruvilē pukuntatik kaṇṭa ōriyic cārntār yāvarum oru cēra niṉṟu, pēririccaliṭṭāṟ pōla, ivvūrilē kalleṉṉum nakiyoliyuṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟum; kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kiṭaṅkil eṉṟa ūrum ciṟappikkap peṟṟuḷatu.
‘āḷil peṇṭir tāḷiṟ ceyta, nuṇaṅku nuṇpaṉuval’ eṉṉum toṭar niṉivu kūrattakkatu. kaṇavaṉi yiḻanta kimmi makaḷir tam uyirvāḻkkikku vēṇṭum poruḷiṉ poruṭṭu muyaṉṟu nūṟkiṉṟa mika nuṇṇiya pañcu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. puṟappāṭṭilum, ‘paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuval’ eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. akkālattu illiliruntu makaḷirum purintu ciṟappeytiya ittoḻil ippoḻutu nam nāṭṭil arukiṉatu peritum iraṅkattakkatu. maliyiṉiṭattē tuyil kiṉṟa piṭiyiṉ pakkattil mēkam maṟittalāl, āṇyāṉi ataṉik kāṇappeṟātu piḷiṟā niṟkum eṉṟum; malic cāralil uḻutoḻil puriyum kuṟavaratu iḷamakaḷir kariya viraliyuṭiya mantiyi viruntāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭu, kuṭam pōlum palāppaḻattiṉ cuḷiyi ataṟku aḷippar eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppaṉa viyakkaṟ pālaṉa. kāṭṭakattu vēṭṭuvaṉ peṟum uṇavup poruḷkaḷum (59), talivi kiḷiyit tūtu viṭutalum (376) naṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiluḷḷa uḷḷuṟi yuvamaṅkaḷellām viyakkaṟ pālaṉavē.
‘nīriṉṟamiyā vulakam pōla’, ‘periyōr nāṭi naṭpiṉallatu - naṭṭu nāṭārtam moṭṭiyōr tiṟattē’ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷ,
‘	nīriṉ ṟamiyā tulakeṉiṉ yāryārkkum
vāṉiṉ ṟamiyā toḻukku.’
‘	nāṭātu naṭṭaliṟ kēṭilli naṭṭapiṉ
vīṭilli naṭpāḷ pavarkku.’
eṉṉum kuṟaṭpākkaḷiṉ karuttit taḻuviyuḷḷaṉa.
niṉṟa collar nīṭutōṉ ṟiṉiyar
eṉṟu meṉṟōḷ piripaṟi yalarē
tāmarit taṇṭā tūti mīmicic 
cāntiṟ ṟoṭutta tīntēṉ pōlap
puriya maṉṟa puriyōr kēṇmi
nīriṉ ṟamiyā vulakam pōlat
tammiṉ ṟamiyā nannayan taruḷi
naṟunutal pacatta lañcic
ciṟumi yuṟupavō ceypaṟi yalarē.
kuṟuntoki
eṭṭuttokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nānūṟṟiraṇṭu akavaṟpākkaḷiyuṭiyatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām nāṉkaṭic ciṟumiyum, eṭṭaṭip perumiyum uḷḷaṉa. itil kapilar pāṭiyaṉa irupattoṉpatu pāṭṭu.
ivaṟṟil, ciṟumalikku, yāṉiyum; nāriyiṉ ciṟiya paciya kālukku, tiṉiyiṉ tāḷum; maliyiṉiṉṟu iḻiyum aruvikku, iṟṟi marattiṉatu kalliṭattē paraviya veḷḷiya vērum; 1talivaṉ perumūccukku, pātiyiravil tuyilum yāṉiyiṉ uyirppum; tāmari yilikku, kaḷiṟṟiṉ ceviyum uvamiyāka vantuḷḷaṉa. ciṟiya kōṭṭil periya paḻam tūṅkiṉāṟ pōṉṟu, taliviyiṉ uyir mikac ciṟitu, avaḷuṟṟa kāmam mikap peritu eṉṟum; taliviyiṉ nīriyotta cāyal, talivaṉatu tīyiyotta maṉavaliyik keṭuttiṭṭatu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. talivikkuk kolli maliyiluḷḷa pāvi uvamikkap paṭṭuḷatu. miḷakiṉ ṟaḻiyik kuraṅku aruntum eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. maṟṟum, ivvāciriyar, ‘kēṭuṟṟaviṭattuc ceyta utaviyi aracurimic ciṟappeyti maṟanta maṉṉaṉ pōla naṉṟi maṟantaṉiyāyiṉ’ eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟuva
tākavum; ‘talivaṉatu kēṇmiyāl vanta iṉṉāmiyiṉum tuṟakkaviṉpam iṉitāmō’ eṉṟum, ‘aṉṉi ceyta utavikku uyar niliulakamum āṟṟutaṟ karitu’ eṉṟum talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟuvatākavum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ciṟukuṭi pacippiṉ yāṉiyiṉ kompukaḷi viṟṟu uṇpikkum ōri yeṉpāṉatu kollimali eṉkiṉṟār. maliyaṉatu muḷḷūrk kāṉattip palaviṭattuc ciṟappit tōtukiṟār. ivar kūṟiya, ‘kāṉamaññi yaṟiyīṉ muṭṭi, veyilāṭu mucuviṉ kuruḷi yuruṭṭuṅ, kuṉṟanāṭaṉ’ eṉpataṉ karuttu,‘vantāy pavariyillāmayiṉ muṭṭi yiḷiyamanti, pantā ṭirumpoḻiṟ palvari nāṭaṉ’ eṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉāl tiruc ciṟṟampalak kōviyāril eṭuttāḷap peṟṟatu.
aiṅkuṟunūṟu
eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl akattiṉ pakuti
yākiya marutam, neytal, kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli eṉṉum tiṇikaḷ ovvoṉṟaṉiyum paṟṟit taṉittaṉi kūṟum nūṟu nūṟu akavaṟ pākkaḷi yuṭiyatu. ivvintu nūṟṟiyum iyaṟṟiṉār muṟiyē ōrampōkiyār, ammūvaṉār, kapilar, ōtalāntiyār, pēyaṉār eṉpōr.1 maṟṟit tokikaḷil pōlavē, pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉāriyaṟṟiya kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu itil mutaṟkaṇ uḷḷatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām mūṉṟaṭic ciṟṟaḷavum, āṟaṭip pēraḷavum uḷḷaṉa. itaṉ ovvoru nūṟum pappattup pākkaḷ olvoru taṉmiyiṉavāka iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pakutiyil uḷḷa pattukkaḷ, muṟiyē aṉṉāy vāḻip pattu, aṉṉāyp pattu, ammavāḻip pattu, teyyōp pattu, veṟippattu. kuṉṟakkuṟavaṉ pattu, kēḻaṟ pattu, kurakkuppattu, kiḷḷippattu, maññippattu eṉpaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉ ivvoru pāṭṭilum ‘aṉṉāy vāḻi’ eṉpatu mutaliya coṟkaḷēṉum, avaṟṟiṉ pariyāyac coṟkaḷēṉum varutaliṉ, ivi ippeyariṉa vāyiṉa. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām coṟcuvi, poruṭcuvi tuvaṉṟik kaṟpārkkuk kaḻipēriṉimi payappaṉa. iyaṟki vaḷaṅkaḷi yeḻil peṟa viḷakkuvaṉa.
aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉinam paṭappit
tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉu miṉiya vavarnāṭ
ṭuvalik kūvaṟ kīḻa
māṉuṇ ṭeñciya kaliḻi nīrē.
ivvoru pāṭṭiliruntē innūliṉ arumi, perumi kaḷit terintu koḷḷalākum. talivaṉuṭaṉ avaṉūrkkuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭa talimakaḷ, ‘nī ceṉṟa nāṭu evviyalpiṟṟu?’ eṉa viṉāviya tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu itu. itaṉ poruḷ: ‘aṉṉiyē! vāḻvāyāka! yāṉ collu mitaṉi virumpik kēḷ. aṉṉi! nam malip pakkattu iṟāliṉiṉṟum piḻin teṭutta tēṉ kalanta pāliṉuṅ kāṭṭil, nam talivar nāṭṭilē taḻikaḷ viḻuntu aḻukik kiṭakkum ciṟu kuḻikaḷiṉ kīḻiṭattavāy, vilaṅkukaḷāl uṇṇappaṭṭu eñciyuḷḷa kalaṅkaṟ ciṉṉīrkaḷ eṉakku mikka iṉimiyuṭiyaṉa kāṇ.’ eṉṟu talivi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉpatu. talivaratu nāṭu ituvē pōṟ ciṟantatu eṉṟēṉum, itaṉiṉum ciṟantatu eṉṟēṉum talivi kūṟiyiruppiṉ, avaṭkut talivaṉ pāluḷḷa aṉpu oru ciṟitum viḷaṅkuva tiṉṟu. ‘talivaratu nāṭu evviyalpiṟṟu eṉiṉum, atuvē eṉakkuc ciṟantatāvatu’ eṉṟu kūṟuvatē taliviyiṉ aṉpiṉ tiṟaṉip pulappaṭuppatākum. ataṉiyē, ‘avar nāṭṭu uvalik kūvaṟ kīḻa māṉuṇṭu eñciya kaliḻinīr nam paṭappit tēṉ mayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya’ eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāḷ. taḻikaḷ aḻukik kiṭakkum cēṟṟiṭattil, vilaṅkukaḷ uṇṇak kalaṅkiya ciṟiya nīri, iṟappa iḻintārum  ettuṇi nīr vēṭkiyuṟṟa poḻutum uṇṇutaṟku aruvarā niṟpar. uṇṇiṉ, aḵtu appoḻutu iṉimi ciṟitum ilatātalē yaṉṟip piṉpu pala piṇiyum uṇṭākkip peruntuṉpatti viḷippatumākum. tēṉum, pālum taṉittaṉi mikka iṉimi yuṭiyavāy, ettuṇip periyārālum virumpi yuṇṇappaṭuvaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu maṟṟiyatuṭaṉ kalakkappeṟiṉ, akkalavi parukutaṟku ataṉ mēlum iṉimiyuṭittākum. avvaḷavaṉṟi, aḵtu uṭampiṟku uṟuti payantu, piṟ poḻutukaḷil iṉpam viḷippatu mākum. ‘kalaṅkaṟ ciṉṉīr teruḷāmiyāl uṇpāṉ’ eṉṉum iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuriyum, ‘pāloṭu tēṉ kalantaṟṟē’ eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷum iṅku niṉikkaṟpālaṉa.  ivvāṟṟāl, yāvarāṉum veṟukkattakum kalaṅkal nīr, yāvarāṉum virumpat takum tēṉpāṟ kalaviyiṉum talimakaṭku iṉimi yuṭit tāyiṟṟu. itaṟku eṉṉi kāraṇam? aṉpu eṉpatoṉṟu ettuṇi yiḻinta poruḷiyum uyarnta poruḷākka valla maruntākavuḷḷatu.
‘	ceruppaṭiyav viḷamparuvac cēyaṭiyiṟ ciṟappuṭittāl.’ 
‘	eṉakkavaṉṟaṉ vāyumiḻum puṉal puṉitam1.’
eṉṟu civaṉ kūṟiṟṟāka vuḷḷa tiruppāṭṭukkaḷum ivvuṇmi terippaṉa vaṉṟē? ‘aṉpu eṉpa toṉṟiṉ taṉmi amararum aṟintataṉṟu’2 eṉṉil, itaṉ perumiyi yām eṅṅaṉam urikkēm? talivaṉatu nāṭṭiluḷḷa teṉpa toṉṟē paṟṟi, avviṭattuḷḷa mika iḻinta poruḷiyum taliviyāṉavaḷ piṟaviṭattuḷḷa mikavuyarnta poruḷiṉum uyarntatākak koṇṭāḷ eṉṟāl, avaḷ talivaṉiṭattu ettakiya aṉpuṭiyaḷātal vēṇṭum? tēṉ eṉa vāḷā kūṟātu, ‘nam paṭappit tēṉ’ eṉṟatu, atu piṟaviṭattuḷḷatiṉum iṉitu eṉpatu tōṟṟutaṟku. itaṉāl, kaṟpuṭi makaḷirāvār, tam piṟantakattu ettuṇip peruñ celvam niṟintiruppiṉum, ataṉiṉum tām pukkakattu vaṟumiyi miku mēṉmi yuṭit teṉṟu koḷvar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. tōḻiyi aṉṉāy eṉṟatu arumi pārāṭṭi. iru muṟi viḷittatu ārvam paṟṟi. vāḻiyeṉṟatu avaḷ uṭaṉ pōkkiṟkut tuṇi purinta utavi paṟṟi. vēṇṭu eṉṟatu tāṉ collum poruḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi paṟṟi. ivvāṟu ivvāciriyar aṉpiṉatu tiṟatti iraṇṭu mūṉṟaṭikaḷāl viḷaṅka vitta māṭciyi eṅṅaṉam viyanturikka vallēm?
iṉi, kurakkuppattil, kuraṅkiṉ talivaṉāṉa kaṭuvaṉ, ciṟiya pirappaṅkōl koṇṭu, māri mokkuḷip puṭittu aḻikkum eṉṟum, maliyiṭattuḷḷa kaṭuvaṉ naṟikkoṭi koṇṭu karukkoṇṭa iḷamukilip puṭikku meṉṟum, kuraṅku taṉ mītiruntu pāytalāl vaḷintu nimirum mūṅkiṟkōl, mīṉeṟi tūṇṭil pōl tōṉṟum eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉavum; maññip pattil malarnta vēṅki marattilirunta mayil, aṇikalaṉ aṇinta makaḷir pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum, pūkkoyyum makaḷir pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum, tiṉiyari tāḷilirunta kuruvi eḻuvatu viḻuvatāy varuntuṟac ciṟaki virittu āṭic cellum mayil, pantāṭum makaḷir pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉavum paṭittu iṉpuṟaṟ pālaṉa. iṅkuk kūṟiya ‘mīṉeṟi tūṇṭil’ eṉṟa uvamam, ‘kāṉa yāṉi kiviṭu pacuṅkaḻi, mīṉeṟi tūṇṭiliṉi vakkum’ eṉak kuṟuntoki yuḷḷum (54) pōntuḷatu. matavēḻamāṉatu, kariya malippāṟiyip piṭiyeṉṟu karutit taḻuvum nāṭaṉ eṉpatum, veṟippattil, ‘poyyā marapiṉūr mutuvēlaṉ’ eṉavum, ‘poypaṭu paṟiyāk kaḻaṅkē’ eṉavum kuṟippiṟ kūṟi, veṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉatu (pūcāri) poymmiyi viḷakkuvaṉavum pōlvaṉa nakiccuvi payappaṉavākum. ivaḷ tiṉippuṉam kāttoḻintāl varival eṉṟa talivaṟkut tōḻi kūṟuvatāka, ‘koṭicci kiḷiyōṭṭuṅ kuralik kiḷiyiṉ kuraleṉak karutip piṟaviṭattuḷḷa kiḷikaḷum tiṉip puṉattiṟ pōntu aḻikkum eṉṟu kāvali vilakkuvar pōlum. ākalāl, periya mali nāṭaṉē! ivaḷi viriya varintu koḷvāyāka.’ eṉṟu viyapput tōṉṟak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. piṉṉuḷōrum, ‘āyō veṉuṅ kuraliyō puṉatti yaḻikkiṉṟatē’ eṉa ikkarutti eṭuttamittuḷār. talivi piṉṉāka vantu kaṇ pottiya viṭattu, ‘aḻakiya kāntaṭ pūppōlum kiyāl eṉ kaṇkaḷip putittōyē!’ eṉ neñcam virumpiṉavar nīyalatum uḷar kollō?’ eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟiṟṟāka vuḷḷatu iṉpam viḷikkiṉṟatu. kampanāṭarum, ‘kāraṉṉa kūntaṟ kuyilaṉṉavaḷ kaṇputippa, āreṉṉa lōṭu maṉaleṉṉa ayirttuyirttāḷ’ eṉa ikkarutti yeṭuttu 1irāmāyaṇattiṟ patittu vittār.
talivaṉ ūrnta kutiri pārppaṉak kuṟumakavupōl kuṭumit taliyatu eṉavum; acōkiṉ ṟaḻi neyyōṭu picinta uḻuntu māppōlum eṉavum; poṉpōlum tiṉiyik kaṭṭaḷi (poṉ uriyāṇi) pōlum kēḻal māntum eṉavum; avariyi niriyat tiṉṟa manti, paṇṭa vāṇikar pipōlat tōṉṟum eṉavum kūṟiyuḷḷa uvamikaḷ peritum makiḻcci viḷippaṉavā kiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum pala uvami kaḷum, uḷḷuṟikaḷum itiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ‘veḷḷa varampiṉ ūḻi’ eṉa itil vantuḷatu. tolkāppiyaṉār, ‘ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti’ eṉpataṉāl, tāmari, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉa eṇṇup peyarkaḷ kūṟiyiruppatu iṅkē karutaṟ pālatu. āṇ kuraṅkiṉi, ‘mantik kaṇavaṉ kallākkaṭuvaṉ’, ‘kuraṅkiṉṟalivaṉ’ eṉṟiṅṅaṉam itiṟ kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. ‘kuraṅki ṉēṟṟiṉik kaṭuvaṉeṉṟalum’ eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻuvamit tiruttalum kāṇka. puliyiṉ ēṟṟi, piṭiyīṉṟa kuḻavi, takar, puruvi, paṉṟik kuruḷi, paṉṟi yoruttal, mantippaṟaḻ, mantip pārppu eṉṟivi mutaliya marapukaḷum, pala vaḻakkaṅkaḷum itiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
akanāṉūṟu
eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nāṉūṟṟoru akavaṟ pākkaḷi yuṭiyatu. iḵtu, akam eṉavum, akappāṭṭu eṉavum, neṭuntoki eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷiṉ aṭiyaḷavu ciṟumi patiṉmūṉṟu, perumi muppattoṉṟu. ittokiyiṉ mutal nūṟṟirupatu pākkaḷik kaḷiṟṟuyāṉi niri eṉavum, aṭutta nūṟṟeṇpatu pākkaḷi maṇimiṭi pavaḷam eṉavum, iṟuti nūṟu pākkaḷi nittilakkōvi eṉavum āṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvar. itiṟ kapilar pāṭiyaṉa patiṉeṭṭup pākkaḷ.
ivaṟṟil, vāḻikkaṉi, palākkaṉikaḷāl cuṉiyiṭattu niṟinta tēṉi, vēṭki mikutiyāl nīreṉṟu karutiyuṇṭa kaṭuvaṉ, ayaliluḷḷa miḷaku koṭi vaḷarap peṟṟa cantaṉa marattiṉ mēl ēṟamāṭṭātu malarppaṭukkiyil kaṇpaṭi koḷḷum eṉṟum; palaviṉ paḻaṅ koḷḷumāṟu kuravar nāṭṭiya kurampi utirnta vēṅkip pūvāl mūṭap paṭṭiruppataṉip puliyeṉṟu karuti añcutaluṟṟa vēḻam, maḻi tavaḻum maliyilē kaḻi maṭiyap pāyntu ōṭā niṟkum eṉṟum; mūṅkiliṉ ṟuḷiyil kāṟṟup pukum ōci kuḻaliciyākavum, aruvi nīr vīḻcciyiṉōci muḻaviṉ kuralākavum, kaliyiṉ kuralōci peru vaṅkiyamākavum, vaṇṭiṉ pāṭṭu yāḻiciyākavum koṇṭu, mantiyiṉ avi nōkki viyappuṟumpaṭi, mūṅkil vaḷarnta pāṟiyil acin tāṭum mayil viṟali pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum; aruviyiṟ pāynta karuvirali yuṭiya manti, palāppaḻattit teppamākak koṇṭu, cāraliluḷḷa ūr muṉṟuṟiyiṟ ceṉṟu cērum eṉṟum; uḷḷuṟi porunta aḻakākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
talivaṉ varintu koḷḷa varukiṉṟamiyit talivikku aṟivikkaluṟum tōḻiyāṉavaḷ, ‘kuḷirnta kaṇṇum, taḷir pōlum mēṉiyum uṭiyāḷē! vāṉaḷāviya maliyiṉiyuṭiya nam talivar varitaṟ poruṭṭu vantamiyāl, eṉakku uṇṭākiya makiḻcciyiṉ aḷavu colliṉ, nāṭu vaṟaṅkūravum kalappi toḻiliṉṟit tuñcavum kōṭi mikka pacumiyaṟṟa kāliyil, mali pōlum karikaḷiyuṭiya periya kuḷam niṟiyum paṭiyākap perumaḻi poḻinta vikaṟiyil pallōrum uvanta uvaki yellām eṉṉuḷḷattē peytu vittāṟ pōlum’ eṉṟu taṉ makiḻcciyip pulappaṭutturippatākak kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. ‘ēri niṟintaṉiya celvaṉ kaṇṭāy’ eṉṟa tēvāram iṅkē karutattakkatu. 
talimakaṉ ciṟip puṟattāṉākat talimakaḷ tōḻik kurikku miṭattu, ‘tōḻī! nām, avar iruḷiṭi varutaliṉ ētatti yañci akaṉṟu valittiruppavum, eṉatu neñcu eṉṉiyum niṉṉiyum kēḷātu kiyikantu, paṭukuḻi kaḷiyuṭiya vaḻiyil iruḷil mitikku miṭattu avaratu taḷarnta aṭiyit tāṅkumāṟu cellā niṉṟatu’ eṉṟatākak kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. ‘karu nāyiṟu pōlpavar kāloṭu pōy, varunā ḷayalē varuvāy maṉaṉē’ eṉṟu kampar kūṟiyatu iṅkē niṉikkattakkatu.
‘vēṅkiyiṉatu poṉpōlum pūṅkottukkaḷic  cūṭi, vaḷinta villit tōḷiṉ mītirutti, palāppaḻattil viḷinta tēṟali iḷiyaroṭum mānti, kuṟipiḻiyāta veṉṟiyiyuṭiya nāy piṉṉē vara vēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟa kuṟavaṉ kuḷaviyiṉ putal kurutiyoṭu poruntumāṟu muḷḷam paṉṟiyi eytu vīḻttum kuṉṟu’ eṉṟu kāṉavaratu vēṭṭi kūṟiyirukkiṟatu.
‘īṉṟu iḷitta peṇ puliyiṉ pacikku uṇavu tarumāṟu malik kukiyiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭa pulippōttu, āmāṉiṉ peṇ alarumpaṭi, āmāṉēṟṟip pāyntu, valamuṟa vīḻtti, kariya kalliṭamellām civakkum paṭi iḻuttuvarum’ eṉap pulivēṭṭam kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. 
piṟaruṟṟa tuṉpattiṟkup piṟarum varuntuvar tammiyuṟṟa tuṉpam tamakkō eḷitu eṉṉum poruḷuṭittāka, 
‘	piṟaruṟu viḻumam piṟarum nōpa
tammuṟu viḻumam tamakkō tañcam ,’
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.
vaṇṭiṉi, ‘aṟukāl yāḻicip paṟavi’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruk kiṟatu. 
irutalip puḷ pōla iruvarkkum uyir oṉṟākiya kēṇmiyar eṉṟum; maḻiyiṟ kalitta picci mukiyiṉ verin pōlum kaṇ eṉṟum; yāṉik kayiṟṟup puṟam pōlum kalliṉ mēl ōṅkiya ciṟu neṟi eṉṟum; ēṟṟiṉ imiliṟ pūṭṭiya kayiṟu pōlat talivaṉatu tiraṇṭa tōḷil aṇinta māli puraḷa  eṉṟum uvamikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  naḷḷi yāṉavaṉ, irappōrkku maḻi curantāṟpōl, tērum, kaḷiṟum nalkuvaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
7. puṟapporuṭ pakutiyum piṟavum
puṟanāṉūṟu
eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nāṉūṟṟoru akavaṟ pākkaḷiyuṭiyatu. puṟattiṉ pakutiyākiya veṭci mutaliya tiṇikaḷukkuriya tuṟip poruḷaminta nāṉūṟu pākkaḷi yuṭimiyiṉ puṟanāṉūṟu eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. itu puṟam eṉavum, puṟappāṭṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. innūliṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ pala citintu viṭṭamiyiṉ, pākkaḷiṉuṭiya aṭikaḷiṉ ciṟumi perumi terintu koḷḷak kūṭavilli. ‘innūlāl, paṇṭik kālattu ittamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar kaḷākiya muṭiyuṭi vēntar, ciṟṟaracar, amiccar, cēṉit talivar, vīrar eṉkiṟa palaruṭiya carittiraṅkaḷum, kaṭiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ carittiraṅkaḷum, kaṭic caṅkap pulavar palaruṭiya carittiraṅkaḷum, akkālattuḷḷāruṭiya naṭi mutaliyaṉavum, iṉṉum paṟpalavum naṉkup pulappaṭum’ 
itiluḷḷa pākkaḷil, kapilar pāṭiyaṉa irupatteṭṭu. avaṟṟuḷ, iraṇṭu pāṭṭuc celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉip pāṭiyavi; tirumuṭikkāriyip pāṭiyavi nāṉku. pēkaṉip pāṭiyatu oṉṟu. pāriyip pāṭiyavi ēḻu. pāri tuñciya vaḻi iraṅkip pāṭiyavi eṭṭu. pāri makaḷirik koṇṭu ceṉṟu viccikkōṉip pāṭiyatu oṉṟu. iruṅkōvēḷip pāṭiyavi iraṇṭu. tām vaṭakkirukkum poḻutu pāriyi viḷittup pāṭiyatu oṉṟu. makaṭpāṟ kāñciyākap potuvē pāṭiyavi iraṇṭu. pāri tuñciyataṟku iraṅkip pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ mikavum urukkamāṉavi.
celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ tam kiyip paṟṟi, ‘numki melliya’ eṉak kūṟiyapoḻutu, ‘yāṉi, kutiri, tērkaḷi muṟiyāṉē celuttiyum, villi nāṇēṟṟi ampiṉi vaḻaṅkiyum pōr puriyavum, paricilarkku ariya aṇikalaṅkaḷi yaḷikkavum niṉ kikaḷ valiya vākum; ūṉiyum, tuviyiyum, kaṟiyiyum, cōṟṟiyum uṇṭu varuntum ceyalallatu vēṟu ceyal aṟiyāvākaliṉ, niṉṉip pāṭuvār kikaḷ meliyavāyiṉa’ eṉṟum; pāri tuñciya piṉ, paṟampiṉi nōkki niṉṟu, ‘perum pukaḻi yuṭiya paṟampē! matuvuṭaṉē, tuviyiyum, ūṉiyu muṭiya cōṟṟi virumpiyavāṟē tarum mikka celvam mutirntu, muṉpu emmoṭu naṭpuc ceytāy; ippoḻutu, pāri iṟantāṉākak kalaṅkic ceyalaṟṟu, nīrvār kaṇṇi yuṭiyēmāyt toḻutu niṉṉi vāḻttic cellutum’ eṉṟum kūṟiyiruttalāl, ivvāciriyar matuvum, ūṉum uṇṇu miyalpiṉar eṉac cilar karutāniṟpar. mika paḻiya nāḷil, evarum ivvaḻakkaṅkaḷik kuṟṟa meṉṟu karutiṟṟilar eṉpataṟku, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa maṉu mutaliya miruti nūlkaḷē takka cāṉṟākum. eṉiṉum, nallicip pulavarākiya kapilar ivvaḻakkamuṭiyār eṉpataṟku yām eḷitil iṇaṅkak kūṭavilli. ivvāciriyar tāmē iṉṉā nāṟpatil ivaṟṟik kaṭintirukkiṉṟaṉar. perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭiyil, antaṇarkku vēṟu tūya uṇavu kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum karutaṟpālatu. pulavarkaḷ tammip pāṇar mutaliyōr pōl vittup pāṭum vaḻakkamuṭiyarākaliṉ, kapilarum tammip pāṇarpōl vittup pāṭiyuḷḷā reṉak koḷḷutal nēritu eṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. iṅṅaṉam pāṭuvatuṇ ṭeṉpatu, ciṟukuṭi kiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉik kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pāṭiya, ‘yāṉ vāḻunāḷum paṇṇaṉ vāḻiya . . . pacippiṇi maruttuvaṉillam, aṇittō cēyttō kūṟumi ṉemakkē’ eṉṉum puṟap pāṭṭāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. annāḷil, aracar mutaliyōrum, pāṇar pōlvāri iṟappa viḻintārāka votukkātu, avarkaḷ pāl mikka aṉpupārāṭṭi vantirukka vēṇṭum eṉa, itu pōṉṟavikaḷāl karutappaṭukiṟatu. 
celvakkaṭuṅkōvip pāṭiya pāṭṭu oṉṟu mikka poruṭciṟappuṭittā yiruttaliṉ, ataṉiyum, ataṟku, ‘vēṟupaṭa vanta vuvamat tōṟṟam’ eṉṉum uvamaviyaṟ cūttira vuriyil, pērāciriyar tam nuṇṇaṟivu māṭciyāṟ kaṇṭu eḻutiya uriyiyum īṇṭut tarukiṉṟām.
‘	viyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻukap 
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāa
tiṭañciṟi teṉṉu mūkkan turappa
voṭuṅkā vuḷḷat tōmpā vīkik
kaṭantaṭu tāṉic cēra lātaṉi
yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam1
poḻuteṉa variti puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti
māṟi varuti malimaṟin toḷitti
yakaliru vicumpi ṉāṉum 
pakalviḷaṅ kaliyāṟ palkatir virittē’	 (puṟam - 8)
ippāṭṭu, uvamikkappaṭum poruḷākiya cēralātaṉi uvamiyākkiyum, uvamiyākiya tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilattip poruḷākkiyum vittu, uḷḷuṟi uvamam pōla, uvamiyaṭi poruḷukkum, poruḷaṭi uvamikkum maṟuttuk koḷḷumāṟu iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷateṉak koṇṭu, piṉ varumāṟu viḷakkukiṉṟār pērāciriyar.
“viyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻuka” eṉṟamiyāl, ivaṉ taṉ ṟaṉmiyaṉā yoḻukuva taṉṟip piṟa varacar vaḻippaṭa māṭṭāṉ; matiyamāyiṉ, oḻinta kōḷum cellat tāṉum cellum eṉṟum; ‘pōkam vēṇṭip potuc coṟ poṟātu’ eṉṟamiyāmal, ivaṉ iṉpa nukarcciyiṉ poruṭṭuc ciṟappillāk kaṭṭuri yeytāṉ; matiyamāyiṉ, irupat teḻuvar makaḷiroṭu pōkan tuyttu, ellārkkum oppa aṟañceyyātu, urōkiṇi mēṟ kaḻi peruṅ kātal koṇṭu ciṟappillāk kaṭṭuri yeytum eṉṟum; ‘iṭañ ciṟiteṉṉu mūkkan turappa’ eṉṟamiyāl, matimaṇṭilam eññāṉṟum taṉ ṉellik kaṇṇē varum eṉṟum; ‘oṭuṅkā vuḷḷam’ eṉṟamiyāl, matiyam tēyntu oṭuṅkum eṉṟum; ‘ōmpāvīki’ eṉṟamiyāl, matiyamāṉatu nāṭōṟum orōvōr kaliyākap palluyirkkum iṉpam payakkumāṟu taruvatallatu, uṭiya vellām oru kālē koṭātu eṉṟum; ‘kaṭantaṭu tāṉi’ eṉṟamiyāl, matikkut tāṉiyākiya tāraki yellām pakik katirākiya pariti maṇṭilattiṟkut tōṟkum eṉṟum; iv vāṟṟāl uvami yaṭiyellām etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ‘vīṅku celal maṇṭilam’ eṉavē, matiyam kaṭiyāyiṉār katiyiṟ cellum; pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ taliyāyiṉār katiyiṟ cellum eṉṟum; ‘poḻuteṉa variti’ eṉavē, nāṭōṟum nāḻiki vēṟupaṭṭu eṟitti eṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ poḻutu ceyyāṉ eṉṟum; ‘puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti’ eṉavē, tōṟṟōr pōṉṟu oḷi maḻuṅkic celkiṉṟā yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ piṟar tōṟṟōṭak kāyntu cuṭar pōla viḷaṅkuvaṉ eṉṟum; ‘māṟi varuti’ eṉavē, tiṅkaṭōṟum māṟip piṟatti yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ oru peṟṟiyē nili peṟṟāṉ eṉṟum; ‘mali maṟin toḷitti’ eṉavē, mali cārnta vaḻit tōṉṟā yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ taṉṉāṭṭu maliyiṉum mīkkūṟappaṭum eṉṟum; ‘akaliru vicum
piṉāṉum’ eṉavē, avaṉ ivvulakattu nili pēṟuṭiyaṉ eṉṟum; ‘pakal viḷaṅkali’ eṉavē, avaṉ irupoḻutum viḷaṅkum eṉṟum; poruṭ kuriya aṭiyellām avvāṟē etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.” kapilaratu pāṭṭu eṉṉum māṇikkatti matikka vallār pērāciriyarē pōlum!
‘	nallavun tīyavu malla kuviyiṇarp
pullili yerukka māyiṉu muṭiyavi
kaṭavuḷ pēṇē meṉṉā vāṅku
maṭavar melliyar celliṉuṅ
kaṭavaṉ pāri kivaṇ miyē.’ 	(puṟam - 106)
‘	pāri pāri yeṉṟupala vētti
yoruvaṟ pukaḻvar cennāp pulavar
pāri yoruvaṉu mallaṉ
māriyu muṇṭīṇ ṭulakupurap patuvē.’	 (puṟam - 107)
‘	nāḷaṉṟu pōkip puḷḷiṭi taṭpap 
pataṉaṉṟu pukkut tiṟaṉaṉṟu moḻiyiṉum
vaṟitu peyarkuva rallar neṟikoḷap
pāṭāṉ ṟiraṅku maruvip 
pīṭukeḻu maliyaṟ pāṭi yōrē.’	(puṟam - 124)
patiṟṟuppattu
eṭṭuttokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl, muṭiyuṭi vēnta rākiya cērar patiṉmaric caṅkappulavar patiṉmar pāṭiya nūṟu akavaṟpākkaḷi yuṭiyatu. pappattup pākkaḷuḷḷa pattup pakutikaḷit tokutta tākaliṉ, itu patiṟṟuppattu eṉap paṭṭatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām puṟattiṇikkuriya tuṟip poruḷamintaṉa. ovvoru pāṭṭiṟkum avaṟṟiluḷḷa ciṟappuṭiya toṭarāl peyariṭṭiruppatum, vaṇṇamum, tūkkum eḻutiyiruppatum, ovvoru pattiṉ īṟṟilum pāṭap paṭṭōr varalāṟṟi yuṇarttum patikam kūṟiyiruppatum innūliṉ ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru ciṟappuṭiya toṭarāl pāṭaṭukkaḷukkup peyar vittiruppatu koṇṭu, paṇṭiyōr, pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ ovvoru colliyum naṉku ārāyntu, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟappuṭiyaṉa kaṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṉum uṇmi pulaṉākiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam peyar mutaliyavum, patikamum eḻutiṉōr iṉṉāriṉṉāreṉat tuṇiyak kūṭavilli eṉiṉum, avi mikka paḻamiyuḷḷaṉaveṉṟē terikiṟatu. ovvoru patikattiṉ piṉpum, avvaracarkaḷ pāṭiṉōrkku aḷitta paricil iṉṉaveṉṟum, avarkaḷ aracu vīṟṟirunta kālam ittuṇiya veṉṟum terivikkiṉṟa kaṭṭurit toṭarkaḷ variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu, av varacarkaḷ tamiḻiṉiṭattum, tamiḻp pulavariṭattum ettuṇip perumatippuṭiya reṉpatum, ettuṇip peruvaṇmi yuṭiya reṉpatum naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. patiṟṟuppattil, eḻām pattuk kapilar pāṭiyatākum. ataṉāṟ pāṭappaṭṭōṉ celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ eṉṉum cērar perumāṉāvaṉ. ēḻām pattiṉ mutaṟpāṭṭiliruntu, vēḷpāri tuñciya piṉpu kapilar celvakkaṭuṅkōvi yaṭuttu itu pāṭiṉāreṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. itiluḷḷa,
pularnta cāntiṟ pularā vīki
malarnta mārpiṉ māvaṇ pāri
muḻavumaṇ pulara viravala riṉiya
vārāc cēṭpulam paṭarntōṉ aḷikkeṉa
irakku vārē ṉeñcik kūṟēṉ
ītta tiraṅkā ṉīttoṟu makiḻāṉ
īttoṟu māvaḷ ḷiyaṉeṉa nuvaluniṉ
nallici taravan ticiṉē
eṉṉun toṭarkaḷ pāriyiṉ arumiyiyum, kapilaratu perun taṉmiyiyum, celvak kaṭuṅkōviṉ vaṇmiyiyum terivittu, uḷḷattik kariyac ceykiṉṟaṉa.
celvak kaṭuṅkō vāṉavaṉ antaṇark kallatu paṇitalaṟi yāṉ eṉṟum, naṭṭōrk kallatu kaṇṇañcāṉ eṉṟum, nilantiṟam peyaruṅ kāliyāyiṉum kiḷanta col poytta laṟiyāṉ eṉṟum, nakiyiṉum poyyā vāymiyum, pakivar puṟañcoṟ kēḷāp puritī roṇmiyum uṭiyaṉ eṉṟum, vēḷviyāṟ ṟēvar kaṭaṉum, kēḷviyāl muṉivar kaṭaṉum, putalvarāṟ pitirar kaṭaṉum celuttiyavaṉeṉṟum, avaṉatu mātēvi kāmar kaṭavuḷumāḷuṅ kaṟpiṉaḷ eṉṟum ippattilē kapilar pārāṭṭik kūṟiyuḷḷār. koṭumaṇam, pantar eṉṟa ūrkaḷum, nēri, ayiri eṉṟa malikaḷum celvakkōvukku uriyavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉpamē tuykkum vaṭapulam (uttara kuru) itiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivvaracar perumakaṉ, māya vaṇṇaṉi maṉaṉuṟap peṟṟa
vaṉeṉṟum, akkaṭavuḷukku okantūr eṉṉum patiyiṉi yaḷittava ṉeṉṟum patikam kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivaṉ irupattinti yāṇṭu aracu vīṟṟiruntā ṉeṉṟum, kapilarukkuc ‘ciṟupuṟameṉa nūṟāyiraṅ kāṇaṅ koṭuttu, naṉṟā veṉṉum kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟu, taṉ kaṇṇiṟ kaṇṭa nāṭellām kāṭṭik koṭuttāṉ’ eṉṟum patikattiṉ piṉṉuḷḷa kaṭṭurikaḷ terivikkiṉṟaṉa. cēramāṉpāṟ kapilar iṅṅaṉam perum paricil peṟṟa ceyti, iḷañcēralirum poṟiyip peruṅkuṉṟūrk kiḻār pāṭiya oṉpatām pattil, ‘kapilaṉ peṟṟa vūriṉum palavē’ eṉṟu uvamiyil vittōti yiruntalālum naṉku valiyuṟuttap paṭukiṉṟatu.
iṉṉā nāṟpatu 
patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkiṉuḷ 1oṉṟāya innūl, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uḷpaṭa nāṟpattoru veṇpākkaḷi yuṭiyatu. itiluḷḷa ovvoru pāṭṭum, iṉṉatu iṉṉatu ‘iṉṉā’ eṉak kūṟutaliṉ, iḵtu iṉṉā nāṟpatu eṉap peya reytiṟṟu. itaṟku etirāka, iṉṉatu iṉṉatu ‘iṉitu’ eṉak kūṟum pākkaḷi yuṭiya nūl iṉiyavi nāṟpatu eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟip pārttup pāṭiyatākum eṉak karutaṟku iṭaṉuṇṭu. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ, kaṭic caṅkap pulavarkaḷālum, ciṟitu piṟkālattu viḷaṅkiya pulavar kaḷālum, iyaṟṟap paṭṭaṉaveṉat terikiṟatu. ivaṟṟuṭ cila nūlkaḷ akap poruḷākiya iṉpattik kūṟāniṟka, pala nūlkaḷ aṟam mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya nītikaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avi kūṟum nītikaḷ tām, perumpālum ellā makkaḷum mēṟ koḷḷat takkavāyum, uṟuti payappaṉavāyum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiluḷḷa oppuyarvillā nīti nūlkaḷiliruntē tamiḻp perumakkaḷiṉ mēṉmi malimici viḷakkup pōl yāvarkkum viḷaṅkā niṟkum. naṭuvu niliyuṭiya mēṉāṭṭup pulavarkaḷum ivvuṇmiyiṉi aṟintu kūṟi vittiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟirukkavum, tiru. em. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār, M.A. avarkaḷ, tām eḻutiya tamiḻārāycci (Tamil Studies) eṉṟa puttakattil, tamiḻ makkaḷi varampiṉṟip puṟam paḻittuk kūṟi varumiṭiyē, ‘paḻaṅkālattil tamiḻ makkaḷ oḻukkattil mikat tāḻnta niliyiliruntaṉar; ātalāṟṟāṉ tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliya nīti nūlkaḷ pala tōṉṟuvavāyiṉa.’ eṉkiṉṟaṉar. nīti nūl oṉṟu milliyāyiṉ, akkālattu makkaḷellām oḻukkattiluyarntavar eṉpatu avaratu karuttup pōlum! avaratu tarukka vaṟivutāṉ iruntavā ṟeṉṉē! avar piḻiyiṉi avaratu neñcamē yeṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiṭi viṭā toṟukkum eṉpatu tuṇipu. 
iṉi, ivvāciriyar, tuṉpiṉ mūlaṅkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa veṉak kaṇṭu, avaṟṟiyē tokutteṭuttuk kūṟiyiruppatu pārāṭṭaṟ pālatoṉṟām. makkaḷ yāvarum tām virumpuvatu iṉpamē yāyiṉum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷi yaṟintu, ēṟṟam peṟṟi naṭavāmiyāl, tuṉpamē peritum eytu kiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu ekkālattiṟkum potuvāka okkum, iṉpattiṉ kāraṇatti yaṟintu mēṟkoḷḷutaliṉum, mutaṟkaṭ ceyyaṟ pālatu, tuṉpattiṉ kāraṇatti yaṟintu, ataṉiṉ nīṅku talākum. ātalāṟṟāṉ, antaṇmi pūṇṭa kapilar, tuṉpiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷit tokutturippārāyiṉar eṉka.
innūl, tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliyaṉa pōṉṟu kūṟa vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷiyellām aṭivupaṭa vakuttuk kūṟa vanta taṉṟu ākaliṉ, itaṉi, avaṟṟōṭu oppiṭṭup pārttal poruntātu. oru pēraṟiñar oruvāṟāka ulakiyali virintu nōkkuṅkāl, avaruḷḷattil iṉṉāveṉat tōṉṟiyavikaḷ, itil avvappaṭiyē vikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉaveṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum. itil, nītikaḷē yallāmal, ciṟcila makkaḷiyaṟki mutaliyavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. orē karuttup palaviṭattil vevvēṟu toṭarkaḷāṟ kūṟappaṭṭu mirukkiṟatu. kaḷḷuṇṭal, kavaṟāṭal, ūṉuṇṭal  eṉpaṉa itiṟ kaṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. innūlil vantuḷḷa, ‘ūṉit tiṉṟūṉip perukkutal muṉṉiṉṉā,’ ‘kuḻavikaḷuṟṟa piṇiyiṉṉā’, ‘kallārurikkuṅ karumap poruḷiṉṉā’ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷōṭu, iṉiyatu nāṟpatil vantuḷḷa ‘ūṉit tiṉṟūṉip perukkāmi muṉṉiṉitē’, ‘kuḻavi piṇiyiṉṟi vāḻta liṉitē’, ‘kaṟṟaṟintār kūṟuṅ karumap poruḷiṉitē’ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷ oṟṟumi yuṟutal kāṇka.
poruḷuṇarvā rilvaḻip pāṭṭuritta liṉṉā iruḷkūr ciṟuneṟit tāntaṉippōk kiṉṉā
aruḷillār taṅkaṭ celaviṉṉā viṉṉā
poruḷillār vaṇmi purivu. 	(10)
mūttanāyaṉār tiruviraṭṭi maṇimāli
ituvum, piṉvarum iraṇṭu pirapantaṅkaḷum, patiṉōrān tirumuṟit tokuppiṟ cērntuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟukkum, caṅkap pulavar pāṭiya eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭukkaḷukkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumikaḷ, ‘nakkīrar’ eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 11-ām tirumuṟiyil vantuḷḷa mūtta nāyaṉār tiruviraṭṭi maṇimāli, civaperumāṉ tiruviraṭṭi maṇimāli, civaperumāṉ tiruvantāti yeṉṉum pirapantaṅkaḷ mūṉṟiyum aruḷic ceyta kapila tēvanāyaṉā reṉpavar ivarēyeṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvar’ eṉṟu makā makōpāttiyāyar, tiru. u.vē. cāminātiya ravarkaḷum 2‘kapilar ceyta vēṟu pirapantaṅkaḷ cila patiṉōrān tirumuṟiyiṟ cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa’ eṉṟu, tiru. a nārāyaṇacāmi aiyaravarkaḷum oruvāṟu nekiḻntu kūṟiyuḷḷamiyum kāṇka. ‘eṉiyantāti coṉṉavaṉ kapilaṉ’ eṉṟu civapirāṉ kūṟiyatākat tiruviḷiyāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itu, veṇpāvum, kaṭṭaḷik kalittuṟiyumākac coṟ ṟoṭar niliyāy maṇṭalittu varum irupatu pāṭṭukkaḷi yuṭiyatu. tamiḻil mūtta piḷḷiyārākiya vināyakak kaṭavuḷip paṟṟik kūṟuvatil innūlē mutaṉmiyuṭiyatu eṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. itilirunta iraṇṭu ceyyuṭkaḷ iḷampūraṇa vaṭikaḷāl mēṟkōḷāka eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tiruvākkuñ ceykarumaṅ kikūṭṭuñ ceñcoṟ
peruvākkum pīṭum perukkum - uruvākkum 
ātalāl vāṉōru māṉi mukattāṉik
kātalāl kūppuvartaṅ ki. 	(1)
civaperumāṉ tiruviraṭṭi maṇimāli
itu, veṇpāvālum, kaṭṭaḷik kalittuṟiyālum civaperumāṉip pāṭiyatōr pirapantam; coṟṟoṭar niliyāka vuḷḷatu. itil muppattēḻu pāṭṭukkaḷ ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭuvaṉa.
aṭiyōmit tāṅkiyō vāṭi yuṭuttō
kuṭiyōmpa mānitiyaṅ koṇṭō - poṭiyāṭu
neṟṟiyūr vāḷarava nīḷcaṭiyāy niṉṉūri
yoṟṟiyū rākkiṟ ṟuri.	 (15)
civaperumāṉ tiruvantāti
itu, nērici veṇpā nūṟuṭiyatāyc coṟ ṟoṭar niliyāy, iṟutip pāṭṭiṉ iṟutic cīr mutaṟpāṭṭiṉ mutaṟ cīrāka maṇṭalittu muṭiyap peṟṟuḷatu. itaṉkaṇ akap poruṭṭuṟiyaminta pākkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām, maṭakkum, tiripum poruntiyaṉa. civaperumāṉip paṟṟiya ivviraṇṭu pirapantaṅkaḷilum tiruvoṟṟiyūr, tirumutukuṉṟu, tiruvaṇṇāmali, tirukkōvalūr, tiruvalañcuḻi, tiruccattimuṟṟam, tiruvārūr, tiruppaḻaṉam, tiruviyāṟu, tirukkoṭumuṭi mutaliya pala tiruppatikaḷ pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
‘	ūruma toṟṟiyū ruṇkalaṉum veṇṭaliyē 
yūrum viṭiyoṉ ṟuṭitōlē - yūrum
paṭanāka maṭṭār paṇamāli yītō
paṭanāka maṭṭār paricu. ’	(6)
kapilam
tamiḻiluḷḷa pirapantaṅkaḷukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟunūl pāṭṭiyal eṉappaṭum. paṉṉiru pāṭṭiyal eṉa vaḻaṅkum nūliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ piṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa peyarkaḷāl, avaṟṟi iyaṟṟiṉōr caṅkap pulavar paṉṉiruva reṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. ippoḻutu paṉṉirupāṭṭiyal eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu, paṉṉiruvar taṉittaṉi yiyaṟṟiya pāṭṭiyal
kaḷiliruntu ciṟcila cūttiraṅkaḷi yeṭuttut tokuttu vitta tākal vēṇṭum. atililuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ caṅkap pulavari
yaṟṟiyaṉa veṉpatu tuṇipāyiṉ, ataṉuḷ kapilar peyarum kāṇappaṭuvatāl, avarum pāṭṭiyal iyaṟṟiyuḷḷāreṉṟum, atu kapilam eṉa vaḻaṅkaṟkuriyatākum eṉṟum koḷḷalākum.
muṭipu
itukāṟum ceyta ārāycciyāl, tamiḻ moḻiyi vaḷarttaṟ poruṭṭākap paṇṭi nāḷilē maturiyampati yiṟṟamiḻc caṅkam naṭipeṟṟu vantateṉpatum, caṅkattu vīṟṟirunta nallicip pulavar perumakkaḷuḷḷē kapilarāṉavar kalviyilum, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟuvatilum, vāymi, aṉpu mutaliya kuṇaṅkaḷilum oppuyar villārāy viḷaṅkiṉareṉpatum, avaratu perumiyi akkālattu vēntarkaḷum pulavarkaḷum oruṅku pārāṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷeṉpatum, vaḷḷalākiya vēḷpārikku avar āruyirt tōḻarāy viḷaṅkiṉareṉpatum, vēḷpāri tuñciya piṉ avaṉatu piriviṟku āṟṟātu palavāṟu pulampiṉareṉpatum, pārimakaḷirit takutiyuḷḷa āṭavarkku vāḻkkip paṭutta vēṇṭi mika muyaṉṟaṉareṉpatum, cēramāṉ celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ, maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri, vēḷpēkaṉ mutalāyiṉāriyum avar pāṭiyuḷḷāreṉpatum, kuṟiñcittiṇi pāṭuvatil nikaraṟṟa āṟṟaluṭiyavareṉpatum, āriyavaracaṉ pirakattaṉit tamiḻaṟivuṟutta avar pāṭiya kuṟiñcippāṭṭum, kuṟiñcikkaliyum mutaliya ceyyuṭkaḷ, coṟ ceṟivum, poruṭ polivum uṭiyavāy, aṉittaḻakum porunti viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉpatum, avaṟṟiliruntē paṇṭi nāḷil tamiḻ aṭintirunta mēṉmiyum, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uḷḷap perumiyum naṉku veḷiyākiṉṟaṉaveṉpatum, piṟavum teḷivuṟuttap peṟṟami kāṇka.
vāḻka maṉṉavaṉ vāḻka maṉṉuyir 
vāḻka centamiḻ vāḻka cemmiyē.


akattiyar
varalāṟu

3. akattiyar varalāṟu
tamiḻp pulavarkaḷāka ippoḻutu aṟiyappaṭuvōr yāvariṉum kālattāl muntiyavarum, iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻukkum ciṟantatōr ilakkaṇañ cey taruḷiyum, tamiḻc  caṅkam niṟuviyum, naṉmāṇākkar palarkku nūlaṟivuṟuttiyum tamiḻmoḻiyip pōṟṟi vaḷartta perumiyil mutaṉmi yuṟṟiruppōrum āciriyar akattiya ṉārāvar. akattiyar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum pōntavareṉap pala katikaḷum kūṟāniṟpa, avar teṟkiṉkaṇ iruntavarēyeṉa orucārār kūṟupa. 'ceṅkōṉṟaric celavu' eṉṉum ōr paḻiya nūlil ciṟappuppāyiram pōṟ kāṇappaṭum oru ceyyuḷ 'paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟut talippāyccal  ēḻ teṅka nāṭṭu muttūr akattiyaṉ kūṟṟu' eṉa avvuri kūṟukiṉṟatu . akattiyar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum pōntavarāka, teṟkilē tāṉ iruntavarāka,teṟkiliruntum vaṭakkē ceṉṟu piṉṉum teṟkiṉ kaṇ mīṇṭavarāka, eṅṅaṉam āyiṉum ākuka. avar iṉṉa nāṭṭiṉar, iṉṉa kuṭiyiṉareṉa yām ippoḻutu ārāyntu muṭivu kaṭṭavallē mallēm. avarukkum tamiḻukkum iyipu kāṭṭit tollōr palarum kūṟiyavi kaḷi oruvāṟu tokutturittuc ciṟitu ārāycci nikaḻttutalē ippoḻutu ceyaṟpālatu.
civaperumāṉ maliyariyaṉ pāviyārit tirumaṇam puritaru kālattē tēvar, muṉivar mutalāya pal kaṇattārum imayattil tiraṇṭiruntamiyiṉ poṟiyāṟṟātu vaṭapuvi tāḻntu teṉpuvi yuyarntatāka, appoḻutu iṟivar akattiyaṉārit teṟkiṉkaṇ potiyiliṟ ceṉṟirukkumāṟu vēṇṭik koḷḷa avarum aṅṅaṉamē pōntiruntaṉar; puviyum tuliyottatu eṉṟu kantapurāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. akattiyar iṟivariṭam viṭi peṟṟuc cellum poḻutu; 'teṉṟēyam tamiḻ nāṭeṉṟum, aṅkuḷḷa māntarellām tamiḻkkalvi mikkāreṉṟum kūṟuvar; aṉiyavar kēṭpa vaṟṟukkum viṭiyiṟuttal vēṇṭumākaliṉ centamiḻ iyal nūliṉi emperumāṉ aṭiyēṟku aruḷic ceyyavēṇṭum' eṉṟu kuṟiyirappa, aṅkaṇar avarkku mutaṉūl aruḷic ceytaṉar eṉṟu tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉi yuṇarttum ceyyuṭkaḷ,
'	viṭikoṭu pōvā ṉoṉṟi vēṇṭiṉa ṉēkun tēyam
toṭipeṟu tamiḻnā ṭeṉṟu collupa anta nāṭṭiṉ
 iṭipayiṉ mānta rellām iṉṟami ḻāyntu kēḷvi 
uṭiyava reṉpa kēṭṭārk kuttara murittal vēṇṭum,' 1 
'	cittamā cakala vantac centami ḻiyaṉūl taṉṉi
attaṉē yaruḷic ceyti yeṉṟaṉaṉ aṉiyāṉ ṟēṟa
vittaṉi mutaṉūl taṉṉi maṟṟatu teḷinta  piṉṉum 
nittaṉē yaṭiyēṉ eṉṟu niṉṉaṭi kāṇpē ṉeṉṟāṉ' 12
eṉpaṉa, civaperumāṉē akattiyarkkut tamiḻaṟivuṟutta mutaṟ kurava reṉpataṉi,
irumoḻikkum kaṇṇutalār mutaṟ kuravar' 3 
teṉmoḻiyi ulakamelān toḻutēttu,
kuṭamuṉikku valivuṟuttār  kollēṟṟup pākar'4 
eṉṟu kāñcippurāṇamum kūṟukiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum kāñcip purāṇa āciriyarākiya civañāṉa muṉivar akattiyarkku vaṇakkam kūṟumiṭattu, avar kāciyiṉiṉṟum pōntatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar . acceyyuḷ piṉ varuvatu:
'	kāciyi ṉiṉṟum pōntu kampartā  maruḷap peṟṟu 
mācilāk kacci mūtūr maṉṉivīṟ ṟiruntu pūmēl 
ācilāt tamiḻ parappi yaruntamiḻk kuravu pūṇṭa
tēciṉāṉ malaya veṟpiṟ kuṟumuṉi tiruttāḷ pōṟṟi' 5
akattiyar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum pōnta varalāṟākat tol kāppiyap pāyira vuriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu,  'tēvarellāruṅkūṭi yām cēra viruttaliṉ mērut tāḻntu teṉṟici yuyarntatu , itaṟku akattiyaṉārē āṇṭiruttaṟ kuriya reṉṟu, avari vēṇṭikkoḷḷa, avarum teṉṟicikkaṭ pōtukiṉṟavar kaṅkiyāruḻic ceṉṟu kāviriyāri vāṅkikkoṇṭu,   piṉṉar emataṅkiyāruḻic ceṉṟu,  avar makaṉār tiraṇatū mākkiṉiyāri vāṅkikkoṇṭu pulatti yaṉāruḻic ceṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ piṟanta kumariyār ulōpā muttiriyāri avar koṭuppa nīrēṟṟu irīip peyarntu, tuvarāpatip pōntu nilaṅkaṭanta neṭu muṭiyaṇṉalvaḻikkaṇ aracar  patiṉeṇmariyum, patiṇeṇkuṭi vēḷi1 ruḷḷiṭ ṭāriyum, aruvāḷariyum koṇṭu pōntu, kāṭu keṭuttu nāṭākkiya, potiyiliṉkaṇ iruntu, irāvaṇaṉik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatari āṇṭu iyaṅkāmi vilakkiṉar, eṉpatu.
itil tirukkalyāṇamum tamiḻ kēṭṭatum kūṟappaṭā viṭiṉum teṉṟici yuyarnta noymmitīra akattiyar vaṭak kiṉiṉṟum pōntār eṉpatu vēṟu cila ceytikaḷuṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
vīracōḻiyattiṉ aviyaṭakkac ceyyuḷoṉṟu avalōkitaṉ pāl akattiyar  tamiḻ kēṭṭaṉareṉak kūṟum.
atu,
āyuṅ kuṇattava lōkitaṉ pakka lakatti yaṉkēṭ 
ṭēyum puvaṉik kiyampiya taṇṭami ḻīṅku rikka 
nīyu muḷiyō veṉiṟkaru ṭaṉceṉṟa nīḷvicumpi  
līyum paṟakku mitaṟkeṉko lōcollu mēntiḻiyē'2
eṉpatu.
avalōkitaṉ eṉpāṉ vicēṭika taricaṉattiṉ āciriyaṉākiya kaṇātaṉ eṉpar. ōr camaṇamuṉi yeṉak kūṟuvārum uḷar .
akattiyaṉārkku āciriyar murukakkaṭavuḷ eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakku muṇṭu. civañāṉa cittiyārilum,civappirakācattilum muṟiyē,
'	arumaṟiyā kamamaṅkam aruṅkali nūl terinta
akattiyaṉuk kōtturikkum aruṭkuruvāṅ kuruḷi'3
'	alakilpala kaliyaṅkam āraṇaṅka ḷuṇarnta 
akattiyaṉuk kōtturikku maṇṇal'1
eṉṟu murukapperumāṉ akattiyarkku urittami kūṟiyiruk kiṟatu. ivaṟṟil tamiḻ urittaṉa reṉak kiḷantōtap paṭā viṭiṉum 'arumaṟiyākamam aṅkam aruṅkali nūl'ellām uṇarntavarkku urikku meṉṟa kuṟippāl ottateṉpatu tamiḻ nūli yuṇarttumpōlum eṉṟu karuta vēṇṭiyiruk kiṟatu.  itu tuṇipaṉṟu, tolkāppiyap pāyira viruttiyil, civañāṉa muṉivar 'tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vaṭakkaṭ piṟa velliyu muḷavāka  vēṅkaṭatti yelliyākak kūṟiṉār. akattiyaṉārkkut tamiḻic ceviyaṟivuṟutta centamiḻp paramācāriyaṉākiya aṟumukakkaṭavuḷ varip peṉṉum iyipupaṟṟi yeṉpatu' eṉṟaṉar. ivi yellām aṟiñar ārāyccik kuriyaṉa.
akattiyar civapirāṉpāl tamiḻ kēṭṭaṉar eṉpataṟ kuriya cāṉṟukaḷē mikutiyāka vuḷḷaṉa. kalviyiṟ periyarāya kamparum tamatu irāmāyaṇattu akattiyap paṭalattil,
'	uḻakkumaṟi nāliṉu muyarntulaka mōtum
vaḻakkiṉu matikka viyiṉummarapi ṉāṭi
niḻaṟpoli kaṇiccimaṇi neṟṟiyumiḻ ceṅkaṭ
ṭaḻaṟpuri cuṭarkkaṭavuḷ tantatamiḻ tantāṉ.'2
eṉpataṉāl civapirāṉ ṟanta tamiḻi ulakiṟkut tantavar eṉṟu akattiyarik kūṟiyuḷḷār. 
iṉi, muṉ kāṭṭiyavaṟṟāṉē akattiyar vēṟu vēṟiṭaṅ kaḷiliruntum teṟkilē pōntaṉa reṉpatu peṟappaṭṭu varalāṟukaḷ tammilē malivaṉapōl kantapurāṇat tiṉiṉṟum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. avi: 
"oru kālattil tēva iruṭiyākiya nārataṉ cūḻcciyāl vintamaliyāṉatu mēruvuṭaṉ māṟukoṇṭu mēleḻuntu vāṉ vaḻiyellām aṭittiṭā niṉṟatu. ataṉāl ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ mutaliya puttēḷir tām iyaṅkumāṟu kāṇātu kavaṟciyuṟṟu, appoḻutu mēruviṉ orucār tavampurintu koṇṭirunta akattiyaṉāri yuṉṉik kuṟiyirappārāyiṉar. aṉṉavar kuṟippu tam uḷḷattē tōṉṟappeṟṟa kuṟu muṉivarum 'vintattiṉ āṟṟali yoṭukki vāṉavar kuṟiyiṉi nīkkuvēm' eṉa eṇṇi, aruṭ kaṭalāya civaperumāṉi niṉintu aṉpiṉāl uḷḷaṅ kuḻintu urukiniṉṟaṉar. aṉpar aṉpukkeḷiyarāya iṟivarum akattiyaṉārkkuk kāṭci tantu 'vintamaliyi vēroṭuṅ keṭukkum āṟṟal niṉakku vaḻaṅkiṉām. nī ceṉṟu ataṉi yaḻittup piṉpu potiyili yaṭintu aṅkaṇ vikiyiruppāy' eṉak kūṟi, kiliyiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa ēḻ natiyuḷḷum tūymi mikkatākiya kāviriyi aḻittu,'nī kuṟṟamaṟṟa kuṟumuṉi taṉṉoṭum celvāyāka' eṉap paṇittu maṟintaruḷiṉar. vitarppa maṉṉaṉ pāvi ulōpā muttiriyāri maṇantu meyyaṟivu muṟṟiya cittaṉ eṉṉum mintaṉi yaḷitta vaḷḷalāya akattiyaṉārum aṅkaṇar tiruvaruḷiṟ ṟōyntu, kāviriyik kamaṇṭalattiṟ peṟṟuk koṇṭu, teṉṟici nokkic ceṉṟaṉar. ceṉṟavar, vaḻiyiṭi vañcaṉi yiḻitta kiravuñcaṉ eṉṉum arakkaṉi, maliyāyk kiṭantu piṉ kumārak kaṭavuḷiṉ tirukki vēlāl viḷiyumāṟu vitittum, kācit tiruppati yaṭintu ulaka nātarāya kaṇṇutaṟ perumāṉi vaḻipaṭṭu ēttiyum, vinta maliyip pilattilē pukumāṟu aḻuttiyum, vilvalaṉ vātāvi yeṉṉum arakkar iruvari maṭivittum, koṅku nāṭṭilē civaliṅkap perumāṉi yeḻuntaruḷvittup pūcaṉi purintum, āṉi mukaṉaruḷpeṟṟuk kāviriyip perukaviṭuttum, tiruk kuṟṟālattilē tirumāluruvit tamatu tirukkiyālē kuḻivittuc civaliṅkamākki vaḻipaṭṭum, potiyili yaṭintu vīṟṟiruntaruḷiṉar" eṉpaṉa. piṉvarum ceyyuṭkaḷ iṅku nōkkaṟ pālaṉa: 
'	cempoṉmāl variyiṉ pāṅkiṟ civaṉaṭi yuṉṉi nōṟṟē
ampurā ciyimuṉ ṉuṇṭō ṉamarntaṉa ṉavaṉīṇ ṭuṟṟāl
umparpōy niṉṟa vinta moṭuṅkumeṉ ṟiyāru mōrntu
kumpamā muṉiyi yuṉṉi yiṉiyaṉa kūṟa luṟṟār.'1
	(kanta - vintakirip paṭalam)
'	aṉiya kāli yaruṅkayi lāyamēl 
iṉitu vikiya vēḻnati taṉṉuḷum
puṉita mākiya pūmpuṉaṟ poṉṉiyip 
paṉima ticcaṭip paṇṇavaṉ muṉṉiṉāṉ.'2
	(mēṟpaṭi - akattiyappaṭalam)
"	attaṉaṅ koruva vaṉṉā ṉaruḷaṭin taṅka ṇīṅki
meyttaku matali vēṇṭi  vitarpparkōṉ payanta lōpā
muttiri taṉimuṉ vēṭṭu mutukkuṟit tiṇmi cāṉṟa
cittaṉi yaḷitta vaḷḷal teṉṟici nōkkic ceṉṟāṉ."1
	(mēṟpaṭi - vintampilampuku paṭalam)
"	pintamiḻ muṉivaṉ vāṉṟōy paṉivari yataṉi nīṅkik
kantarañ ceṟipoṟ kōṭṭuk kaṭavuḷar variccā reyti
antama taṭintōrk kaṅka ṇaruḷiṉāṟ ṟaṉatu mūla
mantira muṇarttu mukkaṇ vāṉavaṉ kāci pukkāṉ."2
	(mēṟpaṭi - vintampilampuku paṭalam)
"	kaṅkiceṉ ṟoḻukuṅ kācik kaṟimiṭaṟ ṟakila nātaṉ
paṅkaya vaṭikaḷ tāḻntu paravalcey tāṇṭu nīṅkit
tuṅkati koṇṭa vintat tolvarik korucā rēki
aṅkata nilimi nōkki yaṟivaṉoṉ ṟiyampa luṟṟāṉ." 3
	(mēṟpaṭi - akattiyap paṭalam)
"	aṟukumati natipuṉiyuñ ceñcaṭiyem perumāṉi
	yakattuṭ koṇṭu
ciṟukumuru vuṭiyamuṉi nāraṇaṉār tirumuṭimēṟ ceṅki 			yōccik
kuṟukukuṟu keṉavirutti yoḷḷarakkiṟ puṉipāvik 			kōla mītu 
maṟukutaḻa luṟṟeṉṉak kuḻivittōr civaliṅka vaṭivañ 			ceytāṉ."4
	(mēṟpaṭi - tirukkuṟṟālap paṭalam)
ivvāṟu potiyiliṉkaṭ pōntirunta akattiya muṉivar umiyammiyār tirukkalyāṇattiṉpoḻutu imayattiṟkuc ceṉṟiruntu puviyiṉ ṟaṭumāṟṟattip pōkkumāṟu perumāṉ kaṭṭaḷiyāl mīṭṭum potiyili yaṭintaṉar eṉat terikiṉṟatu. itaṉi, 
"	kiṟpuṟu māyi valla kiravuñca variyum vinta 
veṟpatum vaṉmi cinta vilvala ṉoṭuvā tāvi
kaṟpaṉi yakaṉṟu māyak kāviri nīttat tōṭu 
muṟpakal paṭarnta teṉṉa muṉivaraṉ ṟeṉpāḷ pōṉāṉ."1
	(kanta - tirukkalyāṇap paṭalam)
eṉṉum tiruppāṭṭāṉ aṟika. ivvāṟṟāl akattiyar vevvēṟiṭattu niṉṟum pōntamipaṟṟi eytuvatōr māṟupāṭum iṉmi kāṇka. kantapurāṇam ulōpā muttiri
yāri vitarppanāṭṭaracaṉ putalviyeṉak kūṟāniṟpa, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar avarip pulattiyaṉāruṭaṉ piṟantārākak kūṟuvatu ōr māṟupāṭām. tirukkiliyilirunta kāviriyic civapirāṉpāṟ peṟṟāreṉpatum, kaṅkiyāruḻic ceṉṟu kāviriyāri vāṅkikkoṇṭā reṉpatum pōlvaṉa orumippaṭuttu nōkkaṟpālaṉa.
"	amara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ ṟaṉātu
karakaṅ kaviḻtta kāvirip pāvi"2
eṉa maṇimēkaliyum ivvaralāṟu terikkiṉṟatu. tiruk kalyāṇattiṉ muṉpē muṉikkaracar potiyilil iruntār eṉpataṉāl appoḻutē avar tamiḻ aṟintiruntārātalum vēṇṭum. akattiyar civapirāṉ pakkal tamiḻ aṟintami kūṟum iruvēṟu tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇamum 'veḷḷimaliyil tēvar, muṉivar mutalāyiṉār kūṭiyiruntuḻip puviyiṉ vaṭakīḻ ellitāḻntu teṉmēṟ kelli uyarāniṟka, appoḻutu akattiyaṉārip potiyilukkuc cella viṭutta iṟivar avarkkut tamiḻ mutaṉūl aruḷic ceytār ' eṉṟu potuvē kūṟukiṉṟaṉavaṉṟit tirumaṇattic cuṭṭik kūṟukiṉṟila. iṉi, akattiyaṉār kaṭaluṇṭā reṉpatum, avar mēruvil tavamiyaṟṟumpoḻutu  vintamali uyarntu piṉpu avarāl pātalattil aḻuttappaṭṭateṉpatum, maṟṟum orukāl avar vaṭakkil irukkumpoḻutu vaṭapāl tāḻntu teṉpāl uyarntu nilitaṭumāṟiya puvi avar potiyili yaṭintavuṭaṉ camaṉeytiṟṟeṉpatum ākiya ceytikaḷ nilanūl uṇmikaḷ palavaṟṟi nam niṉiviṉkaṭ koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. kaṭaluṇṭa ceyti oru kālattil imayappakuti kaṭalākaviruntu piṉ mēleḻuntataṉiyum, vintattiṉ cerukkaṭakkiyatu imayam mēlōṅkiya poḻutu vinta maliyiṉ eḻucci kuṟintataṉiyum, vaṭakīḻttici tāḻntatu āṅkaṇ ōr nilappakuti kaṭal (cākaram) āyataṉiyum, teṉmēṟku uyarntatu kumarikaṇṭamākiya tamiḻakattiṉ perumpakutiyi vuviya paḻiya kaṭalkōḷiṉ piṉpu orucār kaṭaṟpakuti nilam (paracurāmaṉ peṟunāṭu) ākiyataṉiyum tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟaṉa. innikaḻccikaḷukkellām kāraṇarā yiruntavar iṟivaṉ ceyyalākum ceyali avaṉukku ottapaṭiyākaviruntu ceyyum perumi vāyntavar eṉṉalāl iviyellām iṟivaṉ ceyaleṉpatu peṟappaṭum. niṟka. 
akattiyaṉār orumuṟi mēruvil iruntapoḻutu vintam uyarntateṉṟum, maṟṟorumuṟi imayatti aṭinta poḻutu vaṭatici tāḻntu teṉṟici yuyarntateṉṟum, ovvoru muṟiyum avar  teṟkil vantu appiṟaḻccikaḷip pōkkiya ruḷiṉā reṉṟum kēṭkappaṭutaliṉālē nilamāṉatu piṟaḻ veytāmip poruṭṭu muṉivarpirāṉ ekkālattum potiyiliṉkaṇ iruttaṟpālarpōlum eṉṟu karuta vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu. avar yāṇṭum tamiḻmuṉiyeṉṟum, potiyiṉ muṉiyeṉṟum kūṟappaṭutal cintikkattakkatu.
"	teṉṟamiḻnāṭ ṭakaṉpotiyiṟ ṟirumuṉivaṉ
        ṟamiḻccaṅkañ cēru vīrēl
eṉṟumava ṉuṟiviṭamā mātaliṉā
        ṉammaliyi yiṟiñci yēkip 
poṉṟiṇinta puṉalperukum poruniyeṉun
        tirunatiyiṉ poḻiya nākak 
kaṉṟuvaḷar taṭañcāraṉ makēntiramā
        neṭuvariyuṅ kaṭaluṅ kāṇṭir."1
eṉa irāmāyaṇattu nāṭaviṭṭa paṭalam kūṟuvatum kāṇka.
immuṉivar perumāṉ pukaḻ paṉṉūlkaḷilum parantu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. curuṅkavurikkiṉ civapirāṉum, civakumārarumaṉṟi vēṟu yāriyum taṉiyē oppuccolla voṇṇāta attuṇip perumi vāyntavar ivar eṉal amiyum. cilappatikāra maṅkala vāḻttiṉkaṇṇē pukāric ciṟappikka vēṇṭiya viṭattu,
"	potiyi lāyiṉum imaya māyiṉum
patiyeḻu vaṟiyāp paḻaṅkuṭi keḻīiya
potuvaṟu ciṟappiṟ pukārē yāyiṉum
naṭukkiṉṟi niliiya veṉpa tallati 
oṭukkaṅ kūṟār uyarntō ruṇmiyiṉ 
muṭitta kēḷvi muḻutuṇarn tōrē" 1 
eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟiya poruṇ moḻi iṅkē pōṟṟaṟ pālatākum. aṭiyārkku nallārum 'uyarntōr' eṉpataṟku 'muṉivaṉum iṟivaṉum aracaṉum' eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉār. akattiyaṉār purintaṉavākak kūṟappaṭum kaṭal kuṭittal mutaliya ceytikaḷukku vēṟu uḷḷuṟipporuḷ koḷḷutal cālātu eṉiṉum, avi, iṟivaṉaruḷ niṟiyap
peṟṟa periyārāl ulakilē ceyyalākāta poruḷ oṉṟu
milli yeṉṉum potuvuṇmiyi viḷakkuvaṉavākak kōṭal amiyum.
"	kitavaṉ karantu vikuṅ kaṭaliveṟ paṭakkuṅ kiyiṟ
peytuḻun tellit tākkip parukiṉāṉ piṟicēr ceṉṉi
aiyaṉa taruḷip peṟṟārk katicaya miteṉkol mūṉṟu
viyamut toḻiluñ ceyya vallava ravarē yaṉṟō?" 2
eṉṟu parañcōtiyār tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟuvatu īṇṭu nōkkat takkatu.
iṉi, ittakiya perumi vāynta akattiyaṉār tamiḻc caṅkap pulavaruḷḷē mutalvarāka vitteṇṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivar kāciyi lirunta vaṭamoḻic caṅkattāroṭu māṟupaṭṭu, avar cerukki yaṭakkutaṟ poruṭṭut tamiḻc caṅkam niṟuviṉar eṉpatu pōlum katikaḷ pala vuḷa. teṉmaturi yakattē taliccaṅkam nilaviya ñāṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻ pōṉṟu ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya teṉpatum, vaṭamoḻi vaḷarccikkum caṅkamirunta teṉpatum meymmiyākumēl, itu pōlum katikaḷ ettuṇik kālamāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa veṉpatu piṉ ārāytaṟ pālatu. iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuriyil taliccaṅka varalāṟākak kūṟappaṭuvatu:
'taliccaṅkam, iṭiccaṅkam, kaṭiccaṅkam eṉa mūvakip paṭṭa caṅkam irīiyiṉār pāṇṭiyarkaḷ. avaruḷ, talic caṅkamiruntār akattiyaṉārum, tiripura meritta viricaṭik kaṭavuḷum, kuṉṟeṟinta murukavēḷum, murañciyūr muṭi nākarāyarum, nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉum eṉa ittoṭakkattār aiññūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭu nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉa reṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa ettuṇiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāriyum, mutukurukum, kaḷariyāviriyum eṉa ittoṭak kattaṉa. avar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟunāṟpatiṟ ṟiyāṇṭu caṅka miruntā reṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅkam irīiyiṉār kāyciṉa vaḻuti mutalākak kaṭuṅkōṉ īṟāka eṇpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉpa. avaruḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār. eḻuvar pāṇṭiya reṉpa. avar caṅka miruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭalkoḷḷappaṭṭa maturi yeṉpa. avarkku nūl akattiyam' eṉpatu.
kaḷaviyaṟporuḷ kaṇṭār kaṭiccaṅkattāruṭ kaṇak kāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār ākaliṉ, ivvaralāṟu āyirat teṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē nilipeṟṟa toṉṟākum. kaḷaviyaluri ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭum vaṭivil eḻutap peṟṟatu 7,8-m nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, akkālan tāṉum, ulakattil ettaṉiyō pala purāṇaṅkaḷum, varalāṟu kaḷum tōṉṟutaṟku muntiya tākaliṉ, attuṇip paḻaṅ kālattil poyyā nōṉpuṭiya pulavar perumakkaḷāl uruppeṟṟu vaḻaṅkum ceytikaḷip puṟakkaṇit tiṭuvatu neṟiyuṭimi yākātu. taliccaṅkap pulavarkaḷil tiripura meritta viricaṭik kaṭavuḷukkum, kuṉṟeṟinta murukavēḷukkum muṉpu akattiyaṉār mutalil vikkappaṭṭiruppatu aṟiñar cintaṉikkuriya toṉṟām. iṭiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷum avar muṉ vikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. caṅkam irīiyiṉār pāṇṭiyar eṉṟē kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, akattiyarāl caṅkam niṟuvappaṭṭateṉṟu koḷḷutaṟku iṭaṉilli. eṉiṉum avar caṅkap pulavaruḷḷē mutaṉmiyuṟṟiruntamiyāṉum, piṟavāṟṟāṉum avvāṟu kūṟutalum amiyum eṉka. āciriyar akattiyaṉārāl iyaṟṟap paṭṭa ilakkaṇam akattiyam eṉpatu. atu muccaṅkattārkkum nūlāyirunta teṉpatu kaḷaviyaluriyāl aṟiyappaṭuvatu. akattiyattik kuṟitta vēṟu ceytikaḷ iṟutikkaṭ kūṟappaṭum.
iṉi, akattiyaṉārē tamiḻ moḻiyi uṇṭākkiṉava reṉṟum cilar kūṟuvar. 'akattiyaṉ payanta ceñcolāraṇaṅku' eṉpatu pōṉṟavi avarkku mēṟkōḷām. akattiyar civapirāṉ pakkal tamiḻ kēṭṭaṉa reṉpataṟ kuriya cāṉṟukaḷ muṉ kāṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. murukavēḷiṭam kēṭṭaṉa reṉpārum uṇṭeṉpatu terintatē. avar murukap perumāṉpāl tamiḻ peṟṟaṉa reṉpataṟku oru kati kūṟuvārum uḷar. atu, "akattiya muṉivar kāciyilirunta vaṭamoḻivāṇaroṭu māṟupaṭṭu, ammoḻik kiṇiyāṉa maṟṟoṉṟi yaruḷumāṟu murukak kaṭavuḷi vēṇṭa, avar ōr iṭattic cuṭṭik kāṭṭi, 'ammūliyil uḷḷatu; ceṉṟu koḷka. eṉṉalum, muṉivar virintu ceṉṟu, tamiḻ! tamiḻ! eṉak kūvi, aṅkirunta ōliccuvaṭikaḷi vārikkoṇṭu pōntu cevvēḷi vaṇaṅki viṭipeṟṟut tamiḻ moḻiyi ivvulakil nilavac ceytaṉar' eṉpatu. ikkati eppoḻutu tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu pulappaṭavilli. akattiyar vaṭamoḻi vāṇaroṭu māṟupaṭṭami meyyō poyyō? ikkati yeḻutiṉavar māṟupaṭṭavar eṉpatu meyyē. tamiḻ akattiyarāl uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉpārkku ikkatikaḷum ātaravukaḷām.
maṟṟoru cārār ikkatikaḷi liruntē tamiḻ akattiyaṉārkku muṉpē viḷaṅkiṉami peṟappaṭum eṉṟu tamkōḷ niṟuvuvar. akattiyar teṟkil varumpoḻutu, teṉṟēyam tamiḻ nāṭākaliṉ aṅkuḷḷār kēṭpavaṟṟukku viṭiyiṟuttal vēṇṭumēyēṉa eṇṇic civaṉārpāl tamiḻ mutaṉūl kēṭṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu purāṇaṅ kūṟuvatu, avarkku muṉpē tamiḻmēṉmiyuṟṟirunta teṉpārkku amiyum cāṉṟaṉṟō? purāṇaṅkaḷiṉ paṭiyē tamiḻ akattiyaratu paṭippaṉṟeṉpatu teḷivām. tamiḻi akattiyarkkum, vaṭamoḻiyip pāṇiṉikkum civaperumāṉ aruḷiṉareṉṟum purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. itu koṇṭu tamiḻ akattiyarālum, vaṭamoḻi pāṇiyālum ākkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu evvaṟi vuṭiyār kūṟuvar? civaṉār akattiyarkku aruḷiyatum ilakkaṇam. akattiyar tamiḻukku aruḷiyatum ilakkaṇam. moḻi oruvarālum uṇṭākkappaṭavilli. aḵtu iyaṟkiyum aṉṟu. ivvuṇmi terittaṟkaṉṟē kalviyiṟ periyarāya kampar akattiyarik kuṟikku miṭattu, 'taḻaṟpuri cuṭarkkaṭavuḷ tanta tamiḻ tantāṉ'1 eṉṟu kūṟiṉavar, 'eṉṟu muḷa teṉṟamiḻiyampi yici koṇṭāṉ'2 eṉṟum kūṟip pōntaṉar. iṉi, purāṇaṅkaḷum ceviyāṟaṟ katikaḷum kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi viṭuttu, ikkāla ārāycci muṟiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭu pārttal kaṭaṉām.
itukāṟum kāṭṭiyavaṟṟi liruntu, iraṇṭu tiru viḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇaṅkaḷum, kāñcip purāṇamum, irāmāyaṇamum akattiyar civapirāṉiṭattē tamiḻnūl kēṭṭaṉareṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, civañāṉa cittiyār, civappirakācam, tolkāppiyap pāyira virutti eṉpaṉa avar murukak kaṭavuḷpāṟ kēṭṭaṉa reṉkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum pōtarum. paḻaṉit talapurāṇam mutalāyiṉavum avar murukavēḷiṭattē tamiḻ nūl kēṭṭami kūṟāniṟkum. eṉavē, avar civapirāṉ, murukavēḷ iruvariṭattum tamiḻ kēṭṭaṉareṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum. civaperumāṉ aruḷiyatu ici nuṇukkam (iṭic caṅkattārkku nūlāya ici nuṇukkattiṉ vēṟu) eṉṟum, kumaravēḷ aruḷiyatu kumaram eṉappaṭuvatu eṉṟum kūṟuvārum uḷar. āyiṉ, collukkum niṉivukku meṭṭāta nuṇporuḷāya iṟivar aṉpar uḷattiṉkaṇ mēvi uṇmi viḷaṅkumāṟu tēṟṟi yaruḷiṉar eṉak kūṟiṉ amiyum; avvāṟaṉṟit tāmē coṟṟoṭar vaṭiviṉatāka ōr nūliyaṟṟi ataṉi aṟivuṟut taruḷiṉa reṉpatu poruntumāṟu yāṅṅaṉam eṉṉum aiyam allatu taṭumāṟṟam nammaṉōrkku uṇṭātal iyalpē. muṟṟaṟivuṭiya iṟivar uyirkaḷellām uytikūṭa vēṇṭu meṉṉum irakkattiṉāl maṟikaḷiyum ākamaṅkaḷiyum tōṟṟuvittup pakkuvamuṭiyārk karuḷ ceytu avaṟṟi ulakiṉkaṇ nilava vippar eṉpatu tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tirumantiram mutaliya aruṇūlkaḷil veḷip paṭiyākak kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. civañāṉa cittiyār pōlvaṉa ataṉi ārāyntum muṭipu kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṟi, ākamaṅkaḷiyē yaṉṟi ellāk kalikaḷiyum, iṟivarē tōṟṟuvittaruḷu kiṉṟaṉa reṉṟum nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avar āṟaṅkamum aruḷiṉa reṉṟu tēvārat tirumuṟi pala viṭattuk kūṟuvatu kāṇalākum. iṟivarāl aruḷappaṭuvatoru nūl uḷatāyiṉ aḵtu emmoḻiyiṉ kaṇṇatēṉum yāvarāṉum pōṟṟaṟ pālatēyām. atu kuṟittu ammoḻiyē ciṟantateṉṟāl, ammoḻiyiṉ vaḻakkum ceyyuḷu mellām uyarntaṉa veṉṟātal koḷḷap paṭumāṟilli. iṟivarāl aruḷappaṭum nūl uṇṭeṉṉum koḷki tamiḻ makkaḷiṭattup paṇṭik kālattum iruntuḷa teṉpataṟku, 
"	viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum." 1
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiramē cāṉṟākum
"	citivila veṉpa mutalvaṉ kaṇṇē," 2
eṉpatum ataṉi valiyuṟuttum. eṉiṉum, nammaṉōr pōṉṟu mutalvarāl nūl aruḷappaṭumeṉpatu poruttamaṟṟa teṉṟu kaṇṭār muṉpum iruntuḷā reṉpatum, avarkaḷ mutalvar aruḷiya nūl eṉpataṟku vēṟu vēṟu vakiyākak karuttuk koḷvārāyiṉa reṉpatum 'viṉiyiṉīṅki' eṉṉum cūttirattup pērāciriyar urikkumāṟṟā ṉaṟiyalākum. atu, 'iṉi muṉivaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭuvatōr nūlilli yeṉpār avaṉ vaḻittōṉṟiya nalluṇarvuṭiyār avaṉpāṟ poruḷ kēṭṭu mutaṉūl ceytāreṉavum, ammuṉivaṉ muṉṉar ākamattup piṟantatōr moḻiyip paṟṟi aṉittup poruḷuṅ kaṇṭu piṉṉar avaṟṟavaṟṟukku nūl ceytār avareṉavum, av vākamattiṉiyē piṟkālattārum oḻukkam vēṟupaṭuntōṟum vēṟupaṭuttu vaḻi nūlum cārpunūlu meṉap palavuñ ceytāreṉavum kūṟupa' eṉpatu.
mukkuṟṟamaṟṟa periyōrkaḷ teḷinta uṇarvu niliyiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭu kūṟuvaṉa vellām ekkālattum piṟaḻāta uṇmikaḷātal  paṟṟiyum, avi iṟivar aruḷ vaḻit tōṉṟuvaṉa eṉpatu paṟṟiyum avaṟṟi yaruḷiṉār iṟivarē yeṉa āṉṟōr kūṟi vantaṉar pōlum? eṉiṉum,
"	āṭpālavark karuḷum vaṇṇamum ātimāṇpum,
kēṭpāṉ pukil aḷavilli." 3
eṉpatu pōlum niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyākiya maṟimoḻi kaṭippiṭittu aṭaṅki niṟpataṉṟi, toṉṟu toṭṭup pēraruḷāḷarāya emperumakkaḷ pallōrāṉum pēcappaṭṭu vantatoru ceytiyi ematu ciṟṟaṟiviṉkaṇ muṉitteḻukiṉṟa ārāycciyiṉ muṭipukku  ilakkākki viṭutal takkataṉṟākaliṉ itaṉi ivvaḷavil niṟuttutum. tamiḻ akattiyarāl uṇṭākkap paṭṭatāmō eṉpatē ivaṇi yārāycci aṉṟeṉpatu purāṇa katikaḷāṟ peṟappaṭṭa teṉiṉum iṟṟi nāḷ ārāycciyāḷar avvaḷavil amitiyuṟuvārallar.
iṉi, akattiyariyaṟṟiyatu ilakkaṇamē yākalāṉum,
"	ilakkiya miṉṟi yilakkaṇa miṉṟē
eḷḷiṉ ṟākil eṇṇeyu miṉṟē
eḷḷiṉiṉ ṟeṇṇe yeṭuppatu pōla
ilakki yattiṉiṉ ṟeṭupaṭu milakkaṇam," 1
eṉa avar tāmē akattiyattuṭ kūṟutaliṉ avvilakkaṇat tiṟku mutalāya ilakkiyam iruntirukka vēṇṭumākalāṉum akattiyaṉārkku muṉpē tamiḻmoḻi nilaviyatākum eṉṟu oru cārār kūṟuvar. iccūttiram akattiyar kūṟiyateṉṉum tuṇipuṭiyārkku iḵtē yamiyum. allātārkku vēṟum kūṟutal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ akattiyarāl uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉpār ulakiṉkaṇ vaḻaṅkum paṉṉūṟu moḻikaḷum yāvarāl uṇṭāyiṉa eṉṟu viṉavuvārkku yātu viṭi kūṟuvar? moḻi (pāṭi) yiyalpu ciṟitum aṟiyātārē moḻi oruvarāl paṭikkappaṭṭa teṉṟu kūṟuvār. kaṭavuḷtāṉum oliyum poruḷumākiya iru kūṟṟulakiṉ iyakkattiṟkum potuvē kāraṇamā yiruppa taṉṟit taṉiyē oru moḻikkuk kāraṇamāva tilli. kaṭavuḷ ōr moḻiyil eḻuttō  allatu nūlō aruḷiṉār eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉṟi, ōr moḻiyi yuṇṭākkiṉareṉa itukāṟum yārum kūṟiyatu milli. avvāṟirukkat tamiḻmoḻiyi akattiyar uṇṭākkiṉa reṉṟu kūṟuvatu nakikkat takkataṉṟō? moḻiyāṉatu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷumeṉa irukūṟuṭiya tāyiṉum ceyyuṭkum mutalāka vuḷḷatu vaḻakkē. avvaḻakkuttāṉ palvēṟiṭattum uyarntōr pālum allōr pālum vēṟṟumiyuṭiyatākik kālantōṟum palakāraṇaṅkaḷāṟ paṟpala tiripiṉiyēṟṟu nikaḻvatoṉṟāka vuḷatu. ulakiluḷḷa ellā moḻikaḷum nāḷaṭivil iṅṅaṉamē tiripuṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. toṉmiyavāy vaḷarcci yuṟṟirunta moḻikaḷ cila vaḻakkāṟaṟ ṟoḻitaliyum, cila moḻikaḷ putiyavāyt tōṉṟi vaḷarntu varutaliyum kaṇkūṭākak kāṇkiṉṟōm. moḻi vaḷarcciyiṉ nilivēṟupāṭukaḷiliruntu ammoḻi kaḷiṉ  kālam mutaliyaṉavum karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟiyum nāṭṭiṉ taṭpa veṭpaṅkaḷukkum, makkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷukkum, piṟavaṟṟukkum ēṟpa moḻikaḷ iyaṟki vēṟṟumiyuṭiya vātalum ārāycciyāṟ ṟeḷiyappaṭum. ivvāṟṟāl moḻi kaḷellām makkaḷiṭiyē iyalpākat tōṉṟi vaḷarntu varuvaṉavē yeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. eṉiṉum ovvoru moḻi yilum mutaṉ mutalil makkaḷ kuṟippiṉṟi yeḻunta cila ōci kaḷum coṟkaḷumē iyaṟki taḻuviyaṉa eṉpatum, makkaḷ kuṟip poṭupaṭṭa muyaṟciyāṉ uṇṭāya piṟavellām perum pālum iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭaṉa veṉpatum ōrntuṇarka.
iṉi, moḻikaḷellām makkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟi avarkaḷāl vaḷarkkap paṭuvaṉavē yeṉpatu kaṇṭāmāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟamuṟiyil vēṟupāṭum uḷatu. aṇittāya kālattil tōṉṟiya toru moḻiyi ārāyiṉ, aḵtu ataṟku muṉpuḷḷa toru moḻiyic cārntu tōṉṟiya teṉpatu kāṇappaṭum. moḻiyiṉkaṇ oru coṟṟāṉum vēṟu collic cārntē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu ellā moḻikaḷiyum avvavaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkuc cārpāyuḷḷa moḻikaḷil oṭukkik koṇṭu celliṉ vēṟu cārpillātu tōṉṟiyateṉṟu karutappaṭum moḻi oṉṟiraṇṭu tāṉ irukkak kūṭum. ammoḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyumē aṟitaṟ kariyavum, aḷaviṟanta viyappiṟ kuriyavum ākum.
iṉi, moḻiyeṉpatu oru cār makkaḷiṭit tōṉṟip poruḷuṇartti niṟkum coṟṟokutiyē. coṟkaḷuṇṭāya neṭunāḷikkup piṉ eḻuttu variyaṟi yēṟpaṭum. avvam moḻikaḷiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉ ōciyik kūṟupaṭuttu eḻuttukkaḷ variyaṟukkap paṭutaliṉ, ovvoru moḻiyilum eḻuttukkaḷ mikkum kuṟintum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
coṟkaḷit toṭarppaṭuttum, ceyyuḷākkiyum vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriya vitikaḷ mutalāyiṉavum nāḷaṭivil naṉ makkaḷiṉ vaḻakkiṉit taḻuvi aṟivuṭiyōrāl ēṟpaṭuttap peṟum. moḻiyiṉ ākkappāṭu iḵtē. iṅṅaṉamākaliṉ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ ākkattik kuṟittu vēṟu kūṟa vēṇṭu vatiṉṟu. iḵtum āṉatēyeṉka.
iṉi, tamiḻil akattiyar iyaṟṟiyatu akattiyam eṉappaṭum ilakkaṇam. atu pērakattiyam eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟum. tamiḻiṉ iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum mūṉṟu pākupāṭṭiṟkum  ilakkaṇamāy mikapparantuḷḷa tākaliṉ pērakattiyam eṉap paṭṭatākum. pērakattiyam eṉṉum peyar nōkkic ciṟṟakattiyam eṉavum avar oṉṟu ceytuḷāreṉac cilar tavaṟāka eṇṇik koḷvārāyiṉar. pērakattiyam paṉṉīrāyiram cūttiraṅ kaḷuṭiyatu eṉpar. aḵtu evvātaravu paṟṟik kūṟap paṭukiṉṟateṉa viḷaṅka villi. eṉiṉumatu mikap parantatoru nūlāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutalākum.
iṉi, akattiyaṉārāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭu, akattiyam eṉa orunūl vaḻaṅkiyatu uṇmitāṉō aṉṟō  eṉavum cilar aiyuṟuvārākiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkuk kāraṇam annūl  ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭāmiyē. akattiyattik kuṟittu aiyuṟuvār akattiyar eṉa oruvar iruntaṉareṉṟutāṉ ēṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭum? paṇṭu toṭṭu āṉṟōr palarum akattiyattik kuṟittuk kūṟi vantirukkum poḻutu, aḵtu ippoḻu tillāmiyē paṟṟi muṉpum iruntatō iṉṟō eṉa aiyuṟutal ciṟitum poruntātu. atu tali iṭi kaṭi eṉṉum mūṉṟu caṅkattārkkum nūlā yiruntateṉa iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri kūṟukiṉṟatu. iṭic caṅkattārkkum kaṭic caṅkattārkkum nūlā yiruntateṉa avvuri kūṟukiṉṟa tolkāppiyamum, avvuriyālaṟiyap paṭukiṉṟa caṅkap pulavar pāṭṭukkaḷiṟ  palavum ippoḻutum irukkiṉṟaṉa vaṉṟō? appaṭi yirukka avvuri kūṟum akattiyamum, vēṟu cilavum ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭā toḻiyiṉum  muṉpu iruntilaveṉṟu kūṟutal eṅṅaṉam poruntum? kaḷaviyaluri kūṟuvatoṉṟē yaṉṟu. 
tolkāppiyattiṟku uri kaṇṭār palarum akattiyattik kuṟittuk kūṟi vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. yāpparuṅkala virutti, naṉṉūl mayili nātaruri mutaliyavum ataṉik kuṟittup pēcukiṉṟaṉa. avvuri yellām akattiyattik kuṟittup potuvākap pēcuvatu māttiram aṉṟi, āṇṭāṇṭup pala cūttiraṅkaḷiyum akattiyaruṭiya veṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. urikaḷē yaṉṟi nūlkaḷuḷḷum  cila akattiyar koḷki yiviyeṉak kiḷantōtukiṉṟaṉa.
"	vīṅkukaṭa luṭutta viyaṉkaṇ ñālattut
tāṅkā nallicit tamiḻkkuviḷak kākeṉa
vāṉō rēttum vāymoḻip palpukaḻ
āṉāp perumi akattiya ṉeṉṉum
aruntava muṉiva ṉākkiya mutaṉūl," 1
eṉṟu paṉṉirupaṭalap pāyiram akattiya meṉṉum mutaṉūlik kuṟippiṭutalum kāṇka.
"	taraviṉṟākit tāḻici peṟṟum," 1
eṉṉum ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttira vuriyil 'iṉi ivvāṟu vanta koccakaṅkaḷi yellām oru variyaṟippaṭuttup pāttōṟum iṉañcērttip paṇṇiṟkut tiṟampōlap piṉṉuḷḷa āciriyaraṭakkuvar; ataṉi akattiyamum tol kāppiyamum uṇarntu avartam karuttaṟinta āciriyar avvāṟaṭakkāmikkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar' eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. avvāciriyar kūṟum kāraṇam iviyeṉa avar piṉpu eṭuttukkāṭṭiya vellām pērāciriya ruriyāka viruttaliṉ, akattiyamum tol kāppiyamum uṇarntavareṉa avarāṟ cuṭṭap peṟṟavar pērāciriyarē yeṉpatu tuṇipu. itiliruntē kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṉpiṉ neṭunāḷvari akattiyam vaḻaṅki vantuḷa teṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalākum. 
eṉiṉum ataṟku yārum urikaṇṭār eṉat teriyāmiyiṉ uriyāḷarkaḷ kālattil annūl muḻuvuruvuṭaṉ kāṇakkūṭā vāṟu citintu pōyiṟṟeṉṟu karutavēṇṭi yirukkiṟatu.
iṉi, akattiyam muttamiḻukkum ilakkaṇamām eṉpataṟkum pala ātaravukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
"	mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya taṉmit tāyiṟ ṟōṉṟu moḻip pulavaratu piṇṭa meṉpa," 2
eṉṉum ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttira vuriyil, 'tōṉṟu moḻip pulavar atu piṇṭa meṉpa' eṉṟataṉāṟ piṇṭattiṉiyum aṭakki niṟpatu vēṟu piṇṭamuḷa teṉak koḷka. atu mutanūlākiya akattiyam pōlum. eṉṉi? aḵtu iyaṟṟamiḻ, icittamiḻ, nāṭakattamiḻ eṉṟu mūṉṟu piṇṭattiṉiyum aṭakki niṟṟaliṉ' eṉṟu pērāciriyarum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟiyuḷḷār. uvamaviyal iṟutic cūttira vuriyiṉum  'akattiyaṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mūṉṟu tamiḻiṉum' eṉap pērāciriyar kūṟiṉār.
cilappatikāra vuriyil, aṭiyārkku nallārāl mēṟ kōḷākak kāṭṭap peṟṟa
"	avitām, cāntik kūttē vinōtak kūtteṉ
ṟāyntuṟa vakuttaṉa ṉakattiyaṉ ṟāṉē.
eḻuvakik kūttu miḻikulat tōri
āṭavakuttaṉa ṉakattiyaṉ ṟāṉē." 1
eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷ pōlvaṉavum akattiyar nāṭakattamiḻ iyaṟṟiyava reṉpataṉit terivippaṉa vākum, cilappatikāra vurippāyirattu 'nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam, akattiyam mutalāyuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷum iṟantaṉa' eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyiruppatum kāṇka. avar, avvurip pāyirattē 'tēva viruṭiyākiya kuṟumuṉipāṟ kēṭṭa māṇākkar paṉṉiru varuṭ cikaṇṭi yeṉṉum aruntavamuṉi . . . cāra kumāraṉeṉa appeyar peṟṟa kumaraṉ ici yaṟitaṟkuc ceyta ici nuṇukkamum' eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu, akattiyaṉār icit tamiḻukkum āciriyarāvā reṉpataṉik kuṟippiṭuvatākum. akattiyar icittamiḻ nūliyaṟṟiyataṉṟi, ici pāṭuvatilum mutaṉmi yuṭiyavarāvar eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇṭu. atu tolkāppiyap pāyira vuriyil akattiyar varalāṟu kūṟu miṭattu irāvaṇaṉik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu irākkatari āṇṭiyaṅkāmi vilakkiṉar' eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarāṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷatu. maṟṟum avar,
"	teṉṉavaṟ peyariya tuṉṉarun tuppiṟ
ṟoṉmutu kaṭavuṭ piṉṉarmēya
varittā ḻaruvip poruppiṟ poruna." 2
eṉṉum maturik kāñciyaṭikaḷum icceytiyik kuṟippaṉa vākak koṇṭaṉa reṉpatu avvuriyāl viḷaṅkum. atu, 'irāvaṇaṉit tamiḻ nāṭṭiyāḷātapaṭi pōkkiṉa. kiṭṭu taṟkariya valiyiṉiyuṭiya  paḻami mutirnta akattiyaṉ piṉṉē eṇṇappaṭṭuc cāṉṟōṉā yiruttaṟku mēviṉa oppaṟṟavaṉē; irāvaṇaṉ teṉṟici yāṇṭami paṟṟit teṉṉavaṉ eṉṟār. akattiyaṉit teṉṟici yuyarnta noymmipōka iṟivaṉukkuc cīroppa iruntā ṉeṉpatupaṟṟik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟār. itaṉāl akattiyaṉuṭaṉ taliccaṅkattup pāṇṭiya ṉiruntu tamiḻārāynta ciṟappuk kūṟiṉār' eṉpatu. 'tañci vāṇa ṉicikkurukap - paṭucilip pāvi' (345) eṉṉum tañci vāṇaṉ kōvic ceyyuḷum, 'icikkurukap paṭucili - irāvaṇaṟ piṇikkak kuṟumuṉipāṭum icikkurukap paṭṭa potiya mali' eṉṉum ataṉuriyum itaṉik kuṟittiṭutal kāṇka. tirunēlvēlit talapurāṇamum itu kūṟā niṟkum.
ivikaḷiliruntu ilaṅkiyar vēntaṉākiya  irāvaṇaṉ mūvēntaratu tamiḻ nāṭṭilum āṭci naṭāttat toṭaṅkiṉa ṉeṉpatum, iciyil vallōṉākiya avvirāvaṇaṉi akattiyaṉār iciyil oṭṭi veṉṟu avaṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭik kiviṭ ṭakalumāṟu  ceytaṉa reṉpatum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. akattiyaṉār ici pāṭip pāṟi nekiḻnturuki yāḻiṉip paṟṟik koḷḷumāṟu ceytaṉa reṉṟum, avviyāḻiṉi yeṭuppataṟku irāvaṇaṉ pāṭi appāṟiyi nekiḻac ceyya māṭṭāṉāyiruppa, akattiyaṉārē mīṇṭum pāṭi ataṉi nekiḻvittu yāḻiṉi yeṭuttaṉa reṉṟum avar iciyil veṉṟa varalāṟu kūṟuvar.
ivvāṟāka vuḷḷa pala mēṟkōḷkaḷum, katikaḷum akattiyaṉār iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻum valla vittakar eṉpataṉiyum, avar ammūṉṟu pākupāṭṭiṟkum ilakkaṇañ ceytuḷḷār eṉpataṉiyum terivippaṉavākum.
civañāṉa muṉivar, pāyira viruttiyil muttamiḻ eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇṭāṉamikku akattiyattik kāraṇamākak kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. atu,
"aṟṟēlaḵtāka, 'eṇṇeṇpa vēṉi yeḻutteṉpa' eṉavum 'cattamuñ cōtiṭamum' eṉavum, iyaṟṟamiḻōṭu ciṟanteṭut teṇṇappaṭum cōtiṭam mutaliya piṟa kalikaḷum uḷavāka muttamiḻ eṉat tamiḻāciriya rellām ivaṟṟiyē vitantukoṇṭa teṉṉiyō veṉiṉ, aḵtokkum; cōtiṭam mutaliya piṟa kalikaḷellām āriyattiṉum, tamiḻiṉum ēṉi moḻikaḷiṉum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi oppa nikaḻtaliṉ avaṟṟi vēṟu vitikka vēṇṭāmiyāṉum, iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum mūṉṟum tamiḻ nilattuc cila vēṟu pāṭuṭimiyiṉ avaṟṟi vēṟu vitikka vēṇṭutalāṉum, atu paṟṟi akattiyattuḷ immūṉṟumē yeṭuttōtiṉā rākaliṉ muttamiḻeṉa vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa veṉa aṟika" eṉpatu. iṅṅaṉam aruntavak koḷki akattiyaṉāri yaṟṟiya teṉṟu peṟappaṭum. akattiya meṉṉum oppuyarvaṟṟa taṉit tamiḻ mutuṉūliṉik kuṟittut tollāciriyar pallōr koḷkikkum muṟṟilum māṟākac cuppiramaṇiya tīkkita reṉpār tamatu pirayōka vivēkattil maṉampōṉapaṭi kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
atu "ēḻiyaṉ muṟiya tetirmuka vēṟṟumi - vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ - ṟōtiya pulavaṉu muḷaṉoru vakiyā - ṉintira ṉeṭṭām vēṟṟumi yeṉṟaṉaṉ, eṉa akattiyattiṉ kaṇṇum, 'aintira niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ' eṉat tolkāppiyap pāyirattiṉ kaṇṇum varutaliṉ ivarkaṭku mutaṉūl pāṇiṉīyamum aintiramumā meṉka" eṉpatu.
itil tolkāppiyattik kuṟittu ivar kūṟiyiruppatit 'tolkāppiyar' eṉṉum ārāycci yuriyil eṭuttuk koḷvōm. akattiyar pāṇiṉīyatti mutaṉūlākak koṇṭaṉar eṉṉum ivaratu koḷkiyē iṅku nōkkaṟpālatu. ettaṉiyō palanū lāciriyar uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ koḷkikku māṟāka ivar koṇṭa ittuṇipiṟku ātaravāka vuḷḷatu akattiyat tiluḷḷateṉṟu ivar kāṭṭiya cūttirattil, 'ēḻiyaṉmuṟiya  tetir muka vēṟṟumi - vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ - ṟōtiya pulavaṉumuḷaṉ' eṉṉum pakutiyē.  aḵtu akattiyar cūttirantāṉō  aṉṟō eṉpatu piṉpu pārppōm. akattiyamākavē vittuk koḷḷiṉum, itil pāṇiṉiyeṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭavilliyē. viḷivēṟṟumiyip peyariṉ vikārameṉap pāṇiṉi kūṟiyiruttaliṉ 'ōtiya pulavaṉ' eṉṟatu pāṇiṉiyātal vēṇṭum eṉṉil, pāṇiṉi kūṟi yiruppatum ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa ōr pulavaṉ koḷkiyeṉa ēṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu? aṉṟiyum pāṇiṉīyam eṉpatu iṟṟikku ēṟakkuṟiya iraṇṭāyirattinnūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu tōṉṟiyatu. akattiyamō pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ātiyūḻiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya teṉpa. avvāṟirukka akattiyar pāṇiṉīyattik kaṇṭu nūliyaṟṟiṉa reṉpatu iṟumpūtē. kālatti viṭṭuviṭuvōm. pāṇiṉīyameṉpatu vaṭamoḻic canta nūlām. akattiyamō tamiḻiṉ iyal ici nāṭaka meṉṉum mūṉṟukkum ilakkaṇamāvatu. ici, nāṭakaṅkaḷi viṭuttu iyal oṉṟiyē yeṭuttuk koḷḷiṉum, atu, piṟa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭāta akaṉum puṟaṉumāya poruṭpāku pāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiyatu. akattiyarkkup pāṇiṉīyam mutaṉūlā meṉṟa tīkkitar ivvuṇmikaḷic ciṟitum aṟintilarō, aṟintu vittum vaṭamoḻiyē emmoḻikkum tāyātal vēṇṭumeṉṉum paṟṟiṉāl vaḻukki viṭṭaṉarō aṟintilēm.
avar piṟitōriṭattu, 'yām innūluṭ perum pāṉmiyum taṟcamayam taṟpavaṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiṉām. ipperum pāṉmiyum kūṟiyatu yātupaṟṟi yeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻ moḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉpataṟiyātu camñāpētattālum pāṭi vēṟṟumiyālum ikaḻntu vēṟeṉpāri nōkki yeṉka'  eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatiliruntu irumoḻikkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumiyic ciṟitum ōrntilar eṉat teritaliṉ akattiyattik kuṟittu avar tavaṟākak kūṟiyatil viyappoṉṟu milli yeṉka.
tamiḻil aḷavaṟṟa ārāycci yuṭiyareṉṟu karutap paṭukiṉṟa cuvāmināta tēcikarē tamatu ilakkaṇak kottil tamiḻiṉ perumiyip puṟakkaṇittu iḷañciṟār pōṉṟu muṉṉoṭu piṉ maliyap pala kūṟuvārāyiṉar. avar,
"	tolkāp piyantiru vaḷḷuva rātinūl
vaṭamoḻi niyāyam vantaṉa cilavē"1
"	tamiḻi ṉiyāyan tantaṉa palavāl." 2
eṉṟu kūṟiyavar, uṭaṉē ataṟku muṟṟilum māṟāka
"	aṉṟiyun tamiḻnūṟ kaḷavili yavaṟṟu
ḷoṉṟē yāyiṉun taṉittami ḻuṇṭō
aṉṟiyu minteḻut tāloru pāṭiyeṉ
ṟaṟiyavē nāṇuva raṟivuṭi yōrē.
ākiyāl yāṉu matuvē yaṟika" 3
"	vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi yeṉumiru moḻiyiṉum
ilakkaṇa moṉṟē yeṉṟē yeṇṇuka." 4
eṉṟu muḻaṅki makiḻvārāyiṉar. tolkāppiyam tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil vaṭamoḻi niyāyam vantaṉa eṉkiṉṟār. vantaṉaveṉṟē koḷḷiṉum avar kūṟiyapaṭi avi cilavē yaṉṟō? 'tamiḻ nūṟku aḷavili' yeṉṟu kūṟivittu, aṭuttu 'oṉṟēyāyiṉum taṉittami ḻuṇṭō' eṉṟu kūṟiyatāṟ peṟṟateṉ vaṭacoṟ cila  arukik kalantuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu tāṉē? atukoṇṭu 'ilakkaṇa moṉṟē yeṉṟē yeṇṇuka' eṉa eṅṅaṉam kūṟat tuṇintaṉarō! avar karuttup paṭi āṅkilac col cila tamiḻiṟ kalantuviṭiṉ āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻiṟkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭiyatē!  'ainteḻuttāloru pāṭi' eṉa avar kūṟiyiruppatu paḷḷiyiṟ payilum ciṟārum nakiyāṭaṟ kēṟṟa taṉṟō? ovvoru moḻiyum piṟa moḻikaṭkup potu vāyuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷit tavirttu ittaṉi yittaṉi  yeḻuttukkaḷā ṉiyaṉṟuḷa veṉakkāṭṭi, ataṉpiṉ tamiḻukkuḷḷa kuṟiyik kūṟi yirupparēl avar naṭuniṉṟu kūṟiyatākak karuta iṭaṉuṇṭu aṉṉō! perumpulami vāynta oruvar ciṟitu amiti yiṉmiyāl tam perumikkup periyatō riḻukkiṉit tēṭik koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. oru kālattavarāya tīkkitarum tēcikarum iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyiruppa vaṟṟi nōkkuḻi, vaṭamoḻi matam paṟṟiya ivvēḻaṅkaḷiṉ tarukki yaṭakkum kōḷaripōlum tamiḻp pulavar akkālattil iruntilar pōlum eṉak karuta vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu.
potu veḻutti nīkki ainteḻuttāloru  pāṭi yuṇṭō veṉṟu kūṟiyatu vīṇvaḻak keṉpataṉit tēcikar tāmē aṟintiruppareṉpataṟku, avar piṉpu,
"	potu veḻuttāṉuñ ciṟap peḻuttāṉum
īreḻuttāṉu milaṅkun tamiḻmoḻi." 1
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatē cāṉṟākum.
maṟṟum avar 'irutiṇiyum, āṇpāl peṇpāl viṉiyīṟum vaṭamoḻikkilli. mūṉṟiliṅkamum, mutalīṟṟu vēṟṟumikaṭ kurupukaḷum tamiḻiṟkilli. mēlum āṇṭāṇṭuk kāṭṭutum' eṉa ōriṭattukkūṟi, maṟṟōriṭattu
"	cila peyareccamuñ cila viṉittokiyum
iruvaki yuvamiyum vaṇṇac ciṉiccolum
paṇput tokiyeṉap pakarum vaṭanūl" 2
eṉac cūttirañ ceytu, 'pakarum vaṭanūleṉavē tamiḻk kaṭāteṉka' eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatu tamiḻiyalpum vēṟu, vaṭa moḻiyiyalpum vēṟu eṉa avar koṇṭuḷā reṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟaṉṟō? eṉiṉum avar, tām muṉpu kūṟiyataṉi muṟṟilum tavaṟu eṉṟu kāṭṭik koḷḷa maṉamporuntātu, 'āyiṉ īrilakkaṇamum  oṉṟeṉṟu pāyirattiṟ kūṟiyateṉ
ṉeṉiṟ perumpāṉmi nōkki yeṉka' eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
oṉṟeṉpataṟkuc ciṟupāṉmiyum iṭamilli yeṉpataṉi, tolkāppiyar aintiratti mutuṉūlākak koṇṭaṉa reṉṉum pirayōka vivēka nūlāratu koḷkiyi maṟukku mukattāṉē civañāṉa muṉivar pāyira viruttiyiṟ kāṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. atu,
'aintira nōkkit tokuttāṉeṉiṉ, tamiḻ moḻip puṇarccikkaṭpaṭum ceykikaḷum, kuṟiyīṭukaḷum, viṉik kuṟippu viṉittoki mutaliya cila collilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi mutaliya coṟpākupāṭukaḷum, akam puṟameṉṉum poruṭ pākupāṭukaḷum, kuṟiñci veṭci mutaliya tiṇip pākupāṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum, veṇpā mutaliya ceyyuḷilakkaṇamum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāmiyāṉum, iviyellān tāmē paṭittuk koṇṭu ceytāreṉiṉ, muntunūl kaṇṭeṉpataṉōṭu muraṇutalāṉum muṟkālattu mutaṉūl akattiya meṉpatūum, ataṉ vaḻittākiya tolkāppiyam ataṉ vaḻi nūleṉpatūum tuṇiyappaṭu meṉka' eṉpatu.
iṉi, taṉittamiḻ mutaṉūlākiya akattiyam tōṉṟutaṟku muṉ tamiḻiṉ nilimi yātākumeṉṟu pārppōm.
akattiyar pala pulavarkaḷōṭu caṅkamiruntu tamiḻā rāyntaṉar; māṇavar palarkkut tamiḻaṟivuṟuttiṉar; akattiya meṉṉum muttamiḻilakkaṇam aruḷic ceytaṉar; eṉṉum ic ceytikaḷē, ataṟku muṉ eṇṇiṟanta nūlkaḷōṭu kūṭit tamiḻ taḻittirukka vēṇṭu meṉpataṉip pulappaṭuttā niṟkum. akattiyam oṉṟiyē koṇṭu nōkkutum. oru moḻiyil periyatō rilakkaṇam tōṉṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉ ataṟku muṉpē naṉmakkaḷiṉ vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ataṉkaṇ malintiruttal vēṇṭum. ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum pala iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
"	vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷu nāṭic
centami ḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu
muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa veṇṇip."
eṉṟu tolkāppiyap pāyiraṅ kūṟuvatu ivvuṇmiyi  naṉku viḷakkuvatākum. akattiyattiṟkum itu poruntum eṉka. āyiṉ akattiyam muttamiḻkkum periyatōrilakkaṇa mākaliṉ atu tōṉṟutaṟku aṭiyāka vaḻakkē yaṉṟi eṇṇiṟanta ceyyuṭkaḷum iruntiruttal vēṇṭu meṉpatu poruttamē; ilakkaṇamum vēṟu iruntirukka vēṇṭu meṉpatu eṉṉi? akattiyamē mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟiya ilakkaṇa mākum; eṉṉil, kūṟutum orumoḻiyāṉatu tōṉṟi ettaṉiyō āyiram āṇṭukaḷāyiṉa piṉpē atu muṟṟilum tiruntiya niliyaṭiyap peṟuvatākum. ataṟku muṉpellām vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ilakkaṇattiṟkum, ilakkaṇam vaḻakkuc ceyyuḷkaḷukkum āka iṅṅaṉam oṉṟukkoṉṟu, ātaravāyiruntu nāḷaṭivil moḻiyāṉatu vaḷarcciyum tiruttamum aṭintu, vantu koṇṭiruppatē iyaṟki. avvāṟaṉṟi, mutaṉ mutalākavē ōr pērilakkaṇam ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭateṉpatu iyaṟkikku ottatākātu. aṉṟiyum akattiyar cūttirattiruntē avarkku muṉ vēṟu ilakkaṇam iruntami aṟiyap paṭukiṉṟatu. yāpparuṅkala iṟutic cūttira viruttiyil akattiyaṉāratu cūttiramāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷatu piṉ varuvatu:
"	kaḷaviṉuṅ kaṟpiṉuṅ kalakka millāt
talivaṉun taliviyum pirinta kālik
kiyaṟu tuyaramoṭu kāṭcik kavāvi
evvamoṭu puṇarntu naṉimikap pulampap
pāṭap paṭuvōṉ patiyoṭu nāṭṭoṭum
uḷḷuṟut tiṟiṉē yuyarkaḻi yāṉantap
piyu ḷeṉṟu paḻittaṉar pulavar." 1
itil 'paḻittaṉar pulavar' eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu itaṟku muṉ vēṟu ilakkaṇa nūl iruntamiyit terivikku maṉṟō?
āyiṉ, akattiyatti mutaṉūleṉṟu kūṟuva porun
tātām  eṉiṉ, aṉṟu; mutaṉūleṉṟu ellā nūṟkum kālattāl muṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatiṉṟu. mutaṉūl iṉṉateṉpatu,
"	viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉūlākum" 2
eṉṉum marapiyaṟ cūttirattāl aṟiyappaṭum. ac cūttira vuriyil pērāciriyar, tāmē talivarāyiṉārum, at talivari, vaḻipaṭṭut talivarāyiṉārum mutaṉūl ceyvareṉavum, muṟ kālattu mutaṉūl akattiyamē yeṉavum piṟkālattāṉum perumāṉaṭikaḷ kaḷaviyal ceytāṅkuc ceyyiṉum atuvum mutaṉūlē yāmeṉavum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē muṟkālat tēṉum piṟkālattēṉum iṟivaṉō avaṉaruḷ vaḻippaṭṭa periyārō piṟitoru nūliṉ vaḻittaṉṟic ceyyum nūl mutaṉūleṉappaṭum eṉpatāyiṟṟu. kaḷaviyalukku muṉpē akapporuḷi virittuṇarttum nūlkaḷ uḷavākavum kaḷaviyal mutaṉūleṉappaṭuvatu pōṉṟē akattiyamum mutaṉūleṉap paṭukiṉṟatu eṉka. aṟṟēl., civañāṉamuṉivar  tolkāppiyap pāyirattiluḷḷa 'nilattoṭu muntunūl' eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarkku 'centamiḻ nilattu moḻiyōṭu muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟum nūl' eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, 'eṉṟatu ammoḻi tōṉṟuṅ kālattuṭaṉ ṟōṉṟiya nūleṉa ataṉ ṟoṉmi kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟeṉpatu. eṉavē akattiyameṉpatu peṟṟām' eṉṟa teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, muṉivarar karuttu iṅku icivuṭittaṉ ṟeṉpatē emataṟiviṟkup pulaṉāvatu. itaṉi muṉṉarē maṟutturittārum uḷar. civañāṉa muṉivar akattiyar varattāṟ piṟantavareṉpa. ataṟkup pala poruttaṅ
kaḷum uḷa. avar akattiyaṉārkku vaṇakkaṅ kūṟiyiruppatum muṉpu kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ivvāṟṟāl akattiyaṉār pāl aḷavu kaṭanta aṉpuṭiyār eṉpatu peṟappaṭiṉum, atu paṟṟiyē aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāreṉṟāl avar perumikku ēlātatoṉṟām. muṉivaratu karuttiṉ meymmiyi nallaṟivuṭiyōr ārāyntu veḷippaṭupparēṟ kāṇpēmāka.
iṉi, yāpparuṅkala viruttiyil "akattiyaṉār aṟuvaki yāṉanta vōttiṉuḷ itaṉi
"	iyaṟpeyar cārtti yeḻuttaḷa peḻiṉē
yiyaṟpā ṭillā veḻuttā ṉantam" 1
eṉṟār eṉka." (cū. 4- uri) eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttaliṉ akattiyar āṉanta vōttu eṉavum oru nūl iyaṟṟiyuḷḷāreṉpatu peṟappaṭum. āyiṉ, tamiḻ nūṟ kaṭalmuḻutum parukit tiḷit
tōrāṉa pērāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkaḷiṉ karuttu itaṟku muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭuḷatu.
pērāciriyar, uvamaviyal iṟutic cūttira vuriyilē "iṉi āṉanta vuvamiyeṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟam akattiyaṉār ceytāreṉak kūṟupavarākaliṉ aviyiṟṟi yevvāṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, avikaḷtām akattuḷḷum piṟacāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuḷḷum varutaliṟ kuṟṟamākā; akattiyaṉārāṟ ceyyap paṭṭa mūṉṟu tamiḻiṉum aṭaṅkāmi vēṟu āṉanta vōt teṉpatu oṉṟu ceytārāyiṉ akattiyamum tol kāppiyamum nūlāka vanta cāṉṟōr ceyyum kuṟṟam vēṟu paṭā veṉpatu" eṉṟum, marapiyalil 'citiveṉappaṭupavi' eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyil "taṉṉā ṉoruporuḷ karutik kūṟaleṉpatu malipaṭu kaṭāttiṉi āṉantak kuṟṟameṉap piṟkālattā ṉoruvaṉ oru cūttiram kāṭṭutalum . . . . . . pōlvaṉa" eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. eṉavē akattiyaṉār āṉanta vōttuk kūṟavilli yeṉpatē pērāciriyar karuttātal peṟappaṭṭatu. ivar akattiyamuṇarn tavareṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karutiyuḷḷāreṉpatu muṉpu kāṭṭap peṟṟatu. itaṉi 'āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ' eṉṉum ceyyuḷiṟ cūttiravuriyil "aḵteṟṟāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ itaṉ mutaṉūl ceyta āciriyaṉ akattiyaṉār collu māṟṟāṟ peṟutu meṉṟavāṟu" eṉap pērāciriyar tāmē kūṟutal valiyuṟuttā niṟkum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar malipaṭu kaṭāttiluḷḷa 'tīyiṉaṉṉa voṇceṅkāntaḷ' eṉṉum aṭikkup poruḷ eḻutiya viṭattē "itaṟku naṉṉaṉeṉṉum peyar tīyōṭaṭutta taṉmiyiṉ, āṉantamāy, pāṭiṉārum iṟantāreṉṟu āḷavanta piḷḷi yāciriyar kuṟṟaṅ kūṟiṉārāleṉiṉ, avar aṟiyātu kūṟiṉār; ceyyuḷ ceyta kucikaṉār āṉantak kuṟṟameṉṉuṅ kuṟṟa maṟiyāmaṟ ceyyuḷ ceytārēl, ivar nallicip pulavarākār; ivar ceyta ceyyuḷi nallicip pulavar ceyta ēṉic ceyyuṭkaḷuṭaṉ caṅkattār kōvāmal nīkkuvar; aṅṅaṉam nīkkātu kōttataṟkuk kāraṇam āṉantak kuṟṟameṉpa toru kuṟṟam icceyyuṭku uṟāmiyā ṉeṉṟuṇarka" eṉap potu vakiyāṟ kūṟivittu, piṉpu ivvaṭiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉ niliyi ārāyntum kuṟṟamiṉmiyi nāṭṭukiṉṟār.
avar avvaḷavil niṉṟiruppiṉ icceyyuḷil kuṟṟam iṉṟeṉṟāreṉum āṉantak kuṟṟameṉpatu oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉa uṭaṉpaṭṭārāvar. āṉāl avar avvaḷavil nillātu "nūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kūṟukiṉṟa pattuvakik kuṟṟattē 'taṉṉāṉoru poruḷ karutik kūṟal' eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟattip piṉṉuḷōr āṉantak kuṟṟameṉpatoru kuṟṟameṉṟu nūl ceytataṉṟi akattiyaṉārum tolkāppiyaṉārum ikkuṟṟam kūṟāmiyiṟ cāṉṟō ceyyuṭku ikkuṟṟa muṇṭāyiṉuṅ koḷḷāreṉa maṟukka" eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu āṉantak kuṟṟam avarkku uṭaṉpaṭāmiyi viḷakkukiṟatu. akattiyaṉār āṉantak kuṟṟaṅ kūṟavilliyeṉa avar tuṇinturippatu, pērāciriyar aṅṅaṉam kūṟiyiruttalāṉum tolkāppiyattil atu kāṇappaṭāmiyāṉum pōlum? akattiyar ciṟitēṉum eṭuttiyampuvatu muṟiyē yaṉṟō?
yāpparuṅkala āciriyar amita cākaraṉār 'āṉanta mutaliya ūṉam' eṉṟu ataṉic curuṅkak kūṟivittaṉar. ataṉ urikārar āṉantatti aṟuvakippaṭuttu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟi, avvilakkaṇaṅ kaṭku mēṟkōḷāka vēṟu cūttiraṅ kaḷiyum kāṭṭiṉar. ataṉōṭu avar akkuṟṟaṅkaḷukku utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭum vaḻi malipaṭu kaṭāttil vevvēṟiṭatti liruntu patiṉāṉku aṭikaḷi yeṭuttuvittu, avaṟṟil akkuṟṟaṅkaḷ amintu kiṭakkumāṟu kāṭṭuvārāyiṉar.
